## 1. System overview

TAQINOR OS is a multi-tenant ERP for a Moroccan solar installer. A browser loads a
**React/Vite single-page app** (`frontend/`). All traffic enters through **nginx**
(ports 80/443), which reverse-proxies three upstreams: the SPA static bundle, the
**Django REST API** (`backend/django_core`, served by gunicorn on :8000), and a
**FastAPI AI/OCR service** (`backend/fastapi_ia`, uvicorn on :8001). The SPA calls
the Django API under the prefix `/api/django/…` and the AI service under
`/api/fastapi/…`. Django persists everything to **PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)** and
uses **Redis** as cache plus Celery broker; a **Celery worker** (same Django image)
runs async jobs such as quote-PDF generation. Generated PDFs and uploads live in
**MinIO** (S3-compatible object storage, buckets `erp-pdf` and `erp-uploads`).
Authentication is cookie-based JWT (httpOnly refresh cookie); every API request is
scoped to the caller's `company` (the tenant). The FastAPI service shares the same
Postgres for its OCR (Zhipu) and natural-language-SQL-agent (LangChain) features,
both JWT-protected and key-gated.

```
            ┌──────────────┐
  Browser → │    nginx     │  :80 / :443  (+127.0.0.1:8090 lead webhook listener)
            └──────┬───────┘
        ┌──────────┼───────────────┬───────────────────┐
        ▼          ▼               ▼                   ▼
   frontend   django_core      fastapi_ia          (static SPA)
   (Vite SPA) gunicorn :8000   uvicorn :8001
   /api/django/*               /api/fastapi/*
        │          │               │
        │          ▼               ▼
        │     PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)  ◄── shared DB
        │          │
        │          ├── Redis  (cache + Celery broker)
        │          ├── Celery worker (async PDFs, same Django image)
        │          └── MinIO  (erp-pdf, erp-uploads)
```

Request flow, front to back: SPA dispatches a Redux thunk → axios `GET/POST
/api/django/<app>/…` with the JWT cookie → nginx → gunicorn/Django → DRF ViewSet
(queryset filtered to `request.user.company`) → Postgres → JSON back. Quote PDFs
are the exception: the ViewSet hands off to the vendored premium engine (sync via
`/proposal`, or async via Celery) which renders with WeasyPrint and stores the file
in MinIO.

## 4. Backend, app by app

All multi-tenant models carry a `company` FK → `authentication.Company`. ViewSets
filter `get_queryset()` by `request.user.company` and force-assign `company` in
`perform_create` (never read from the request body). The literal tenant field is
**`company`** — there is no field named `tenant_id`.

> **Contrainte transversale de conception (VX125).** Budget de densité de
> signaux : max 3 signaux ambiants simultanés par écran de liste, jamais deux
> redisant le même chiffre ; badge de maturité `<BetaBadge>` sur les modules
> jeunes avec critère de retrait objectif. Voir `docs/design-density-budget.md`.

API prefixes (from `erp_agentique/urls.py`, all under `/api/django/`):
`authentication` → root, `stock/`, `crm/`, `ventes/`, `parametres/`, `roles/`,
`reporting/`, `contact/`, `installations/`, `sav/`, `records/`, `imports/`
(dataimport), `custom-fields/`, `documents/`, `public/` (tokenized PDFs, no login),
`pos/`, `scm/`, `mrp/`, `transport/`, `douane/`, `ecommerce-connect/`,
`promotions/` (this batch's 7 new apps + the pre-existing `pos`, all `_APP_URLS`).
JWT lives at `token/`, `token/refresh/`, `token/verify/`.

### authentication — tenant root, users, JWT  *(path: `backend/django_core/authentication`, NOT under apps/)*
Owns the tenant (`Company`), the user model, registration, and JWT issuance.
- **Company** — `nom`, `slug` (unique), `actif` (bool), `date_creation`; **NTDMO27** adds `tours_actifs` (bool, default True) — company-wide off-switch for auto-appearing `<ProductTour>` visits (any company, not demo-only, unlike `mode_presentation_actif`). The tenant every other model points at.
- **CustomUser** (extends AbstractUser) — `company` FK→Company; `role` FK→roles.Role (nullable); `role_legacy` (deprecated CharField admin/responsable/normal, now kept in sync with `role`'s tier on create/update + a one-off additive data backfill); derived `menu_tier` property = the **authoritative** menu tier read from the *new* Role (Administrateur→admin, Responsable→responsable, Utilisateur/custom→normal; superuser→admin; legacy fallback only when role-less), exposed on `/auth/me/` and the JWT and used by the sidebar; `tier_for_role` + the pure `authentication/role_tiers.py` are the single source of truth; `poste`, `phone_number`, `avatar_key` (MinIO); `is_protected` (owner-account guard), `is_active`, `is_superuser`; **`supervisor`** self-FK (nullable, Feature E) driving team/subtree record-visibility. Record-visibility scoping lives in `authentication/scoping.py` (`record_scope_for`, `visible_user_ids`, `scope_queryset`) and is applied opt-in on the list+detail querysets of crm/ventes/installations/sav (only the new scoped roles narrow; admins/legacy/custom roles see all; users always keep their own records). Buy prices gated by `can_view_buy_prices` (`prix_achat_voir`).
- Endpoints (mounted at `/api/django/`): `POST /auth/register-company/` (public onboarding: new company + admin) · `POST /register/` (admin adds user to own company) · `GET /auth/me/` · `POST /auth/logout/` · `POST /auth/token/refresh/` · `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /users/…` + `POST /users/{id}/avatar/` (Administrateur + Responsable tier — `IsAdminOrResponsableTier`, limited tier blocked) · `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /companies/…` (superuser). **PACT116** gives `register-company` its first frontend door — public `/register` route (no login, no ERP layout), previously reachable only by direct API call.
- **Tenant lifecycle (SCA18-22).** `Company.statut` (actif/suspendu/fermeture, mirrors the historical `actif` bool both ways) gates login/JWT-refresh/API access — enforced in the authenticator (`cookie_auth.py`) and JWT issuance, never a middleware (SCA18, applied where the request already resolves the user — zero extra queries). `selectors.active_companies` (SCA19) is the single source of "sweepable" companies for beat fan-outs. `services.close_company`/soft-close (SCA21) moves a tenant to `fermeture` + timestamps `date_fermeture` (30-day grace before purge). A staff-only founder console (SCA22, `IsSuperuserConsole`) at `auth/console/tenants/` (list + usage counts via other apps' selectors, never their models) + `.../statut/` (change actif/suspendu/fermeture) + `.../note/` (free-text `plan_flag`, never billing). **SCA46** adds `Company.benchmarking_opt_in` (opt-in consent, default False) gating the k-anonymized cross-tenant aggregates (`core.benchmarking`). **SCA23** adds a "day-2" tenant test (`tests/test_day2_tenant.py`) as a CI sellability gate: registers a second company and exercises its full journey (quote→PDF proposal→accept) in isolation from the first, catching cross-tenant regressions the single-company test suite would miss. **N100/N101 (2026-08-05, the ERP is now FOR SALE):** console gains `auth/console/tenants/creer/` (`views_console_create.py` — Company + CompanyProfile + first admin invitation + `init_roles` + SCA20 signup hooks, idempotent on slug/email) and the tenant payload gains plan/seats/health/licence-billing (import-guarded reads); `CustomUser.is_taqinor_support` (migration 0027) = editor-support flag granting only the right to REQUEST an impersonation (adminops NTADM22); public `POST /api/django/auth/signup-demande/` (design partners) is parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED` (default OFF → 404, throttled + honeypot) and only ever creates a `DemandeInscription` — accounts are created solely by founder approval in the console.

### crm — sales funnel + clients
Leads from creation through funnel stages, client records, Odoo-style chatter,
duplicate detection/merge, reversible archive.
- **Client** — `company` FK; `type_client` (PARTICULIER/ENTREPRISE); `nom/prenom`, `email` (optional), `telephone`, `adresse`; Moroccan IDs `cin/ice/if_fiscal/rc`; `custom_data` JSON. Unique `(company, email)` when email set. **NTCRM14** adds `derniere_alerte_dormance` (timestamp, anti-spam guard on the dormant-account nudge).
- **Lead** — `company` FK; `client` FK→Client (nullable); `owner` FK→CustomUser; `stage` (**STAGES.py keys**: NEW/CONTACTED/QUOTE_SENT/FOLLOW_UP/SIGNED/COLD, default NEW); `perdu` (bool lost-flag) + `motif_perte`; `canal` (META_ADS/WHATSAPP_CTWA/SITE_WEB/REFERENCE/TELEPHONE/WALK_IN/AUTRE); `priorite`, `tags`, `relance_date`; `type_installation` (RESIDENTIEL/COMMERCIAL/INDUSTRIEL/AGRICOLE); energy profile (`facture_hiver/ete`, `ete_differente` bool, `regularisation_8221` bool); roof/site + pump fields; `source` (OS_NATIVE/ODOO_IMPORT_TEST/SITE_WEB); `is_archived` (bool) + `archived_by/at`; `custom_data` JSON; `web_questionnaire`/`web_estimate` JSON (quote-journey v2: pro/agricole answers without a column + the exact numbers shown to the visitor, whitelisted server-side; migration crm/0062 — the webhook also maps 'professionnel' → INDUSTRIEL and writes one automatic chatter note per created lead).
- **LeadActivity** — `lead` FK; `kind` (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE); field-change log (`field/old_value/new_value`) or manual `body`; `user` FK; `bulk` bool.
- **LeadTag / Canal / MotifPerte** — per-company managed lists for tags, channels, loss reasons (each has `archived` bool; Canal has `protege`).
- **WebsiteLeadPayload** — raw webhook capture from taqinor.ma; `payload` JSON, `processed` bool, `lead` FK (never loses inbound data).
- **Parrainage** (referral program, N98) — `company` FK; `parrain` FK→Client (the referrer); `filleul_lead` FK→Lead and/or `filleul_client` FK→Client (the referred) + free-text `filleul_nom`; `statut` (en_attente/converti/recompense_versee); `recompense` (Decimal, pre-filled from `parametres.CompanyProfile.referral_reward`); `notes`; `created_by`. Feature on/off via `CompanyProfile.referral_enabled`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/crm/`): `clients/` and `leads/` ViewSets (CRUD) plus `leads/{id}/archiver|restaurer|whatsapp-devis|devis-auto|noter|merge|bulk`, `leads/{id}/duplicates`, `leads/doublons`, `leads/historique`, `leads/export-xlsx`, `clients/export-xlsx`; managed-list ViewSets `tags/`, `canaux/`, `motifs-perte/`; `parrainages/` (referrals); `assignable-users/`; `POST webhooks/website-leads/` (public, static secret).
- **management/import_odoo_leads** (N107) — `manage.py import_odoo_leads <path> --company <slug|id> [--dry-run]`: idempotent Odoo `crm.lead` importer reusing the `dataimport` parser (CSV/XLSX) + JSON; forces company server-side, reconciles on normalized email/phone + the existing `(company, external_system, external_id)` unique key (never duplicates), stage names from STAGES.py (unknown → NEW). No-op without a file. The real 619-lead extraction stays manual/gated on the actual Odoo backup (PII, never committed). **FG242** adds **ConcurrentPerte** — on a LOST lead, captures the winning competitor + price/devise/motif (reuses the existing `Lead.perdu` flag, no hardcoded STAGES.py stage); acting user + company server-side, optional LeadActivity chatter note; ViewSet `concurrents-perte/` (`?lead=`). Migration crm/0029. **FG204** adds **PointContact** — a per-lead multi-touch attribution journal (canal reusing `Lead.Canal`, source, date, ordre, optional paid-channel cost) with a timeline + first/last-touch summary selector; endpoints `points-contact/` (+ `attribution/`) and a `leads/{id}/points-contact/` read action. Migration crm/0030.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 13 crm ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour identical, isolation now auto-covered by the generic tenant sweep test). **ARC18/56** add nullable `tiers` FKs mirroring `Client` (migration crm/0050) and `Lead` (migration crm/0051) into the `tiers.Tiers` directory (`tiers_bridge.py`). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/crm/platform.py` (the first platform manifest, alongside `contrats`) declaring `searchable_models`/`record_targets`/`agent_actions_module` — `agent_actions.py` exposes read-only lead/client agent actions.
- **SiteProfile** (DC12, previously undocumented here) — a REUSABLE per-client energy/roof/pumping profile (OneToOne→`Client`; taxonomies reuse `Lead`'s choices rather than redeclaring a divergent second list), saved once instead of re-entered on every devis. Endpoint `site-profiles/`; consumed read-only by `apps.ventes` via `selectors.site_profile_for_client` — see the ventes `prefill-site` action (**WIR99**), the action that finally gave this model a caller.
- **LB39** — the VX95 « Annuler » toast on a lead-stage change was PATCHing back to the previous stage and always hitting the funnel guard's 400 (a lead never regresses an étape) — the undo was dead in production. `LeadSerializer` now accepts a write-only, never-persisted `undo` boolean: `validate()` allows the regression ONLY when a server-side check (`_undo_of_last_stage_change`) confirms the PATCH is the EXACT inverse of this lead's most recent `stage` chatter entry, within a 300 s window — any other backward move stays refused. No model/migration change.
- **NTMIG26** adds a certification layer on **Partenaire** (apporteur d'affaires / sous-revendeur, historical table `compta_partenaire`): `niveau_certification` (aucun/enregistre/certifie/or/platine), `date_certification`, `date_expiration_certification`, `specialites` (JSON), `nb_deploiements_reussis` (denormalized mirror). `services.poser_compteur_deploiements` is the ONE write path for the counter — called from `apps.migration` (which owns the deployments table), never a direct `Partenaire.objects.update()` from another app. Migration crm/0069.
- **Dormance & engagement (NTCRM14/15/16/29)** — `management/detecter_comptes_dormants` + `selectors.comptes_dormants(company, seuil_jours=90)` flags signed `Client`s with no recent activity; `clients/dormants/` (list) + `clients/{id}/relancer-dormance/` (nudge, stamps `derniere_alerte_dormance`). `engagement.py` (NTCRM16) is a pure/stateless multi-signal engagement score (0-100: fréquence contact/activité récente/paiements à temps/devis acceptés, Chaud/Tiède/Froid labels like `scoring.py`'s lead-scoring) at `clients/{id}/engagement/` + `clients/engagement-bulk/`. `selectors.portefeuille_commercial` (NTCRM29) — a commercial's owned accounts + risk flags, at `clients/mon-portefeuille/`. Frontend: `DormantAccountsWidget.jsx` + `dashboard/PortfolioWidget.jsx` on `CrmCockpit`.
- **SalleVente / SalleVenteItem / SalleVenteVue** (NTCRM17-19) — a tokenized public page bundling several devis/documents/links for ONE lead OR ONE client (never both/neither, `clean()`-enforced); security modelled on `ged.PartageGed` (unpredictable `token`, `expires_at` default 30 d, optional hashed `password_hash`, `actif` kill-switch). `SalleVenteItem` (devis/document/video_lien/note, `ordre`). `SalleVenteVue` logs each public visit (hashed IP only). `selectors.salle_vente_analytics`/`salle_vente_summary_for_lead`; **NTCRM27** `detecter_signal_interet_salle_vente` flags repeat views on a salle linked to an open lead and emits a `salle_vente_signal_interet` domain event (best-effort). Endpoints: `salles-vente/` CRUD (+ `items/`, `DELETE items/{item_id}/`, `analytics/`), `leads/{id}/salle-vente-analytics/`; public `GET /api/django/crm/salle-vente/<token>/` (frontend `/salle-vente/:token`, no login/layout). Frontend: `pages/crm/salle-vente/PublicSalleVentePage.jsx` (public) + `pages/crm/leads/SalleVenteAnalyticsBadge.jsx` (internal, on the lead workspace).
- **Apporteur / DealEnregistre** (NTCRM20-22) — a B2B deal-registration registry, deliberately SEPARATE from the pre-existing `Partenaire`/FG234 lead-submission portal (a concurrent-registration protection window has no equivalent there). `Apporteur` (nom/type/commission %/RIB/`token_acces` — read-only portal secret, no CustomUser account). `DealEnregistre` (`apporteur` FK, `lead` OneToOne, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete/expire/**a_payer** — NTCRM22 sets this + `montant_commission_du` on the linked devis's acceptance; `clean()` refuses a second apporteur registering the SAME prospect — email/phone/client identity match — while another's protection window, default 90 d, is active). Endpoints: `apporteurs/`, `deals-enregistres/` (+ `approuver/`, `rejeter/`, `a-payer/`); public read-only `GET /api/django/crm/apporteur-portail/<token>/mes-deals/` (token-only, never a session).
- **Defi** (NTCRM23/24/28) — a temporary, PUBLIC team gamification challenge (date window + leaderboard), distinct from the private/permanent individual `ObjectifCommercial` (FG39); reuses `ObjectifCommercial.Metric` strictly (no second metric taxonomy). `selectors.classement_defi` ranks users by the défi's metric over its window. Endpoints: `defis/` CRUD (+ `classement/` NTCRM24 leaderboard, `export-xlsx/` NTCRM28), frontend `/crm/defis` (DefisPage, visible to the whole team, not just managers).
- **NTCRM26/30 — playbook + account-review gap closure.** `Playbook.condition` (JSON `core.rules` condition tree evaluated against `{type_installation, canal}`, empty = applies to every lead) + `selectors.playbooks_recommandes(lead, stage)` (similarity-recommended playbooks, consumed by the pre-existing `lead_playbook_view`). **NTCRM30** closes the backend test-coverage gap on `PlanCompte`/`RevueCompte` (structured account-review notes, scaffolded by NTCRM10/11 migration crm/0063, frontend already wired by PACT105 — previously undocumented here): a review posted to `revues-compte/` appears immediately, most-recent-first, in the account plan's timeline.

### ventes — quotes, orders, invoices, credit notes, payments, quote engine
The largest app: full quote→order→invoice→recovery lifecycle plus the vendored
premium quote-PDF engine.
- **Devis** (quote) — `company` FK; `reference` (per company+month); `client` FK→crm.Client; `lead` FK→crm.Lead (nullable, lead-primary quoting); `statut` (**brouillon/envoye/accepte/refuse/expire**); `mode_installation` (residentiel/industriel/agricole); `option_acceptee` (sans_batterie/avec_batterie); `etude_params` JSON (kWc, production, autoconso, payback, pump CV/HMT/débit…); `taux_tva`, `remise_globale`; versioning (`version`, `version_parent`, `superseded_by`, `is_active`); discount approval (`remise_approuvee`, `remise_approuvee_par`); `fichier_pdf` (MinIO key). **FG52** adds `devise` (ISO 4217, default MAD) + `taux_change` to **Devis** and **Facture** (and `parametres.CompanyProfile.devise_defaut`): on API create without an explicit devise, the company default is applied (fallback MAD); the premium PDF `fmt()` and UBL export (`dgi_export.py`/`utils/ubl.py`) emit the document currency. No base-currency conversion (currency is document-borne). **SCA47** adds `prix_par_kwc` (Total TTC ÷ kWc, DecimalField) — derived and FROZEN write-once at first save when a kWc + total exist (null for kWc-less/pompage devis, never recomputed after); internal generator/BI signal ONLY, same regime as `stock.Produit.prix_achat` — never on a PDF or client-facing output. **SCA39** adds `Meta.indexes` `(company, statut)` + `(company, date_creation)` on Devis and Facture (concurrent `AddIndex`, no blocking lock) — the money-path query subset of NTPLT20. **NTCPQ16** adds `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (self-FK + économique/standard/premium tag — a tier variant is a full draft Devis, distinct from the size-variant `version_parent`/QJ15 and the sans/avec-batterie couple). **NTCPQ13** adds `devis_origine`/`numero_renouvellement` (self-FK to the renewal chain's root devis; the source devis is never touched — distinct from the T10 revision, which only applies to a not-yet-accepted devis). **NTCPQ11** adds `clauses_appliquees` (JSON snapshot of the applicable `cpq.ClauseCGV` list, frozen once at brouillon→envoyé, read-only by the quote_engine, never a new renderer).
- **LigneDevis** — `devis` FK, `produit` FK→stock.Produit, `designation`, `quantite`, `prix_unitaire`, `remise`, `taux_tva` (nullable → falls back to devis rate; 10% panels / 20% other). **NTCPQ18** adds an optional `lot` FK→`LotDevis` (NULL = hors lot, historical behaviour unchanged).
- **NTCPQ13/14/16/18/20 devis satellites** — `ConfigurationDevisSnapshot` (auto fine-grained pre-send config history on a brouillon devis, distinct from version revision), `LotDevis` (multi-site devis: groups `LigneDevis` for its own sub-total + a consolidated total; coexists with the QJ29 multi-villa `groupe_index` display grouping), `AvenantDevis` (amendment diff on an already-accepted devis — `lignes_ajoutees`/`lignes_retirees` JSON snapshots, never `prix_achat`; may re-trigger the NTCPQ7 approval matrix via `approbation_requise`). Endpoints: `devis/{id}/renouveler/` (NTCPQ13), `devis/{id}/lots/` (NTCPQ18), `devis/{id}/avenants/` (NTCPQ14), `devis/{id}/historique-configuration/` (NTCPQ20).
- **BonCommande** (client order) — `devis` OneToOne→Devis (nullable), `client` FK; `statut` (**en_attente/confirme/livre/annule**); marking `livre` decrements stock.
- **Facture** (invoice) — `devis` FK (new échéancier path) **and/or** `bon_commande` OneToOne (legacy path); `client` FK; `type_facture` (**acompte/intermediaire/solde/complete**); `statut` (**brouillon/emise/payee/en_retard/annulee**); `pourcentage`, `libelle`, frozen `montant_ht/tva/ttc`; recovery (`prochaine_relance`, `exclu_relances`); computed `montant_paye`, `avoirs_total`, `montant_du` (= TTC − paid − credits); `fichier_pdf/ubl`. **ARC24** adds an optional `condition_paiement_ref` FK→`parametres.ConditionPaiement` (source of the default `conditions_paiement` label; the TextField stays master/overridable).
- **LigneFacture** — same shape as LigneDevis (`facture` FK).
- **Paiement** — `facture` FK; `montant`, `date_paiement`, `mode` (especes/virement/cheque/carte/prelevement/autre). **SCA45** adds provider-agnostic `provider_ref` + `idempotency_key` (company-scoped uniqueness) so an external payment gateway integration can reconcile/replay-guard without a bespoke field per provider.
- **Avoir** (credit note) + **LigneAvoir** — `facture` FK (PROTECT), `client` FK; `statut` (emise/annulee); `motif`; frozen amounts; offsets the invoice's `montant_du`.
- **DevisActivity** — quote chatter (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE), like LeadActivity.
- **FollowupLevel / RelanceLog** — recovery escalation tiers and per-invoice follow-up trace.
- **ShareLink** — public tokenized link (`token` unique, `devis`/`facture` FK, `expires_at`, 30-day) for WhatsApp PDF delivery without login.
- **ParametresGammes** — PVMRQ (fondateur 18/08) per-company singleton: `deux_gammes` toggle, renamable `nom_essentielle`/`nom_premium`, `marques` JSON (fixed slots Essentielle/Premium → {role: marque}, roles = `ROLES_AUTO_COMPOSITION` mirroring frontend `PRODUCT_CATEGORIES`); read by `_pick_product` (pinned brand wins; missing brand → None, never a substitute) and by the quote-generator auto-fill. Endpoint `GET/PATCH parametres-gammes/` (GET any authenticated company user, PATCH responsable/admin). Migration ventes/0097.
- **MandatPaiement** (XCTR22, previously undocumented here) — recurring payment mandate on a tokenized card (no card data ever enters the ERP); endpoint `mandats-paiement/`. **PACT43** gives it its first internal screen (`/ventes/mandats-paiement`, responsable/admin — list by statut + revoke).
- **RemiseEncaissement** + **LigneRemiseEncaissement** (XFSM19, previously undocumented here) — field cash/cheque deposit declarations (technicien declares, responsable clôture, gap never hidden) + PDF bordereau; endpoint `remises-encaissement/`. **PACT46** gives it its first screen (`/ventes/remises-encaissement`, declaration open to any role, clôture responsable/admin).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/ventes/`): `devis/`, `devis-lignes/`, `bons-commande/`, `factures/`, `paiements/`, `avoirs/` ViewSets; key custom actions: `devis/{id}/proposal/` (**canonical quote PDF, sync**), `devis/{id}/generer-pdf/` (**async Celery**), `devis/{id}/telecharger-pdf`, `devis/{id}/accepter|reviser|approuver-remise|historique|noter`, `devis/{id}/convertir-bc`, `devis/{id}/generer-facture`; **U3 (fondateur 20/08/2026) — LA composition résidentielle est SERVEUR** : `POST devis/auto/` compose ET crée le devis depuis la fiche lead (accepte `target_kwc`/`scenario`/`etude_params` pour CE devis, sans réécrire le lead ; défaut U2 = les DEUX options sans+avec batterie), et `POST devis/composition/` est son DRY-RUN (compose et rend les lignes sans RIEN créer, contrat figé dans `apps/ventes/contract_samples/devis_composition.json`) — les deux lisent la même fonction pure `services.composition_residentielle`, qui porte désormais câbles au mètre, marques épinglées (PVMRQ) et ordre des lignes (PVORD) ; le frontend résidentiel ne compose plus rien ; `bons-commande/{id}/confirmer|marquer-livre|annuler|creer-facture`, `bons-commande/{id}/pdf/` (**ZSAL8** — client BC printable PDF, no buy price, company-scoped 404); recovery (`relances/`, `balance-agee/`, `clients/{id}/releve(-pdf)/`, `factures/{id}/lettre-relance-pdf/`, `niveaux-relance/`); accounting (`journal-ventes/` .xlsx, `numerotation-audit/`); public `GET /api/django/public/document/{token}/` (tokenized PDF, no auth, no buy prices). **WIR99** adds `devis/prefill-site/?client=<id>` — wires the pre-existing `crm.SiteProfile` (DC12, a per-client reusable energy/roof/pumping profile — see §4 crm) into the quote-generator prefill for a lead-less devis, via `crm.selectors.site_profile_for_client` (never a `crm.models` import; company-scoped). **WIR96** adds `devis/{id}/suivi-partage/` — read-only marketing follow-up on a devis (`ShareLink` open tracking + abandoned-quote reminders) via `apps.marketing.selectors` (never a `marketing.models` import); the public-proposal `_stamp_view` open hook now also mirrors into `marketing.OuverturePartage` (was routed but previously never written).
- **models_regulatory.py — FG268-271 dossier réglementaire cluster** (previously undocumented here): **RegulatoryDossier** (raccordement dossier: ONEE/ANRE/distributeur deposit tracking, `devis` FK **PROTECT** since **YDATA2**/migration ventes/0090 — was CASCADE, now a regulatory-proof record survives a devis edge case) + **DossierChecklistItem** (per-step checklist) + **DossierExchange** (FG269 operator-shuttle exchange journal) + **SubventionDossier** (FG270 subsidy eligibility/tracking, `devis` FK also switched to **PROTECT**) + **Regularisation8221** (FG271 Article-33/82-21 regularization workflow, `devis` FK stays `SET_NULL`). Endpoints `dossiers-reglementaires/`, `dossiers-checklist/`, `dossiers-echanges/`, `subventions/`, `regularisations-8221/`. **WIR104** is a recorded LOCALITY DECISION: this cluster stays in `ventes` (the regulatory/paperwork side of the affaire) rather than moving to `installations` (which owns the physical EXECUTION side — `CommissioningRecord`/`HandoverPack`/checklist) — merging them would force a destructive cross-app migration for no gain; it also closes the gap by giving these viewsets a real screen (`/ventes/dossiers-reglementaires`) and retires the duplicate `ventes.FicheTechnique` API surface (`/ventes/fiches-techniques/` had zero caller — removed from `urls.py`; `/stock/fiches-techniques/` is now the ONLY fiche-technique surface; the `ventes.FicheTechnique` model/table is kept, unmigrated, for a possible future data merge).
- **Toiture-3D devis web loop** (`/api/django/ventes/`): `devis/from-layout/` (build a Devis from a finalized roofPro11 layout + mint a proposal `ShareLink`), `devis/{id}/layout/` & `devis/{id}/roof-image/` (store the finalized layout + 3D snapshot); public tokenized proposal channel — `GET proposal/{token}/` (JSON quote data incl. `monthly_production`/`monthly_consumption` + `roof_image_url`), `POST proposal/{token}/accept/` (client e-signature → existing accept service), `GET proposal/{token}/pdf/` (client devis PDF). The website capture page (`/devis/mon-toit`) posts the enriched lead (exact bills, `ete_differente`, `raccordement` incl. `inconnu`, reverse-geocoded `adresse`/GPS) to the CRM webhook; **Meriem designs INSIDE the ERP** (authenticated React route `frontend` `/devis-design/:id`, same-origin cookie session — the roofPro11 builder is Vite-alias-imported from `apps/web`, no second login) and the client signs at the public `/proposition/<token>`. `GET /api/django/ventes/roof-config/` exposes the public MapTiler key same-origin (needs `PUBLIC_MAPTILER_KEY` in the ERP env).
- **quote_engine/** — premium PDF engine. `builder.py` maps an OS Devis → the generator data dict (only sell-side `prix_unitaire`; `prix_achat` excluded) and routes by market mode to one of three renderers: `residential/` (redesigned 3-page residential proposal), `agricole/` (4-page pompage-solaire proposal — cover/at-a-glance, étude+schéma+charts, équipement+prix+FDA+garanties, rentabilité solaire-vs-butane-vs-diesel+signature; modules `renderer/render/theme/cover/study/yield_page/economics_page/charts/schematic/economics/constants/sample_data`), and the legacy `generate_devis_premium.py` (one-page + industriel + fallback). `installations.py` = shared cover-hero photo library that picks the installation photo whose kWc is **nearest** the quote (agricole falls back to residential/industriel of similar power); photos in `assets/installations/<mode>-<kwc>.jpg`. `pricing.py`, `catalog.py`. Buy-price exclusion asserted by `apps/ventes/tests/test_quote_engine_formats.py`; agricole engine by `test_agricole_quote.py`. The old 3 704-line `test_quote_engine.py` was split (19/08/2026) into `_formats` (page counts/totaux/TVA), `_residential`, `_documents`, `_builder` + `_quote_engine_common.py` (shared fixtures); `test_quote_engine.py` remains as a compatibility shim re-exporting those fixtures.
- **solar_design.py** (FG246/247/249) — electrical-engineering helpers: `string_design` (distributes N panels across the inverter MPPT inputs, checks string Vmp/Voc at cold temperature vs the MPPT/voltage window, reports the DC/AC ratio), `match_inverter` (picks a compatible catalogue inverter, classification keywords aligned with `builder.py`, never a price-less product), `optimize_orientation` (tilt/azimuth sweep via the existing PVGIS client). Pure + fully tested (`tests/test_solar_design.py`); not yet surfaced in an endpoint. **FG255** adds `ev_charger_sizing` — EV charging-station (borne de recharge VE) sizing: line current (mono 230 V / tri 400 V √3), dedicated breaker calibre, charge duration/window-fit, recommended standard borne (3.7/7.4/11/22 kW), and PV coupling (solar surplus feeds the borne first, reporting solar-covered kWh + the lifted autoconsommation rate). Pure math, input-freedom preserved, no model/endpoint/PDF change. **FG256** adds `battery_storage_sizing` — two objectives: MAX autoconsommation (store daytime surplus, capped by the re-dischargeable night load) vs BACKUP for N critical hours (usable kWh/kW from critical load × hours); returns usable + nominal kWh (÷ DoD×√round-trip), recommended pack and the binding objective. Pure math. **FG257** adds `simulate_bankable_yield` — financial-grade P50/P90: Performance Ratio = Π(1−loss) over temperature/soiling/wiring/inverter/mismatch/availability, P50 = base×PR, P90/P75 via the Gaussian lower-tail quantile (z=1.282/0.674) of an annual-variability σ (default 6 %); returns PR, loss breakdown, P50/P90/P75, optional specific yield. Pure math. **FG258** adds `hourly_self_consumption` — per-hour min(load, production) over an 8760-h (or 24-h) load curve → real autoconsommation rate, coverage rate, injected surplus, grid import; typical residential/commercial load + PV profiles as fallbacks, and `load_curve_from_xlsx` (parses a column via the already-present openpyxl). Pure math, divide-by-zero guarded. **FG259** adds `net_metering_savings` — values the injected hourly surplus (from FG258) under Moroccan net-metering (loi 13-09): compensated only up to same-tranche import × `compensation_ratio` at the tranche tariff (pointe/pleine/creuse), honours the `surplus_injecte_compense` toggle, optional annual cap + residual spill tariff. Pure math. **FG260** adds `tariff_escalation_projection` — a 20–25-year financial model: year-by-year escalated savings (ONEE escalation × module degradation), cumulative + net, simple & discounted payback year, **NPV (VAN)** and **IRR (TRI)** solved by stdlib bisection/Newton (capped iterations, `None` on non-convergence). Pure math. **FG261** adds `optimize_subscribed_power` — for C&I clients: net grid demand (load−PV) → post-PV peak → recommended subscribed power = ceil(peak×margin) (never above current) + the annual capacity-charge saving, optional kW→kVA via power factor. Pure math. **FG262** adds `module_degradation_curve` — per-year PV production factor (compound or linear, with a year-1 LID drop) confronted against manufacturer warranty floors (e.g. 90% @ yr10, 80% @ yr25), flagging the first breach year + shortfall. Pure math.
- **compatibilites.py** (PVCOMPAT, 21/08/2026) — pairwise electrical-compatibility verdicts `compatible/reserve/incompatible/inconnu` (severity MIRRORS `core.electrique`: current exceedance = alerte/écrêtage, only voltage blocks): `verdict_panneau_onduleur`, `verdict_batterie_onduleur` (delegates to the existing data-driven battery-window rule), `compatibilites_du_produit` (the contract shape of `apps/stock/contract_samples/produit_compatibilites.json` — installability bilan for inverters, ✓/✗ per family, French reasons, price-less products included, NO price ever), `normaliser_phase`. Exposed cross-app via 3 thin `selectors.py` re-exports; consumed by the stock endpoint + `composition_residentielle` (panel↔inverter check with ordered re-pick, never a dead composition; declared lead `raccordement` filters mono / hard-prefers tri, undeclared = byte-identical). `solar_design.py` now plumbs currents (isc/imp, i_max/isc_max — were hardcoded 0.0) and exposes `verdicts_chaines()`.
- **selectors.py** `lignes_devis_pour_automatisation(devis_id, company, *, limite=200)` — read-only, company-scoped devis lines for an automation FOR_EACH loop; no `prix_achat`/margin ever exposed.
- **PalierRemiseVolume** (NTCPQ17) — company-wide automatic volume-discount tier, distinct from the per-price-list `RegleListePrix` (XSAL2): priority-ordered cascade, optional `cumulable` (stacks across categories on top of the line discount), `categorie_nom` string-ref to `stock.Categorie` (never a `stock.models` import); never touches `prix_achat`. Enriches the existing `prix-applicable/?panier=<produit>:<qte>,…` response with a `remise_volume` decomposition (all-zero when no tier configured — historical response unchanged).
- **utils/references.py** — numbering = highest-used + 1 per company+month (savepoint + retry on races); never `count()+1`. **ARC6** relocates the algorithm to `core.numbering` (the foundation home ~15 apps import across app boundaries); this module is now a byte-identical re-export shim.
- **Sweep + performance (ARC4/5, SCA39-43/45/47/49).** **ARC5** sweeps all 21 ventes ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (unified scoping; the public `/proposal` + `ShareLink` endpoints stay explicitly excluded/documented, asserted by `test_arc5_proposal_public_access.py`). **SCA40** collapses the `par_commercial` reporting aggregate into one grouped query (ends an unbounded N+1). **SCA41** makes xlsx exports async past a row-count threshold (`ventes/export/status/` polling, 202 response, company-scoped MinIO key, pilot NTPLT29/30) — wired into **FactureList** (journal ventes / export comptable: on a 202 it polls `ventesApi.exportStatus(jobId)` then downloads). **SCA43** wires `core.request_cache` under the devis-list PDF-preview path (QPERF1 landing — the previously-skipped Devis query budget test now passes). **SCA49** freezes the Devis JSON contract (`etude_params` included) with a serializer-keys snapshot test (`docs/devis-json-contract.md`). **ARC28** adds `apps/ventes/platform.py` (searchable/record/import surfaces). **ARC49/53** — see §5 frontend for the DevisList/FactureList migration onto the shared DataTable engine (parity proven, PDF/status flows untouched; see `docs/parity/ARC49-devislist-parite.md` and `ARC53-facturelist-parite.md`).
- **dgi/** (N105, silent DGI capability) — `dgi_export.py` (`build_ubl_xml`, UBL 2.1 invoice via stdlib `xml.etree`, carries seller+client ICE, per-line VAT, totals; no buy price), `dgi_validator.py` (`validate_dgi_conformity` → list of FR problem messages), `toggle.py` (`is_dgi_enabled(company)`). Armed only by `parametres.CompanyProfile.dgi_export_actif` (default **OFF**): the two facture actions `dgi-export`/`dgi-conformite` and the `dgi_export_facture` management command **404/refuse when OFF**, and the Facture model/serializer/lists are byte-identical (no field, badge, status or column added). Simpl-TVA transmission + certified e-signature remain out of scope (G14). **FG263** adds `solar_design.ppa_model(...)` — a PPA / third-party-investor financial model (pure math): applies FG262 degradation, computes investor revenue (production × PPA tariff + escalation − O&M; NPV/IRR/payback reusing FG260) and client savings (grid − PPA tariff), both perspectives. No model; quote-PDF path untouched. **FG264** adds `solar_design.pumping_cycle_yield(...)` — pure-math daily/monthly pumped-water volume by operating cycle (flat mode = byte-parity with `solar.js`; profile mode integrates débit hour-by-hour weighted by a normalized clearsky irradiation profile); curve-less pumps → None. No model; quote-PDF path untouched.

### stock — catalogue, suppliers, inventory, procurement
Product catalogue, multi-supplier sourcing, stock movements/locations, supplier POs
and returns.
- **Produit** — `company` FK; `nom`, `sku` (unique per company); `prix_vente` (sell HT); **`prix_achat`** (buy price — internal/generator-only, **never client-facing**); `quantite_stock` (canonical), `seuil_alerte`; `categorie`/`fournisseur` FK; commercial sheet (`marque`, `description`, `garantie`, `garantie_mois`, `garantie_production_mois`); pump specs (`pompe_cv`, `hmt_m`, `pompe_kw`, `tension_v`, `courbe_pompe` JSON); `is_archived`; `custom_data` JSON. **ARC27** adds an optional `unite` FK→`parametres.UniteMesure`, mirroring the existing free-text `unite_stock` (which stays master; backfilled via `backfill_unites_mesure`).
- **Protection du catalogue RÉEL (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** `Fournisseur` gagne `is_archived` (migration `stock/0082`) et le cycle produit : `destroy` ARCHIVE au lieu de détruire quand des données le retiennent, `?show_archived=true`, `PATCH unarchive/`, `DELETE force-delete/` (409 si un tarif subsiste). `achats.PrixFournisseur.fournisseur` passe **CASCADE → PROTECT** (`achats/0003`) : supprimer un fournisseur ne peut plus détruire les prix d'achat NÉGOCIÉS. `FournisseurAdmin`/`ProduitAdmin`/`CompanyAdmin`/`ClientAdmin` refusent la suppression depuis l'admin Django (4 verrous, message nommant le chemin supporté) ; `ProduitAdminForm` refuse de VIDER un `prix_achat`/`courbe_pompe` déjà saisi. `CategorieAdmin` reste délibérément sans garde (SET_NULL : les lignes produit survivent).
- **Categorie / Fournisseur / Marque** — referentials (Marque/`archived`). **ARC18** adds a nullable `Fournisseur.tiers` FK (migration stock/0075, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC24** adds an optional `Fournisseur.condition_paiement_ref` FK→`parametres.ConditionPaiement` mirroring the existing `delai_paiement_jours`/`fin_de_mois`/`escompte_pct` fields (backfilled via `backfill_conditions_paiement`).
- N14 (reservation-aware availability): `ProduitSerializer` exposes computed `quantite_reservee`, `quantite_disponible` (= stock − active reservations from `installations.StockReservation`) and a reservation-aware low-stock flag; the legacy `is_low_stock` and `compute_besoin_materiel` are preserved (a chantier's own reservation is not double-counted).
- **MouvementStock** — `produit` FK; `type_mouvement` (entree/sortie/transfert/ajustement); `quantite_avant/apres`; `created_by`; the audit trail for every quantity change.
- **EmplacementStock / StockEmplacement / TransfertStock** — stock locations, per-location quantities (principal derived), and transfers between them.
- **PrixFournisseur** — per-supplier `prix_achat` (internal) for cheapest-sourcing.
- **BonCommandeFournisseur** + **LigneBonCommandeFournisseur** — supplier purchase orders; `statut` (brouillon/envoye/recu/annule); receipt increments stock via MouvementStock.
- **RetourFournisseur** + **LigneRetourFournisseur** — supplier returns; `statut` (brouillon/valide/annule); validation decrements stock.
- **APX18 — photo produit.** `POST/DELETE /stock/produits/<id>/photo/` : la photo n'est PAS un `ImageField` (la garde plateforme ARC26 les interdit) mais un `records.Attachment` (MinIO, cible de registre `stock.produit`), `Produit.photo` n'etant que le pointeur vers la photo canonique — validation par octets magiques, plafond 10 Mo, stockage cloisonne par societe et telechargement same-origin herites. Donnee INTERNE : jamais dans un PDF ni aucune sortie client, jamais rendue a cote de `prix_achat`.
- **Groupe NTWMS — couche entrepôt (`models_wms.py`).** Warehouse execution layered on the existing catalogue/movement models — never a parallel bin hierarchy: zone/allée/casier (`BinLocation`) stays owned by `installations` (FG319), referenced here by string-FK only. **VaguePicking** — multi-source picking wave (`reference` race-safe, `mode_liberation` manuel/auto_heure/auto_seuil), + **LignePicking** (produit/quantité/`bin`/`lot`, `ordre_parcours` = physical walk order). **UniteLogistique** — addressable colis/palette carrying a GS1 SSCC (mod-10 checksum), self-FK `parent` for palette→colis nesting, `sceller()` freezes contents; + **UniteLogistiqueLigne** (contenu + scan audit). **Quai** + **RendezVousTransporteur** — dock scheduling with a server-side overlap guard (`save()`, never just `clean()`) and a `code_checkin` (unpredictable, `secrets`) for the driver kiosk (no ERP login, NTWMS8). **ExpeditionTransporteur** — one shipment of one `UniteLogistique` via a carrier provider (aucun/Amana/DHL/Chronopost/autre; `aucun` = internal label, zero network call). **PlanComptageTournant** — cycle-counting frequency per ABC class, feeds targeted `InventaireSession`s (never a parallel inventory mechanism).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/stock/`): `produits/`, `categories/`, `fournisseurs/`, `marques/`, `mouvements/` (read-only), `bons-commande-fournisseur/`, `emplacements/`, `transferts/`, `prix-fournisseurs/`, `retours-fournisseur/`; NTWMS: `vagues-picking/` (+ `{id}/lancer|configurer-liberation`, `{id}/lignes/{ligne_id}/prelever`), `unites-logistiques/` (+ `{id}/lignes|controler-scan|sceller|etiquette-pdf`), `quais/` (+ `planning`), `rendez-vous-transporteur/`, `expeditions/` (+ `{id}/generer-etiquette|tracking`, `tarifs`), `plans-comptage-tournant/` (+ `generer`); public `POST /stock/public/quai-checkin/` (driver kiosk, no login) + `scanner/resoudre|mouvement/` (mobile scanner station).
- **PACT51** mounts `/stock/paiements-fournisseur` in the `stock` frontend module for the pre-existing `apps.achats.PaiementFournisseur` ledger (XPUR2 RAS-TVA net-payout, XPUR6) — `apps/achats` itself is a separate backend app not otherwise written up in this CODEMAP; its Simpl-TVA export/cross-fournisseur treasury view/escompte flag had no caller anywhere before this screen.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 25 stock ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`. **ARC22** adds `services.create_sous_traitant` — the entry point `gestion_projet.services.creer_sous_traitant_via_master` calls (function-local import) to create the matching `Fournisseur`(type=service)+`SousTraitantProfile` when a project subcontractor is created via the master path, without a cross-app model import. **ARC28** adds `apps/stock/platform.py`.
- **PVCOMPAT** (21/08/2026) — `GET produits/{id}/compatibilites/` (read-only, `IsAnyRole` via the explicit `get_permissions` case, contract `contract_samples/produit_compatibilites.json`): installability + per-family compatibility verdicts computed by `apps.ventes.selectors.compatibilites_du_produit` (cross-app read via selectors, never a models import); rendered by the « Compatibilités » tab of `ProduitDetail` (frontend), which also links to Paramètres → Gammes & marques (brand priority).
- **Procure-to-pay (NTP2P), migrations stock/0086-0089.** Three OFF-by-default toggles on `AchatsParametres`: `budget_departement_actif` (NTP2P4), `onboarding_fournisseur_obligatoire` (NTP2P7), `sod_stricte` (NTP2P37, self-approval guard reused by `frais`). **BudgetDepartement** (NTP2P4) — per-company/`rh.Departement` (string-FK)/année(+mois) spend envelope, annuelle or mensuelle; **EngagementBudget** tracks amount committed by a `DemandeAchat` (actif/libere/consomme). `selectors.verifier_budget_disponible(company, departement_id, periode, montant)` is the pure read check (`controle_actif=False` ⇒ never blocks); `stock.services`/`installations.services.controler_budget_demande_achat` call it at submission. **DossierOnboardingFournisseur** (NTP2P7, 1-1 `Fournisseur`) — en_attente/documents_recus/valide/rejete; **DocumentFournisseur** (its pièces) stores only a `file_key` in MinIO prefixed by company (never a `FileField`, ARC26). **FavorisCatalogueAchat** (NTP2P22) — one row per (company, user) with a JSON list of pinned `Produit` ids, backing `?recent=1`/`favoris/`. `selectors.score_risque_fournisseur(company, fournisseur_id)` (NTP2P8) — pure 0-100 computation (ponctualité + pièces + historique), no external call.
- **CatalogueAchatViewSet** (NTP2P3, `views/catalogue_achat.py`) — read-only catalogue for non-admin demandeurs, exposes `id`/`nom`/`sku`/`categorie`/`fournisseur_prefere`/`prix_achat_dernier` and DELIBERATELY never `prix_vente` (no margin derivable client-side). Endpoints (`/api/django/stock/`): `catalogue-achat/` (+ `favoris/`, `?recent=1`), `budgets-departement/` (+ `{id}/consommation/`, `disponible/`), `engagements-budget/`, `dossiers-onboarding-fournisseur/` (+ `valider-dossier/`, `progression/`), `documents-fournisseur/` (+ `televerser/`, `telecharger/`), `fournisseurs/{id}/onboarding/`, `fournisseurs/{id}/score-risque/`, `fournisseurs/export-conformite/?export=xlsx`.

### pos — vente comptoir / point of sale (Groupe XPOS, new app)  *( `/api/django/pos/` )*
Counter sales of accessories, built entirely on the existing ventes/stock/compta write paths (never a bespoke ledger).
- **Models** — `VenteComptoir` + `LigneVenteComptoir` (XPOS1: a counter sale that, on `valider/`, becomes a real `ventes.Facture` + `Paiement`(s) + a stock movement via `ventes.services`/`stock.services` — never a direct model import; `uuid_client` dedups an OFFLINE-created sale replayed on reconnect, NTRET1; `montant_arrhes`/`marchandise_remise` + `EN_ATTENTE_SOLDE` statut = deposit-on-order for backordered/custom items, NTRET5), `SessionCaisse` (XPOS4: caisse open/close, backed by the EXISTING `compta.Caisse` via `compta.services` — no duplicate cash journal; `numero_rapport_z` race-safe one-shot legal Z-report number, NTRET2; `montant_tpe_compte`/`ecart_tpe` card-terminal reconciliation, XPOS18), `ShareLinkTicket` (XPOS3: tokenized public link to the ticket PDF, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`, kept local to `apps/pos`), `CommandeRetrait` + `LigneCommandeRetrait` (XPOS15: click-and-collect, à_preparer→pret→retire), `ConfigMaterielPOS` (XPOS18: ESC/POS printer config + TPE card-terminal reconciliation), `CodePinCaissier` (NTRET3: hashed quick-lock PIN per company+user — switch cashier on a shared till without a full re-login or losing the in-progress cart).
- Endpoints: `ventes/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/valider`, `{id}/promotions`, `{id}/coupon`, `{id}/arrhes|solde-arrhes|remettre-marchandise` (NTRET5), `{id}/ticket-pdf|ticket-arrhes-pdf|ticket-escpos|ticket-share-link`, `encaisser-facture/`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau`, `factures-recherche/`, `dashboard/`, `dashboard-export/`, `dashboard-retail/` + `dashboard-retail-export/` (NTRET16 — panier moyen/transformation/ventes-m²/top produits, reads `parametres.BoutiquePos`)), `sessions/` (+ `{id}/cloturer`, `{id}/rapport-x` (NTRET2, in-progress read-only), `{id}/rapport-z|rapport-z-pdf` (official, one-shot numbered)), `retraits/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/marquer-pret`, `{id}/remettre`), `config-materiel/`; `verifier-pin/`+`definir-pin/` (NTRET3, PIN lock); public `GET /api/django/public/pos/ticket/<token>/` (tokenized ticket PDF, no auth, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`). Promotions/coupons/gift cards are `apps.promotions` models consumed here (never duplicated) — see the `promotions` section below.

### installations — chantiers / field execution
Installation projects spun up once a quote is signed, through to commissioning and
closure; work orders, checklists, regulatory (law 82-21) tracking. **ARC3** sweeps
all 61 installations ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour
identical). **ARC28** adds `apps/installations/platform.py`.
- **Installation** (chantier) — `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `devis` FK→ventes.Devis; `bon_commande` FK→ventes.BonCommande; `lead` FK→crm.Lead; `statut` (SIGNE/MATERIEL_COMMANDE/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/INSTALLE/RECEPTIONNE/CLOTURE + legacy values); `puissance_installee_kwc`; `type_installation`; `technicien_responsable` FK; `bom` JSON (frozen BoM from devis); `regime_8221` + `dossier_statut` (regulatory); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`; milestone dates.
- **StockReservation** (N14) — `company` FK; `installation` FK; `produit` FK→stock.Produit; `quantite`; `consomme` bool (`unique_together (installation, produit)`). Seeded from the chantier's frozen `bom` at creation; consumed exactly once when the chantier reaches the canonical INSTALLE statut (one `MouvementStock` SORTIE per SKU, idempotent under `select_for_update()`/atomic — re-entering INSTALLE emits nothing); cancel/close releases the remaining (un-consumed) reservation. Drives the reservation-aware availability on the stock serializer (réservé vs disponible) and low-stock alerts.
- **Intervention** (sortie chantier, F3) — `installation` FK; `ticket` FK→sav.Ticket (nullable); `type_intervention` (POSE/RACCORDEMENT/MISE_EN_SERVICE/CONTROLE/DEPANNAGE); `technicien` FK; `equipe` M2M→users (default = chantier installer, set server-side); `camionnette` FK→stock.EmplacementStock (nullable); `date_prevue/realisee`; **`statut`** — its OWN ordered state machine (`a_preparer/prete/en_route/sur_site/terminee/validee` + `STATUT_ORDER`, default `a_preparer`) **completely separate from the chantier statut and the STAGES.py contract** (changing it never touches either). Réf/client/devis/ville/GPS are read-only, pulled from the chantier.
- **InterventionActivity** (F3) — per-intervention chatter (same pattern as InstallationActivity), helper `intervention_activity.py` (creation + tracked-field changes incl. statut + manual notes; user/company server-side).
- **ChecklistTemplate** (N74) — `company` FK; `nom`; `type_installation` (nullable; auto-selects the template for a chantier of that market); `ordre`; `actif`; `protege` (the per-company "Défaut" fallback that carries today's 7 steps). **ChecklistEtapeModele / ChantierChecklistItem** — template steps (now FK→ChecklistTemplate, `unique_together (company, template, cle)`) and per-chantier checklist state; `capture_serie` flags serial-number capture steps (feeds the equipment registry); `fait` bool. Auto-selection (`template_for_installation`, services.py) matches by `type_installation`, falls back to Défaut — behaviour preserved.
- **TypeIntervention / InstallationActivity** — configurable intervention types and chantier chatter.
- **JalonProjet / ModeleProjet (+ ModeleProjetJalon, ModeleProjetBomLigne) / ReunionChantier** (FG293/296/298, `models_projet.py`) — project milestones/phases (étude/appro/pose/MES/réception with `date_cible`/`date_reelle`/`atteint`), chantier-type templates (`services.instantiate_modele_projet` pre-creates standard jalons + appends BoM-type lines to the frozen `bom`, idempotent + additive), and timestamped site-meeting minutes (ordre du jour/présents/décisions/actions, author + company server-side). Endpoints `jalons-projet/`, `modeles-projet/` (+ `{id}/instancier/`), `reunions-chantier/`.
- **FG291 — Projet (programme multi-chantiers, `models_program.py`)** — a `Projet` regrouping the chantiers + devis + tickets of one client/site (ferme à 4 forages, toiture par tranches), with link tables **ProjetChantier/ProjetDevis/ProjetTicket** referencing other apps by **string FK only** (`ventes.Devis`/`sav.Ticket`/`crm.Client` — resolved via FK metadata + tenant-checked, never imported). Its OWN status machine (`brouillon/actif/en_pause/termine/annule`) — **independent of STAGES.py and of devis/ticket document statuses** (attachment never touches them). Reference `PRG-` via the race-safe factory; company/created_by forced server-side. Idempotent `attacher_chantier|devis|ticket` actions (get_or_create); ViewSets `programmes/`, `programme-chantiers/`, `programme-devis/`, `programme-tickets/`. NEW architectural component.
- **FG292 — ProjetTache** (project tasks/sub-tasks with dependencies, on `models_program.py`): `projet` FK→Projet (same-app), `parent` self-FK (sub-tasks), `predecesseur` self-FK (dependency), `assigne` FK→user, `date_echeance`, own `Statut` (a_faire/en_cours/termine — NOT STAGES.py), `ordre`. `clean()` cycle-guards BOTH `parent` and `predecesseur` (rollback in an atomic block). ViewSet `programme-taches/` (`?projet`/`?statut`/`?parent`/`?assigne` filters, tenant checks on every FK).
- **FG294 — BudgetProjet + BudgetEngagement** (budget projet vs réel): `BudgetProjet` (1-to-1 with Projet; HT envelopes matériel/main-d'œuvre/sous-traitance/divers + `tarif_jour_mo` + `seuil_alerte_pct`) and `BudgetEngagement` (attaches a supplier cost — BCF/facture — by string-FK). `selectors.budget_projet_synthese` aggregates ACTUALS — devis via `apps.get_model('ventes','Devis')`, BCF/FactureFournisseur via function-local `apps.stock.selectors`, labour from same-app Installation — vs budget with an over-budget flag (no cross-app model import; import-linter 4/4). ViewSets `programme-budgets/` (+ `synthese`), `programme-engagements/`. INTERNE (responsable/admin — exposes purchase costs). **FG295** adds `selectors.projet_pnl` — consolidated project P&L: REVENUE (client factures on the project's devis, cancelled excluded) − COSTS (matériel/sous-traitance/imports via FG294 engagements + main-d'œuvre) → `marge_brute` + `marge_pct`; action `programmes/{id}/pnl/` (responsable/admin, cross-app reads reuse FG294's `get_model`/selector helpers — import-linter safe). No model. **FG299** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge_equipes` — the FIELD-TEAM workload view: per-technicien capacity (working days × hours) vs allocated Interventions (principal `technicien` OR `equipe` member, de-duped, windowed) with a `sur_reservation` flag + `charge_pct`; endpoint `interventions/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. Distinct from gestion_projet's PROJ18 and STAGES.py. No model. **FG300** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` — double-booking detection: same technicien (principal/équipe) or same camionnette on ≥2 interventions the same day (de-duped, material assets excluded), at `interventions/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **FG301** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) — proposes moving interventions off overloaded technicians to under-loaded ones **without creating an FG300 same-day conflict** (read-only proposal, mutates nothing), at `interventions/nivellement-charge/`. No model. **FG302** adds **IndisponibiliteRessource** (`models_indispo.py`) — a field-resource unavailability calendar: a `technicien` XOR `camionnette` absent over `[date_debut, date_fin]`, `type_indispo` congé/formation/arrêt/autre; ViewSet `indisponibilites-ressource/` + `selectors.ressource_indisponible` that FG299/FG300/FG301 can call to exclude unavailable resources. Migration installations/0019.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/installations/`): `chantiers/` ViewSet + `creer-depuis-devis`, `regime-suggestion`, `{id}/historique|noter|mise-en-service|annuler|reactiver`, `{id}/checklist|cocher-checklist`, `{id}/besoin-materiel|commander-besoin` (now reports a per-SKU `reserve`); `interventions/` (F3: `?statut=`/`?type_intervention=`/`?installation=` filters + `{id}/historique|noter`); `types-intervention/`; `checklist-etapes/` (filterable `?template=`); `checklist-templates/` (N74, named template CRUD, Défaut delete-protected). Frontend route `/interventions` (F4, CHANTIERS menu): list + statut kanban (drag-to-change-status, technicien reassign). **FG303** adds `selectors.planning_camionnettes(company, debut, fin)` — a per-van calendar grouping interventions (via `Intervention.camionnette`) with a daily load, zero-capacity on FG302 indisponibilités (over-reservation visible), at the `planning-camionnettes` read action (IsAnyRole). No model. **FG304** adds **SousTraitant** — a subcontractor registry (métier/contact/ICE/RIB + `actif` archive flag, default True content-type-independent), DISTINCT from material suppliers; company+created_by server-side. ViewSet `sous-traitants/` (métier/actif filters, search). Migration installations/0020. **FG305** adds **OrdreSousTraitance** — subcontractor work orders (FK→SousTraitant FG304 + same-app chantier, race-safe `OST-` ref, prestation/montant/échéance, cycle brouillon→émis→en_cours→réceptionné→clos with lifecycle actions). ViewSet `ordres-sous-traitance/`. Migration installations/0021. **SCA34** converts `OrdreSousTraitance` into the FIRST pilot of the `core.documents.DocumentMetier` kit — a mixed conversion (redeclares `company` byte-identical, gains `created_at`/`updated_at` additively alongside the historical `date_creation`/`date_modification`, widens `statut` 20→32 chars for the abstract field, same 5 choices/default); reference format (`OST-YYYYMM-NNNN`) and the 3 existing lifecycle actions are unchanged (non-regression tested). **SCA36** converts `DemandeAchat` (models_demande_achat.py) into the kit's THIRD pilot (`LigneDocumentMetier` without a totals mixin — a purchase requisition has no money total). **NTP2P40** adds `demandes-achat/<id>/importer-lignes-csv/` — bulk-add requisition lines from an uploaded CSV. **NTP2P41** adds `rfq/<id>/comparatif-pdf/` (print-friendly PDF of `selectors.rfq_comparatif`) and `rfq/<id>/export-xlsx/` (one tab per consulted supplier + a comparatif tab, `apps.records.xlsx.build_workbook`) alongside the FG311 RFQ multi-supplier comparatif. **NTMOB21** adds `views_meteo.py`/`weather.py` — `GET installations/meteo/` (site weather for planning). **NTP2P2** adds **RegleApprobationAchat** + **EtapeApprobationAchat** (`models_approbation_achat.py`, migration installations/0101) — a same-app sequential approval plan for `DemandeAchat`, opt-in per company: a rule matches by montant bracket + optional chantier/programme scope (most-specific wins) and can `autorise_depassement_budget` (NTP2P4); no matching rule = the historical direct approve/refuse behaviour, byte-identical. ViewSet `regles-approbation-achat/`; `demandes-achat/{id}/` gains `etapes-approbation/` (GET), `approuver-etape/`, `rejeter-etape/` — the final step flips the demande to `approuvee`, a rejection to `refusee`. `services.controler_budget_demande_achat` (called at `soumettre`) reaches `stock.selectors/services` (never `stock.models`) to check the NTP2P4 department budget before the workflow starts.

### outillage — durable field tools & kits (F1/F2)
Durable tooling (drills, ladders, meters…), tracked **strictly separate from the consumable stock catalogue** — never sellable, never consumed, never on a client-facing document.
- **Outillage** (F1) — `company` FK; `nom`; `categorie` (free text); `asset_tag`; `numero_serie`; `emplacement` FK→stock.EmplacementStock (nullable; the tool's home location among the existing dépôt/camionnette); `statut` (DISPONIBLE/EN_INTERVENTION/EN_REPARATION/PERDU); `date_achat`; `note`. Optional photo via the generic `records.Attachment` (`outillage.outillage` whitelisted in `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS`).
- **KitOutillage / KitOutillageItem** (F2) — named, reusable tooling kit templates editable in Paramètres; each an ordered list of catalogue tools (`KitOutillageItem.outil` FK→Outillage, `ordre`, `unique_together (kit, outil)`); `type_intervention` (TypeIntervention key) pre-selects a kit; `actif` toggle. Three defaults (pose structure / raccordement / mise en service) seeded on first list (idempotent), fully editable.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/outillage/`): `outils/` (read any role, write responsable/admin; filter `?statut=`/`?emplacement=`, search nom/asset_tag/numero_serie/categorie), `kits/` (seed-on-list, write admin), `kit-items/` (write admin, item company follows its kit). Frontend route `/outillage` (CHANTIERS menu) + Paramètres → « Kits d'outillage » tab.

### sav — after-sales: equipment registry, tickets, maintenance contracts
Tracks installed equipment + warranty clocks and the SAV ticket lifecycle.
- **Equipement** — `company` FK; `produit` FK→stock.Produit; `installation` FK→installations.Installation; `numero_serie`; `date_pose`; `date_fin_garantie(_production)` (computed from `date_pose` + product warranty); `statut` (EN_SERVICE/REMPLACE/HORS_SERVICE); `remplace_par_ticket` FK→Ticket.
- **Ticket** (SAV) — `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `installation` FK (nullable); `equipement` FK (nullable); `type` (CORRECTIF/PREVENTIF); `statut` (NOUVEAU/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/RESOLU/CLOTURE); `priorite`; `sous_garantie` (OUI/NON/A_DETERMINER, computed from equipment warranty if linked); `cout` (internal, never client-facing); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`.
- **TicketActivity** — ticket chatter. **ContratMaintenance** — preventive contracts (`periodicite`, `date_debut`, `derniere_visite`, `actif`, `duree_mois`, `date_renouvellement`).
- **PieceConsommee** (N46) — parts consumed on a SAV ticket: `company` FK; `ticket` FK→Ticket; `produit` FK→stock.Produit; `quantite`; `stock_decremente` (guards double stock moves). Shown on the intervention report by designation/marque/quantité only — never buy price or margin; recording it can decrement stock via `MouvementStock`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sav/`): `equipements/`, `tickets/` (+ `{id}/historique|noter|annuler|reactiver|rapport-pdf`), `contrats-maintenance/`. **NTMOB23** adds `equipements/<id>/partage-qr/` — a QR-shareable equipment record for field techs. **FG280** adds **AlarmeOnduleur** — inverter alarms/faults DISTINCT from the SAV ticket (code/gravité info-warning-critique/équipement, statut active/acquittee/resolue/escaladee), with `acquitter` (server-side user+date, idempotent) and `escalader` (links or opens a SAV ticket) actions; ViewSet `alarmes-onduleur/`. Migration sav/0011.
- **ARC4** sweeps all 15 sav ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`. **ARC37** makes `sav` an emitter on the `core.events` bus — `services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` at the guarded transition site (never a second state machine). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/sav/platform.py`.

### portail — client self-service portal (Groupe FG228-233, new app)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
The Odoo Portal equivalent: a client logs in to see their own devis/factures/chantiers/tickets. Originally built inside `compta`; **ODX12** moved the models here with `db_table = 'compta_<model>'` (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, state-only — no data moved) + a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py`. Links to `crm.Client`/`crm.Lead`/devis/factures/chantiers/tickets are STRING-FK (never a `models` import of ventes/crm/sav/installations). **WIR95** upgraded these from opaque `PositiveIntegerField` ids to REAL `ForeignKey`s string-referencing the target model (`'ventes.Devis'`, `'facturation.Facture'`, `'crm.Client'`, `'crm.Lead'`, `'installations.Installation'`, `'sav.Ticket'`) with `on_delete=SET_NULL, db_constraint=False` — same pattern as `pos.CommandeRetrait.devis`: no DB-level FK constraint (the 5 domains stay decoupled), but a deletion via the ORM now nulls the reference instead of leaving a silent orphan id; the `_id` attname stays usable everywhere the old field was read. Auth surface (portal tokens/accounts) preserved byte-identical — no access widened by the split.
- **Models** — `ComptePortailClient` (tokenized client access account, reuses the client's email — no 2nd identity copy per DC32), `AcceptationDevisPortail` (quote e-acceptance from the portal, `devis` FK), `PaiementFacturePortail` (self-service invoice payment record, `facture` FK), `DocumentClientPortail` (client-visible document refs, `client`/`lead` FK — **WIR94**: the upload is now ALSO deposited as a real `ged.Document` via `ged.services.deposit_document` (lazy cross-app call, never a `ged.models` import) and referenced by `document_ged`; the historical `fichier` FileField is kept for backward compat), `JalonChantierPortail` (site-progress milestones surfaced to the client), `DemandeTicketPortail` (SAV ticket requests raised from the portal).
- Endpoints (new prefix; the SAME ViewSets are also still served under `/api/django/compta/…` for backward compatibility, incl. the public tokenized `portail/<token>/…` views which stay on compta): `comptes-portail/`, `acceptations-devis-portail/`, `paiements-facture-portail/`, `documents-client-portail/`, `jalons-chantier-portail/`, `demandes-ticket-portail/`.

### reporting — dashboards, KPIs, insights, audit log  *(no models)*
Read-only aggregation across crm/ventes/installations/sav/stock, role-filtered.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/reporting/`): `dashboard/`, `search/`, `notifications/`, `calendar/` and `calendar/reschedule/` (agenda events + drag-reschedule), `pipeline/` (funnel value by STAGES, weighted forecast), `reports/sales|stock|service/` (+`?export=xlsx`), `insights/recurring-revenue|audit-log|job-costing|analytics|commissions/`, `archive/client/{id}/` and `archive/chantier/{id}/`. `job-costing` (margin via internal `prix_achat`) and `commissions` (sales commission per `CompanyProfile.commission_mode`) are admin-only.
- **RapportDefinition** (NTEXT10, `rapport_builder.py`, previously undocumented here) — a rejouable cross-tab report definition (source query + optional `pivot_spec`); endpoint `rapport-definitions/` (+ an execute action building rows via `PivotSpec`/`build_pivot`). This batch's **PACT146** fixes the execute response shape: without a `pivot_spec` the payload is now a LITERAL `{'rows': rows}` (the `pivot` key stays absent, not a dynamically-omitted one — `scripts/check_api_shapes.py` only sees literal dict keys) and gives it its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-builder`. **PACT144** gives the pre-existing `ai_governance` "rapport périodique" feature (see below) its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-periode`. **NTEXT11** adds `rapport-definitions/{id}/export/?format=csv|xlsx` — a file export of the replayed definition, reusing the existing subscription-export infra, buy-price/margin columns stripped.

### parametres — company profile, business settings, WhatsApp templates
- **CompanyProfile** (one per company) — identity + Moroccan legal IDs (`ice`, `identifiant_fiscal`, `rc`, `patente`, `cnss`, `rib`); branding (`logo_key`, `signature_key`, `couleur_principale`); `responsable_defaut_leads` FK (default lead owner); quote-gen knobs (`payment_terms` JSON, `quote_validity_days`, `tva_standard/panneaux`, ROI constants `onee_tarif_kwh`/`productible_kwh_kwc`/`rendement_global`, `remise_max_pct`, `discount_approval_threshold`, `agricole_pump_hours`); `default_installer` FK (default technician for new chantiers, N66; NULL = creator is responsable); sales commission (`commission_mode` off/pct_devis/par_kwc + `commission_valeur`, sensitive/admin-only, N99); referral toggle (`referral_enabled` bool + `referral_reward`, N98); silent DGI export master switch (`dgi_export_actif` bool, **default OFF**, N105 — arms the ventes `dgi/` capability, invisible while off); `doc_prefixes`/`doc_numbering` JSON. **SCA27** adds `site_web` (migration 0055) — the tenant's own site URL, consumed by the quote-engine residential PDF footer/legal band instead of a hardcoded founder fallback (byte-identical repli when empty).
- **TauxTVA / ConditionPaiement / UniteMesure** (ARC23/24/27, `models_taxes.py`/`models_payment_terms.py`/`models_units.py`) — per-company referentials, each a `core.TenantModel` subclass (redeclares `company` for its own `related_name`, ARC1 motif): `TauxTVA` (code/libelle/taux, Moroccan rates 20/14/10/7/0, one `defaut=True`) feeds the default rate at devis/facture creation without rewriting an already-frozen document rate; `CompanyProfile.tva_standard` (existing knob, editable in Paramètres) takes PRECEDENCE over the referential's seeded default. `ConditionPaiement` (libellé/délai jours/fin de mois/escompte %) sources the default `Facture.conditions_paiement` label and mirrors `stock.Fournisseur`'s existing numeric fields. `UniteMesure` (code/libellé) mirrors `stock.Produit.unite_stock` (which stays master). All three purely additive/declarative — absent referential falls back to prior hardcoded behaviour; seeded at signup, backfillable via `backfill_conditions_paiement`/`backfill_unites_mesure` management commands.
- **MessageTemplate** — WhatsApp templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance), `corps_fr` + `corps_darija`.
- **EmailTemplate** (FG17, in `models_email.py`) — editable e-mail templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance/notification): `sujet` + `corps` with the same placeholder whitelist as WhatsApp (`{civilite}{nom}{reference}{lien}{n}`), `unique_together company+cle`. Helpers `EmailTemplate.get_template`/`render` (tolerant) for the future automation-email rewire (intentionally NOT wired yet). Endpoints `email-templates/` (CRUD) + `email-templates/effective/` (defaults⊕overrides) + `email-templates/bulk/` (upsert), writes audited.
- **SettingsAuditLog** — who changed which setting field.
- **StatutConfig** (N58, in `models_statuses.py`) — per-company display overlay for chantier/SAV/bon-de-commande statuses: `domaine` + canonical `cle` + `libelle` + `ordre` + `actif` (`unique_together company+domaine+cle`). Display-only — canonical keys & state machines stay in their source models; defaults read live from `Installation.STATUT_ORDER`/`Ticket.STATUT_ORDER`/`BonCommande.Statut` (`statuses_defaults.py`), so output is byte-identical until edited.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/parametres/`): `GET /`, `PUT/PATCH /update/`, `POST /upload-logo|upload-signature/`, `DELETE /delete-logo|delete-signature/`, `GET+PUT/PATCH /messages/`, `GET /audit/`; `statuts/` ViewSet (N58) + `statuts/effective/?domaine=` (full ordered effective list) + `statuts/bulk/` (upsert a domaine). Reads `GET /` and `GET /messages/` are open to any role; every write/audit endpoint (incl. `statuts/` writes) is the Administrateur + Responsable tier (`IsAdminOrResponsableTier`), limited tier blocked.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` — read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record). **NTEXT39** adds `gabarits-document/<code>/apercu/` — renders the same template with SAMPLE data (no real record touched), streamed as `<code>-apercu.pdf`.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` — read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record).
- **ParametresPos / BoutiquePos** (NTRET8, `models_pos.py`) — Paramètres → Point de vente section (`PointDeVenteSection.jsx`, embedded in `ParametresEntreprise`, no dedicated route). `ParametresPos` (one per company) carries `taux_horaire_comptoir` (counter labour rate, distinct from `CompanyProfile.taux_horaire_sav`); the line-discount threshold and printer/TPE config are REUSED as-is from `CompanyProfile.discount_approval_threshold` and `pos.ConfigMaterielPOS` — never duplicated here. `BoutiquePos` marks an EXISTING `stock.EmplacementStock` (string-FK) as a physical point of sale, with its own receipt address/hours + `surface_m2` (NTRET16, feeds the POS retail dashboard's sales-per-m² KPI). Endpoints: `GET /parametres/pos/` + `PUT /parametres/pos/update/`, `pos-boutiques/` ViewSet.
- **Licence tier (NTADM7/8, 2026-08-05)** — `CompanyProfile.plan` (nullable FK→`adminops.PlanLicence`, NULL = full access) + `nb_sieges_max` (nullable = unlimited); `parametres/feature_flags.py::has_feature(company, module_key)` (never restrictive by default — foundation only, nav masking stays on FG391/ODX); `services_licence.py` records plan changes in `SettingsAuditLog` + fires the `plan.changed` webhook; seat counting = `authentication.services.sieges_utilises`.

### scm — supply chain planning (Groupe NTSCM, new app)  *( `/api/django/scm/` )*
Demand forecasting, ABC classification, stock policies, a consolidated reappro dashboard, and a monthly S&OP cycle — a PLANNING layer above `apps.stock`'s existing execution (basic reappro/RFQ/DemandeAchat/transfers/supplier scorecard); reads `apps.stock` only via its `selectors.py`/`services.py`, never a model import.
- **PrevisionDemande** — per produit/segment/period (`YYYY-MM`) demand forecast; `methode` (moyenne_mobile/tendance/saisonnier/manuel). **EvenementDemande** — a signed % demand impact (promo/chantier majeur/rupture fournisseur/saisonnalité) over a date window, applied by `services.generer_previsions`.
- **ClassificationABC** — Pareto A/B/C ranking, recalculated server-side only (`selectors.classifier_abc`) and persisted HERE (not on `stock.Produit`, cross-app boundary) as a OneToOne. **PolitiqueStock** — per-produit min/max/ROP/safety-stock policy; `service_level_pct` seeded from the ABC class (A=95/B=90/C=85) but stays editable; ROP/safety-stock derived fields written ONLY by `services.recalculer_politiques_stock`.
- **CyclePlanificationSOP** — monthly S&OP cycle, a SEQUENTIAL state machine (brouillon→revue_demande→revue_offre→revue_finance→réunion_réconciliation→approuvé→clos) advanced only via `services.avancer_statut_cycle` (no step-skipping) or reopened via `services.reouvrir_cycle` (admin-only, audited). **LigneDemandeSOP** — a FROZEN snapshot of consensus demand per produit, taken automatically on brouillon→revue_demande. **LigneOffreSOP** — mirrored supply snapshot (stock disponible + capacité appro fournisseur) vs the frozen demand, surfacing the offre−demande gap.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/scm/`): `previsions-demande/` (+ `generer`), `evenements-demande/`, `classification-abc/` (+ `recalculer`), `politiques-stock/` (+ `recalculer`), `cycles-sop/` (+ `{id}/avancer-statut|reouvrir|historique|lignes-demande|ajuster-demande|calculer-offre|ecarts|impact-financier`), `tableau-bord-reappro/` (+ `creer-bcf`, groups lines by supplier into draft `stock.BonCommandeFournisseur`s via `stock.services`). Responsable/admin only. Frontend: `/scm/reappro` + `/scm/sop` (admin-only) — see §5.

### mrp — Production / MRP II (Groupe NTMFG, new app)  *( `/api/django/mrp/` )*
Generic manufacturing engine — work centers, routings, capacitated manufacturing orders, MRP net-requirements, finite-capacity scheduling, an MES shop-floor terminal, and standard/actual costing. Distinct and complementary to `installations`' existing lightweight kitting (`Kit`/`KitComposant`/`OrdreAssemblage`) — never rebuilt here; reads `installations`/`stock` only via `selectors.py`/`services.py` or string-FK.
- **PosteDeCharge** — work center (machine/ligne/manuel/sous-traité), daily capacity + an INTERNAL hourly cost (never client-facing, like `Produit.prix_achat`); optional `sous_traitant` FK→`stock.Fournisseur`.
- **Gamme** + **OperationGamme** — a reusable routing (ordered poste+temps standard) for one produit, versioned; optional `kit_source` FK→`stock.KitProduit` read-only for backflush at OF closure when no `kit_ordre_assemblage` link exists.
- **OrdreFabrication** (OF) — capacitated manufacturing order; EXTENDS (never duplicates) `installations.OrdreAssemblage` via an optional `kit_ordre_assemblage` link (when present, that model owns the stock movement). **OperationOF** + **PauseOperationOF** — instantiated-from-gamme operations with MES start/pause/resume/finish timestamps + scrap tracking. **ReservationOF** — internal component reservation bookkeeping (does NOT affect the cross-app `stock.services.available_quantity`, which stays driven by `installations` reservations only).
- **CoutStandard** — versioned, immutable-once-created standard cost (matière/main-d'œuvre/indirect %) per produit — strictly internal, never in a client document.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/mrp/`): `postes-charge/` (+ `{id}/oee`), `gammes/`, `operations-gamme/`, `ordres-fabrication/` (+ `{id}/confirmer|cloturer|annuler|dispo-composants`), `operations-of/` (+ `{id}/replanifier|demarrer|pauser|reprendre|terminer`), `couts-standard/` (+ `figer`), `mrp-run/`, `charge-postes/`, `analyse-couts/`, `oee-postes/`. Frontend: `/mrp/ordres-fabrication|gantt|terminal|oee|analyse-couts` — see §5.

### transport — transport orders (Groupe SUPPLY/NTLOG1-20, new app)  *( `/api/django/transport/` )*
Enlèvement/livraison/inter-site/import/export transport orders, from planning through delivery, with freight cost tracking and carrier disputes. Cross-app links (`ventes`/`stock`/`installations`/`flotte`) are ALL opaque integer string-FKs (`<app>_<model>_id`), resolved at read time via the target app's selectors or a function-local `apps.get_model` — never a static model import. History/attachments reuse the generic `records.Activity`/`records.Attachment` — no new `*Activity`/`FileField`.
- **OrdreTransport** — `numero` (race-safe `core.numbering`); `type_flux` (enlèvement_livraison/inter_site/import/export); its OWN `statut` machine (brouillon/planifié/en_cours/livré/annulé, independent of STAGES.py); `mode_transport` (flotte_propre/affrètement) + `mode_acheminement_physique` (route/mer/air, feeds the CO2 estimate). **LigneOrdreTransport** — merchandise (produit opaque ref, poids/volume/valeur déclarée). **EtapeTransport** — enlèvement→transit→livraison steps.
- **CoutFretReel** — actual freight cost lines (transport/assurance/manutention/dédouanement), the read point a future landed-cost extension (FG316) can consume. **LitigeTransport** — an independent carrier-dispute state machine (ouvert/en_traitement/résolu/rejeté), modeled on `litiges.Reclamation` without importing it; transitions log to the parent order's chatter. **ReserveReception** — a reception-time reserve (photos via `records.Attachment`) that automatically opens a `LitigeTransport`.
- **FacteurEmissionCO2** — per-company, per-mode (route/mer/air) kg-CO2-per-tonne-km factor, editable in Paramètres.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/transport/`): `ordres-transport/` (+ `{id}/etapes|comparer-transporteurs|co2|livrer`), `lignes-transport/`, `etapes-transport/`, `couts-fret/`, `litiges-transport/` (+ `{id}/prendre-en-charge|resoudre|rejeter|reclamer-transporteur`), `reserves-reception/`, `facteurs-emission-co2/`. Frontend: `/transport/ordres` — see §5.

### douane — customs & import/export (Groupe NTLOG, new app)  *( `/api/django/douane/` )*
Currently EXPORT-only (NTLOG14): `DossierExport`/`PieceDossierExport`/`ParametresDouane`. **The import side does not exist here** — NTLOG10's original plan conflicts with the pre-existing `installations.DossierImport` (FG315, owned by the PLAN_SERVICE domain); NTLOG10 stays `[BLOCKED]` pending a reconciliation that must edit `apps/installations` (out of this lane's write scope), and NTLOG11/12/13/21/22/26/30 all depend on it and are BLOCKED with it. **Trap for a future session: `douane.DossierImport` does NOT exist — do not assume it.**
