- **DossierExport** — `numero` (race-safe); `incoterm` (EXW/FOB/CFR/CIF/DAP/DDP); optional `devis`/`facture` FK (read links into `ventes`/`facturation`); ports embarquement/débarquement, `pays_destinataire`, `devise`, `valeur_marchandise_devise`; its OWN `statut` (à_préparer/DUM_déposée/en_dédouanement/levé/clôturé), independent of any import statut. **PieceDossierExport** — required/deposited document slots (facture_export/packing_list/certificat_origine/DUM_export/EUR.1), the file itself in `records.Attachment`.
- **ParametresDouane** — one per company (`for_company` singleton pattern), `regime_douanier_par_defaut`, `alerte_expiration_jours` (J-30/15/7 default), `mention_estimation_droits` (label reserved for NTLOG13/30, both BLOCKED — nothing consumes it yet).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/douane/`): `dossiers-export/` (+ `export`), `dossiers-export-pieces/`, `parametres-douane/`. Frontend: `/parametres/douane` (Paramètres tab, responsable/admin) — see §5.

### ecommerce_connect — Shopify/WooCommerce connectors (Groupe NTRET18/19, new app)  *( `/api/django/ecommerce-connect/` )*
**[GATED: no API key/secret in .env]** — every sync degrades to a total no-op (zero network calls); this module stores NO secret in the database, only non-sensitive metadata. `produit_id`/`facture_id` reference `stock.Produit`/`ventes.Facture` by opaque integer, never a cross-app FK. No frontend screen this batch.
- **ConnexionEcommerce** — one per (company, plateforme shopify/woocommerce); `boutique_url`, `actif` toggle (does NOT bypass the missing-key no-op), last catalogue/orders sync timestamps.
- **ProduitSync** — ERP↔external product mapping; `vendable_en_ligne` opt-in lives HERE (not a new field on `stock.Produit`); sync statut (ok/erreur/en_attente).
- **CommandeSync** — a PAID external order processed into the ERP; idempotency key = (`connexion`, `external_order_id`) so an at-least-once webhook replay never creates a duplicate invoice.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/ecommerce-connect/`): `connexions/` (+ `{id}/sync-catalogue`), `produits-sync/`, `commandes/`; public HMAC-signed webhooks `shopify/webhook/commande/` and `woocommerce/webhook/commande/` (signature = authentication, no login).

### promotions — cart promotion engine (Groupe NTRET12/13/15, new app)  *( `/api/django/promotions/` )*
Configurable cart promotion rules, unique-code coupons, and gift cards — consumed by `apps.pos` at checkout (`services.evaluer_panier`, the ONLY entry point `pos.services` calls). The calculation itself lives in a pure, ORM-free `engine.py`; `services.py` is the ORM↔engine bridge. `categorie`/`produit` reference `apps.stock` by string-FK only. No frontend screen of its own — consumed inside the `pos` coquille (§5).
- **ReglexPromotion** — a configurable rule: `type_regle` (remise_%_produit/remise_montant_panier/n_pour_m/plage_horaire "happy hour"), `priorite` + `cumulable` (non-cumulable rules: only the most advantageous applicable one is kept), optional targeting (`categorie`/`produit`/`montant_min_panier`) and validity window/days/hours. The only model with a CRUD ViewSet — read open to any role (the till needs to know active rules), write responsable/admin.
- **CouponUnique** + **CouponUtilisation** (NTRET13) — a single-code coupon tied to ONE `ReglexPromotion`, `mode_limite` (unique_par_client/global), consumed via `services.consommer_coupon` (server-side, journaled — no standalone CRUD, only through `pos`'s `coupon` action). Distinct from `compta.CodePromotion` (generic marketing campaign code, no usage tracking).
- **CarteCadeau** (NTRET15) — a gift card, sold at the till (no stock line — not an inventoried product), usable as a partial/total payment method across multiple visits until exhausted; debited via `services.debiter_carte_cadeau` (`select_for_update`, never negative).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/promotions/`): `regles/` (+ `simuler`, a dry-run cart-promotion preview for the till, no sale created). Coupons/gift cards have no standalone CRUD — consumed through `pos`'s `{id}/coupon`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau` actions.

### roles — RBAC  *( `/api/django/roles/` )*
- **Role** — `company` FK; `nom` (unique per company); `permissions` JSON (validated against canonical `ALL_PERMISSIONS`); `est_systeme` bool (system roles undeletable). Linked from `CustomUser.role`.
- 2026-06-18 (Feature D): `ALL_PERMISSIONS` expanded to a module×action grid + governance codes (`*_export`, `crm/ventes/sav_reassign`, `technicien_assign`, `prix_achat_voir`, `journal_activite_voir`, scope markers `records_scope_equipe`/`records_scope_sous_arbre`). **NTCPQ36** adds 4 CPQ permission codes: `cpq_regles_gerer`, `cpq_prix_contractuels_gerer`, `cpq_marge_voir` (ELEVATED tier, same palier as `marge_voir`/`prix_achat_voir`), `cpq_approbation_approuver`. `CANONICAL_SYSTEM_ROLES` seeds **seven** roles per company — Directeur, Administrateur (=Admin), Commercial responsable, Commercial, Technicien responsable, Technicien, Viewer — plus the legacy Responsable/Utilisateur kept for existing accounts. Seeder: `init_roles` (also maps owners→Directeur, custom commercial→Commercial; N103: self-heals a drifted same-named system role to `est_systeme=True`). `role_tiers.py` now derives the tier from the authoritative permission signal first (`roles_gerer`→admin, `users_voir`→responsable) with the name mapping as fallback — so a Directeur/Administrateur whose seeded row drifted to `est_systeme=False` still resolves to the admin tier and keeps access to `/users/` and `/roles/` (N103 regression fix), without widening Commercial/Technicien/Viewer.
- Endpoints: Role ViewSet (CRUD, open to the Administrateur + Responsable tier via `IsAdminOrResponsableTier` — limited tier blocked; delete blocked if system or in-use) + `permissions-disponibles/`.

### identity — security & access foundation (Groupe NTSEC, new app)  *( `/api/django/identity/` )*
Company-scoped security controls, all INERT by default (off-by-default `mode`/toggle, never blocks a request until explicitly configured).
- **Models** — `NetworkPolicy` + `IpAllowRule` (NTSEC11: per-company IP/CIDR allowlist, `mode` off/monitor/enforce, enforced by `middleware.NetworkPolicyMiddleware`), `TrustedDevice` (NTSEC14: "remember this device" — skips MFA on a non-expired/non-revoked trusted device only if the company opts in), `IdentityProvider` (NTSEC1: per-company SSO SAML/OIDC federation metadata), `ScimToken` + `ScimGroupMapping` (NTSEC5/6: SCIM 2.0 provisioning bearer token, hashed like `publicapi.ApiKey`; SCIM-group→`roles.Role` mapping via string-id, no `roles.models` import), `BreakGlassGrant` (NTSEC22: time-boxed audited emergency Administrateur elevation that bypasses `enforce_sso`, gated `apps.identity.selectors.is_break_glass_active`).
- Endpoints: `network-policies/`, `ip-allow-rules/`, `trusted-devices/`, `providers/`; SCIM 2.0 `scim/v2/<company_slug>/Users|Groups` (token-authenticated, machine-to-machine); `service-accounts/` (NTSEC24, model lives in `publicapi`, exposed here); `login-banner/` (public); `break-glass/` and `posture/` (NTSEC27 consolidated security posture) — both Directeur-only.

### accessreview — access governance (NTSEC19, new app)  *( `/api/django/accessreview/` )*
- **Models** — `AccessReviewCampaign` (periodic access-review campaign: périmètre all/role/module, dates, statut ouverte/…), `AccessReviewItem` (one row per in-scope user, attested or revoked by a manager — revocation removes the role via `apps.roles.services`, never a direct `roles.models` import), `SodRule` (separation-of-duties: a conflicting permission pair, `severite` info/warning/critique — critique can BLOCK a role assignment that would create the cumul).
- Endpoints: `campaigns/` (+ `{id}/attester/`), `sod-rules/` (+ `violations/`, `seed-standard/`). All `company`-scoped (`TenantModel`).
- **WIR136** — mounting dedup: `identity`/`accessreview` were each mounted TWICE in `erp_agentique/urls.py` — `identity` once inside the shared `_APP_URLS` list (→ both `api/django/` and `api/v1/`) and AGAIN standalone at `api/django/identity/` (harmless duplicate); `accessreview` was mounted ONLY standalone at `api/django/`, so it never got an `api/v1/accessreview/` mirror like every other app. Both are now solely in `_APP_URLS` — same `api/django/…` paths as before, `accessreview` gains its missing `api/v1/` mirror, `frais` (new this run) joins the same list.

### audit — activity log (audit trail)  *( `/api/django/audit/` )*
- **AuditLog** — company-scoped (server-forced, nullable for failed login); `user` FK (null=system) + `actor_username` snapshot; `action` (create/update/delete/status/login/logout/login_failed/pdf/email/whatsapp/export/accept/refuse); `content_type` + `object_id` + `object_repr` (link-back snapshot); `detail`; `timestamp` (UTC, bucketed in Africa/Casablanca at read time).
- Capture: `apps/audit/signals.py` (post_save/post_delete + status-change via pre_save cache) on the main business models, gated by `apps/audit/middleware.py` (records only inside a request → no seed/migration noise); login/logout in `authentication/views.py`, failed login via `user_login_failed`; key actions (PDF/export/WhatsApp) via explicit `recorder.record` calls. Best-effort — never blocks the request.
- Endpoints (gated on `journal_activite_voir`, Directeur-only by default): `stats/` (hourly buckets for a day, per-day for week/month, Casablanca, filterable), `entries/` (paginated filterable list, newest first), `meta/` (filter-bar data).

### records — generic activities + attachments  *( `/api/django/records/` )*
ContentType-based, attachable to Lead/Client/Installation/Ticket.
- **ActivityType** — configurable types (Appel/Email/Relance…), `delai_defaut_jours`.
- **Activity** — generic FK target; `activity_type` FK; `due_date`; `assigned_to` FK; `done` bool + `done_at/by`; `auto_relance` bool (auto-synced from `Lead.relance_date`).
- **Attachment** — generic FK target; `file_key` (MinIO); `phase` (avant/pendant/après for field photos).
- Endpoints: `activity-types/`, `activities/` (+ `mine/`, `{id}/done/`), `attachments/` (+ `{id}/download`, `attachments-count/`).
- **ARC30** switches `ALLOWED_TARGETS` from a literal set to a lazy union — `core.platform`'s `record_targets` surface, computed on first use, never at import — with the 19 historical targets migrated into 8 new `platform.py` manifests (outillage/rh/qhse/kb/ged/flotte/gestion_projet/ao) plus crm/contrats/sav/stock/ventes/installations from ARC29; a drop-in replacement (in/iteration/len identical, non-regression tested). **ARC8/26/SCA4/37** guard logic (no new bespoke `*Activity` chatter, no new `FileField` outside `records.Attachment`/`ged.Document`, no hand-rolled `company` FK model, no hand-rolled "document métier" outside the SCA30 kit) lives in `apps/records/platform_guards.py` — pure text/regex scanners against frozen baselines in `apps/records/platform_baselines/`, consumed by both `scripts/check_platform.py` (CI) and the Django test runner.

### customfields — admin-defined custom fields  *( `/api/django/custom-fields/` )*
- **CustomFieldDef** — `module` (LEAD/CLIENT/PRODUIT), `code` (slug), `type` (TEXT/NUMBER/DATE/CHOICE/BOOLEAN), `options` JSON, `obligatoire/visible_liste/actif`. Values live in each target model's `custom_data` JSON (no schema migration).
- **ARC14** adds a data-driven registry (`app_label`/`model` pairs, not a hardcoded module enum) with 2 pilots — `contrats.Contrat`, `flotte.Vehicule` (each own a `custom_data` JSON field, migrations contrats/0038 + flotte/0057). **ARC31** switches the registry's central loader to `register_from_platform_manifests()` (`CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`), reading the `customfield_models` surface off `core.platform` manifests instead of explicit `register()` calls in each app's `ready()` — the 8 native + 2 pilot models are unchanged, non-regression tested.
- **CustomObjectDef** + **CustomRecord** (XPLT16, previously undocumented here) — no-code custom business objects: an admin defines an object (`code`/`libelle`/`icone`) whose fields are ordinary `CustomFieldDef` rows under `module='custom:<code>'` (full reuse, no second field-definition engine); each row of data lives in `CustomRecord.data`. NTEXT2/NTEXT3 auto-generate the list/form views from the field schema. Endpoints `objects/`, `custom-objects/<code>/records/(<id>/)`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-liste/`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-formulaire/`. **PACT140** gives it its first screens: admin `/parametres/objets-personnalises` (define objects/fields, admin-only) + one generic `/objets/:code` records screen serving every defined object.
- Endpoints: `definitions/` ViewSet.
- **NTEXT24** adds `blueprint.py` — export/import of the platform CONFIG (custom objects/fields), idempotent by natural key (re-import never duplicates). **NTEXT34** adds `objets-catalogue/` (list installable custom-object templates) + `objets-catalogue/installer/<code>/` (install one into the tenant). **NTEXT38** adds `verrouille` bool (migration 0008) on `CustomFieldDef`/`CustomObjectDef` (admin lock against accidental edit/delete) + `verrouiller`/`deverrouiller` actions on `definitions/<pk>/` and `objects/<pk>/`; `audit_plateforme.py` reports it.

### tiers — cross-app party directory (ARC17, foundation app)  *( `/api/django/tiers/` )*
The Odoo `res.partner` equivalent: one identity record instead of the same name/coordinates/legal-IDs being re-entered in 5+ domain models. A FOUNDATION app (import-linter `tiers-is-a-base-layer` — domain apps depend on it, never the reverse); this run only extends the pre-existing `Tiers` model + `TiersViewSet` (`ARC17`) with bridges and read-side reconciliation.
- **Tiers** — `company` FK; identity fields (`nom`/`prenom`/`raison_sociale`, `telephone`/`whatsapp`/`email`/`adresse`/`ville`/GPS, Moroccan IDs `ice`/`rc`/`identifiant_fiscal`/`cin`/`rib`); role flags `is_client`/`is_fournisseur`/`is_partenaire`/`is_soustraitant`; `type_tiers`.
- **ARC18** adds additive nullable `tiers` FKs (string-FK `'tiers.Tiers'`) mirroring identity ONE-WAY from `crm.Client` and `stock.Fournisseur` (migrations crm/0050, stock/0075) into a `Tiers` row, via `apps/{crm,stock}/tiers_bridge.py` + `attacher_ou_creer_tiers` (dedup by company-scoped email/ICE, else create — never duplicates; also persists the role flags on the create path, a real bug fix landed mid-run). **ARC19** extends the same bridge to `compta.Partenaire` (migration compta/0108) and `rh.DossierEmploye` (migration rh/0082). **ARC56** adds the bridge to `crm.Lead` (migration crm/0051) — pre-conversion identity, so a lead already has a `Tiers` before it becomes a client. In every case the historical model stays the IDENTITY MASTER; `tiers` is a mirror only.
- **ARC20** adds read-only cross-referencing: `selectors.find_by_ice`/`find_by_email` (company-scoped lookups) and `selectors.find_duplicates` — a same-ICE/same-email cluster report (e.g. one party registered as both a Fournisseur and a Partenaire) surfaced at `GET tiers/doublons/` (admin-only, no fusion/write).
- **ARC21** is a DECISION, flag-gated OFF: `TIERS_SOURCE_ECRITURE` would make `Tiers` the write-source of identity instead of a mirror — documented in `docs/decisions/ARC21-tiers-source-ecriture.md`, not flipped in this run.
- `management/backfill_tiers` — idempotent one-off command materializing `Tiers` bridges for existing rows across the four bridged models.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/tiers/`): `tiers/` ViewSet (CRUD, company-scoped, no dedicated fine-grained permission — authenticated + scoped suffices) + `tiers/doublons/` (ARC20 report, admin-only).

### documents — field-execution PDFs  *(no models, `/api/django/documents/`)*
- `GET chantiers/{pk}/pv-reception|bon-livraison|dossier-remise|attestation/` — generates post-delivery PDFs for an installation.

### publicapi — clés d'API publique + jobs bulk  *( `/api/public/` )*
- **BulkJob** (`TenantModel`) — job asynchrone export/import déclenché par clé API, traité hors requête (Celery), suivi par `GET jobs/<id>/`, reprenable (`cursor` mémorise le dernier offset traité, `POST jobs/<id>/relancer/` repart de là sans doublon ni saut). `api_key` en `SET_NULL` : révoquer une clé n'efface pas l'historique.
- `POST exports/` + `POST imports/` (create/upsert, journal d'erreurs par ligne), `GET jobs/` + `GET jobs/<id>/` (`Retry-After` tant que le job tourne), `GET exports/<entite>.csv?token=` (tirage CSV synchrone pour Google Sheets `IMPORTDATA()`), `GET /ventes/export-comptable/?layout=quickbooks` (IIF).
- **Gardes d'écrasement des imports (vague 3).** Les importateurs de prix fournisseur, limites de crédit, entités et compteurs d'usage sont passés en REMPLISSAGE-SEUL par défaut, avec aperçu champ par champ (valeur actuelle → valeur du fichier) et journalisation de la valeur PRÉCÉDENTE. Un BUG DE PERTE RÉELLE a été corrigé au passage : une cellule PRÉSENTE mais VIDE écrasait un `prix_achat` saisi à la main.

### trash — corbeille transverse 30 jours (NTUX7)  *( `/api/django/trash/` )*
- **ElementSupprime** (`TenantModel`) — journal générique des soft-suppressions (cible par contenttype, snapshot d'affichage, `expire_le` = suppression + 30 j calculé SERVEUR). Les apps émettent `core.events.record_soft_deleted` sans jamais importer la corbeille. Restauration par registre (`enregistrer_restaurateur('crm.lead', fn)`) avec repli générique. API en LECTURE seule + `POST {id}/restaurer/`. `tasks.py` + beat `trash.purger_corbeille_transverse` purge les éléments dont `expire_le` est dépassé.

### uxviews — vues sauvegardées, favoris, réglages UX  *( `/api/django/uxviews/` )*
- **FavoriUtilisateur** — épinglage personnel (cible générique, `ordre`), strictement privé : 404 pour un collègue, même Directeur. **UxParametres** — réglages UX par société (durées, partage d'équipe, rôles autorisés à définir un défaut). **NTUX28** ajoute des limites anti-abus (migration 0004, ex : nb max de favoris/vues par utilisateur) ; `tasks.py` + beat `uxviews.digest_favoris_obsoletes_hebdo` envoie un digest hebdomadaire des favoris obsolètes.

### offlinesync — file d'écriture hors-ligne multi-module (NTMOB1, new app)  *( `/api/django/offlinesync/` )*
- **OfflineOperation** (`TenantModel`) — journal d'idempotence de la synchro terrain : `module` (crm/ventes/stock/installations/sav), `op_type`, `payload`, `client_op_id` (UUID généré par le terminal, unique PAR SOCIÉTÉ), `statut` (en_attente/appliquee/rejetee/conflit — `conflit` réservé NTMOB2, jamais posé ici), `resultat`/`erreur`. Une op refusée reste en base avec son message (jamais perdue en silence, VX119) et redevient rejouable après correction.
- `registry.py`/`handlers.py` — registre `op_type → (module, handler)` ; un handler résout sa cible via le `selectors.py`/`services.py` de l'app visée (jamais ses `models`/`views`) et doit être écrit « last-write-wins ». Généralise `installations.FieldOp` (un seul module) sans le remplacer — le point de synchro terrain historique (`/installations/sync/`) reste en service, même contrat de réponse.
- Endpoints : `POST operations/batch/` (rejeu idempotent d'un lot, `IsResponsableOrAdmin`, déclaré AVANT le routeur) + `GET operations/[<id>/]` (journal lecture seule, filtrable `?statut=&module=`).

### ai_governance — assistants IA gouvernés  *( `/api/django/ai/` )*
- Rédaction assistée, CR d'intervention dicté, description produit, copilote de configuration, rapport périodique (`rapport-periode/`, `RapportPeriodeView`, NTAI36), surveillance de dérive (PSI). **Clé-gaté** sur les clés existantes ; sans clé → 503 explicite, aucun appel réseau. **`prix_achat` n'est JAMAIS lu** : le prompt produit est bâti sur une liste blanche de 4 champs (nom/marque/catégorie/garantie), pas sur une consigne au modèle. **PACT144** gives `rapport-periode/` its first screen (`/reporting/rapport-periode`, mounted in the `reporting` frontend module) — server metrics + an editable narrative the server rejects if it cites an absent figure.
- **DocumentAiJob** (`TenantModel`) — file de traitement IA, créée par un receiver `post_save` sur `ged.Document` (résolu par chaîne, jamais un import `ged.models`) ; traitée hors requête par la tâche Celery `ai_governance.traiter_document_ai_job`. **ExtractionCorrection** — correction humaine d'une extraction, alimente `taux-correction/`. Endpoints `documents-ai-jobs/` (lecture seule, + `{id}/corriger/`, `taux-correction/`) et `recherche-globale/` (NTAI25 — recherche sémantique cross-module via `core.ai.search`).

### conversation_ai — appels commerciaux enregistrés + transcription (Groupe NTAI, new app)  *( `/api/django/conversation_ai/` )*
- **AppelCommercial** (`TenantModel`) — `lead`/`client` FK chaîne (`'crm.Lead'`/`'crm.Client'`, tous deux optionnels), `fichier_key` (objet audio dans le stockage MinIO, préfixé société), `transcript`, `statut` (non_transcrit/en_cours/transcrit/erreur), `analyse_json` (objections/next_steps/produits/sentiment, NTAI22) + `sentiment`. À NE PAS CONFONDRE avec `voip.Appel` (journal d'appel VoIP, sans audio ni transcript).
- Tâche Celery `conversation_ai.transcrire_appel` (STT best-effort, key-gated : sans fournisseur configuré, l'appel reste `non_transcrit`, aucun appel réseau ni coût).
- Endpoints : `appels/` (upload) + `appels/{id}/analyser/` (NTAI22, analyse LLM du transcript) + `appels/{id}/creer-relances/` (confirmation explicite → crée les relances proposées par l'analyse).

### migration — reprise de données client (NTMIG)  *( `/api/django/migration/` )*
- **ProjetMigration / LotMigration / RapportReconciliation** (`TenantModel`) — un projet de reprise par client, découpé en lots (un lot = une entité : clients, produits, fournisseurs…). Chargement en **remplissage-seul FORCÉ** (`ecraser=False` codé en dur, aucun interrupteur exposé par l'API — une cellule vide ne remplace jamais une valeur saisie), via `apps/dataimport`. Connecteur Odoo (`charger-odoo`) et chargement par système externe partagent le même pipeline.
- `POST lots/<id>/{analyser,charger,charger-odoo,reconcilier,deroger,terminer}`, `POST projets/<id>/terminer`, `GET projets/<id>/rapport` (PV PDF de reprise). Écrans `/migration` (liste) et assistant.
- **Garde-fous d'intégrité :** `projet`/`entite` figés après création (un lot ne peut pas être rattaché au projet d'une AUTRE société) ; un projet clôturé et un lot déjà chargé sont indélébiles (le PV client ne peut pas être effacé) ; une dérogation ne survit pas à un rechargement ; l'`external_system` porte l'id de projet (un rollback ne peut pas supprimer les lignes d'un autre projet de la même société).
- `validation.py` + `stockage.py` (dépôt objet MinIO des fichiers source) : **NTMIG32** `POST lots/<id>/valider-source/` valide le fichier source AVANT chargement (colonnes/types attendus). **NTMIG33** `POST lots/<id>/reprendre/` reprend un lot interrompu à son offset (`offset_reprise`, migration 0003) sans rejouer les lignes déjà traitées. **NTMIG34** `GET projets/<id>/estimation/` — estimation d'effort + checklist de points d'attention avant de lancer une reprise. **NTMIG38** `POST projets/<id>/migrer-a-blanc/` — répétition à blanc (dry-run complet) sans écriture définitive. `tasks.py` + beat `migration.purger_fichiers_migration` purge les fichiers source expirés (migration 0002).
- **NTMIG-playbooks.** **PlaybookInstance** (`TenantModel`) — instantané FIGÉ des étapes d'un `kb.KbArticle` playbook au moment de l'instanciation (une édition ultérieure de l'article ne modifie pas une instance déjà en cours). **DeploiementPartenaire** — trace un déploiement réalisé par un partenaire certifié, alimente `crm.Partenaire.nb_deploiements_reussis` via `crm.services.poser_compteur_deploiements` (jamais un `.update()` direct depuis cette app). `certification.py` (scoring de certification partenaire), `playbooks.py`, `manage.py seed_playbooks` (idempotent). Endpoints `playbook-instances/` (+ `instancier/`, `{id}/cocher/`, `{id}/terminer/`), `deploiements-partenaire/`, `certification/<partenaire_id>/score/`.

### dataimport — CSV/XLSX import  *(`ImportJob`/`ImportJobRow`, `/api/django/imports/`)*
- `POST dry-run/` (preview + column mapping), `POST commit/`, `GET export/{entity}/`. Targets: leads, clients, products.
- **Garde d'écrasement (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** En mode `maj`/`upsert` l'import ÉCRASAIT les champs déjà remplis sans confirmation. Désormais : `ecraser` défaut **False** (remplissage seul — un champ vide se remplit, un champ rempli est PRÉSERVÉ) ; `dry-run/` renvoie `conflits` (par ligne : cible + `ecrasements[{champ, ancienne, nouvelle}]` + `remplissages`) donc l'utilisateur voit AVANT écriture ce qu'un fichier périmé remplacerait ; l'exécution journalise la valeur PRÉCÉDENTE de chaque champ modifié (`ImportJobRow.modifications`/`refuses`, + une ligne `AuditLog` par enregistrement via la primitive existante). Migration `dataimport/0004` additive.
- **ARC13** adds `parsing.py` `iter_rows(file_bytes, filename)` — a generic CSV/XLSX row reader (encoding detection utf-8/latin-1, `,`/`;` separator, normalized headers) that `services.parse_rows` now delegates to (zero behaviour change for the 6 historical FIELD_MAPS targets) and that any tabular-file reader outside `dataimport` can reuse instead of hand-rolling `csv.reader`/`openpyxl`; 3 pilot call sites migrated (rh/contrats views). **ARC32** makes `TARGETS` a lazy union of `FIELD_MAPS ∪ declared_import_specs()` — the `import_specs` surface declared by `core.platform` manifests (6 owning apps: crm already declared, stock/sav/flotte/contrats/rh extended), bridged via `export_registry.declared_import_specs()`; a set-literal non-regression test asserts the 8 historical targets plus any newly-declared target.

### contact — public contact form  *(no models, `/api/django/contact/`)*
- `POST /` — landing-page contact form; **parked by default** (returns 404 unless `CONTACT_FORM_ENABLED=1`).

### monitoring — production supervision (N50/N51/N52)  *( `/api/django/monitoring/` )*
- Models: `MonitoringConfig` (per installed-system provider + credentials, enabled), `ProductionReading` (manual/auto yield), `UnderperformanceFlag`, per-company settings (threshold % + auto-ticket toggle, default OFF).
- Swappable provider interface (registry + `NoOpProvider` default + `FusionSolarProvider` skeleton that no-ops without credentials; no new dependency).
- `configs/` (+ `providers/`, `{id}/sync-now/`), `readings/` (list + manual entry), `settings/`. Under-performance auto-creates an idempotent SAV ticket when enabled.

### notifications — unified notification engine (N75)  *( `/api/django/notifications/` )*
- Models: `Notification` (company + recipient-scoped), `NotificationPreference` (per user×event channel toggles in_app/whatsapp/email). Service `notify()` is best-effort, respects preferences, reuses existing channels (no-op when unconfigured).
- `notifications/` (+ `unread-count/`, `{id}/read/`, `read-all/`), `preferences/`. In-app bell in the header + `/parametres/notifications`.
- **ARC35** registers `EventType.CONTRAT_SIGNE` — consumes the `contrat_signe` seam on the `core.events` bus, notifying the contract's creator (fallback managers) when a contract becomes fully signed; `contrats/receivers.py` additionally logs an ARC8 chatter note + a GED deposit on the same transition (best-effort, isolated failures logged separately per hook). **ARC37** makes `sav` and `gestion_projet` bus EMITTERS (not just consumers): `sav.services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` (→ `EventType.SAV_TICKET_RESOLU`/`SAV_EQUIPEMENT_REMPLACE`), and a project statut change fires `projet_status_change` (→ `PROJET_STATUT_CHANGE`). **ARC38** repatriates `qhse`'s `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (double emission preserved for the existing local publicapi consumer, documented choice) via `qhse/receivers.py`. **ARC39** closes 3 previously-invisible notification gaps: `MONITORING_RAPPORT` (the periodic O&M report send, `monitoring/report.py`, stays a direct `EmailMessage` to the client but now ALSO notifies internal managers via `notifications.services`) and `PAIE_RIB_DIVERGENCE` (the ARC25 RIB-mismatch check already called `notify_many(...)` with this key — it was simply never a registered `EventType`, so it silently dropped; now persisted, zero caller behaviour change) + `PAIE_RUN_PRET` (a payroll run reaching `validee` now notifies payroll managers it is ready for the transfer order/closure).

### automation — no-code rules engine (N72/N73)  *( `/api/django/automation/` )*
- Models: `AutomationRule` (trigger + action config), `AutomationRun` (every run logged), `AutomationApproval` (owner-tier approval step). Fires on the app's own `post_save` signals, best-effort (never breaks the originating save); opt-in.
- `rules/` (+ `{id}/toggle/`), `runs/`, `approvals/` (+ `approve/`, `reject/`). Paramètres → « Automatisations ».
- **ARC34** adds a generic `RECORD_STATE_CHANGE` trigger type — a registry-driven `(model, field)` whitelist read from each app's `platform.py` `automation_state_fields` manifest entry (`record_state_change_targets()`), with French validation at rule-creation time (serializer) and conditions evaluated via `core/rules.py` (FG367, never a second evaluator). 2 pilots emit through their app's `services.py` (`contrats.changer_statut` wrapper, `sav.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` at the guarded transition site) — never a direct model-signal shortcut.
- **NTEXT6** adds `list_sources.py` — a CLOSED registry of list sources for a FOR_EACH loop step (never an arbitrary queryset). **NTEXT7** adds `AutomationScheduledStep` (a frozen context + resume rank + statut, `en_attente/traitee/echouee`) for a WAIT step, resumed by the `automation.process_due_automation_steps` beat task (every 5 min, `beat_tasks.py`). **NTEXT31** adds `AutomationRun.SIMULATION` statut + `rules/{id}/simuler/` — a dry-run (no side effect) evaluation of a rule.

### publicapi — public REST API + webhooks (N89)  *( `/api/public/` data, `/api/django/publicapi/` management )*
- Models: `ApiKey` (hashed, scoped), `Webhook`, `WebhookDelivery`. `Api-Key` auth + per-key DRF throttle; read-only company-scoped `leads/devis/factures/chantiers` (never buy prices); HMAC-SHA256-signed webhooks on lead.created / devis.accepted / chantier.completed / facture.paid (httpx, best-effort). Paramètres → « API & Webhooks ». NTADM41/42 (2026-08-05): webhook events `plan.changed` + `sieges.quota_atteint` (never client data) and `GET /api/public/v1/licence/statut/` (scope `read:licence`, exactly plan_code/modules_inclus/sieges_max/sieges_utilises).

### agent — agentic action catalogue (Group R, AG1)  *( `/api/django/agent/` )*
- No DB model — actions are declared in code via `apps/agent/registry.py` (`AgentAction`: key/label/description/inputs-schema/endpoint/method/required_permission/risk∈internal·outward·irreversible/confirm_summary). `GET actions/` returns the per-caller, company+permission-filtered catalogue (cross-tenant leakage tested). Domain apps register their actions in `ready()` (ventes/crm/stock/sav/installations `agent_actions.py`, AG4–AG9). Execution stays the JWT-relay pattern (Django re-checks permission+company); outward/irreversible actions go through the FastAPI propose→confirm protocol.

### chat — internal team messaging « Discuss » (Group S)  *( `/api/django/chat/` )*
- Models: `Conversation` (dm/channel), `ConversationMember` (role/last_read_at/is_muted), `Message` (text/voice/system/record kinds, soft-delete, pin, reply_to), `MessageAttachment` (image/file/voice + transcript fields), `MessageReaction`, `MessageMention`, + generic shared-record link. Company **and** membership scoped everywhere (non-member 403, cross-tenant 404; company forced server-side). Endpoints: conversations (list/create/archive/read/unread/search/mute/members/leave), messages (`list?conversation=`/create/edit/delete/upload/react/pin/unpin/attachments-download/share-record via selectors). Notifications reuse `notify()` (CHAT_MESSAGE/CHAT_MENTION, mute-aware). Voice memos transcribed by a Celery task → FastAPI faster-whisper (S10/S11), flag `CHAT_TRANSCRIPTION_ENABLED`; v1 real-time is polling (WebSocket upgrade S21 is gated on provisioned infra). **NTMOB30** adds `views_transcription.py` — `POST chat/transcrire/` (multipart `file`), a SYNCHRONOUS generic wrapper around the same S10/S11 Whisper call (never a second client) for dictating a field note straight onto a lead/ticket chatter, no attachment stored; disabled → `{enabled: False}`, frontend falls back to keyboard.

### compta — Moroccan accounting (CGNC): chart, journals, ledger, statements  *( `/api/django/compta/` )*
- Double-entry bookkeeping on the CGNC plan comptable: journaux, **EcritureComptable**/**LigneEcriture** (grand livre), balance/CPC/bilan statements, lettrage. All `company`-scoped.
- **ExerciceComptable** (fiscal year) + **PeriodeComptable** (lockable month/period via `date_verrouillee`) — `services.cloturer_periode`/`rouvrir_periode` lock/unlock. Once a period is locked, `EcritureComptable`/`LigneEcriture` `save()/delete()` raise `ValidationError` (immutability), and `services.verifier_facture_modifiable` is a value-only guard ventes can call (no cross-app model import). **OD manuelles** — `services.creer_ecriture_od` posts a balanced entry with no source document, refused when the period is locked. **À-nouveaux** — `cloturer_exercice` + `reporter_a_nouveaux` carry class 1–5 balance-sheet balances into the new exercise as one balanced opening entry (idempotent via `an_reporte`). Endpoints: `periodes/{id}/cloturer|rouvrir`, `exercices/ecriture-od`, `exercices/{id}/reporter-a-nouveaux`.
- **FG118 — Immobilisation** (fixed-asset register): `company` FK, `libelle`, `categorie` (vehicule/outillage/materiel/mobilier/informatique/autre), `cout` HT, `taux_tva`, `date_acquisition`, `actif`; read-only `montant_tva`/`cout_ttc` props. Company-scoped ViewSet `immobilisations/` (category filter + search).
- **FG119 — Amortissement**: **PlanAmortissement** (OneToOne→Immobilisation; `mode` lineaire/degressif, `duree_annees`, `base_amortissable`, frozen Moroccan CGI `coefficient_degressif`) + **DotationAmortissement** (per-year `montant`/`cumul`/`valeur_nette`, `posted`, FK `ecriture`). `services.generer_plan_amortissement` (idempotent; degressive switches to straight-line-of-residual) and `services.poster_dotation` (balanced écriture debit class-6 / credit class-28 — **respects the period lock**). Actions `immobilisations/{id}/plan-amortissement`, `dotations/{id}/poster`.
- **FG120 — Cession/rebut**: **CessionImmobilisation** (`type_cession` vente/rebut, `prix_cession`, computed `valeur_nette_comptable` = cost − cumulated FG119 amortization, signed `resultat_cession` plus/moins-value, `posted` + FK `ecriture`). `services.poster_cession` posts the balanced disposal écriture (reprise amortissements + sortie class-2 + résultat 6513/7513 + 3481 on sale) — **respects the period lock** and marks the asset inactive. Actions `immobilisations/{id}/ceder`, `cessions/{id}/poster`.
- **ODX15 (2026-07 — moved OUT to `apps.frais`).** FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 — `NoteFrais`, `RapportNoteFrais`, `PlafondNoteFrais`, `BaremeIndemnite`, `IndemniteChantier` — no longer live here: a state-only split (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `db_table` frozen on the historical `compta_*` names, no data moved) relocated the model CODE to `apps/frais` with a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py` for existing importers. Both `/api/django/frais/…` (canonical) and the legacy `/api/django/compta/notes-frais|rapports-notes-frais|plafonds-notes-frais|baremes-indemnite|indemnites-chantier/…` (unchanged) serve the same ViewSets. Full detail moved to §4 **frais**.
- **FG137 — DeclarationTVA** (préparation de la déclaration de TVA): `selectors.preparer_declaration_tva` aggregates from the GL over a period — TVA collectée (4455/44552, crédit−débit) − déductible (3455/34552, débit−crédit) → `tva_a_declarer` = max(0, collectée−déductible−crédit antérieur) with the excess as `credit_reportable`; carries `regime` (mensuel/trimestriel) + `methode` (débit/encaissement). `services.preparer_declaration_tva` freezes a `DeclarationTVA` snapshot (reference `TVA-` race-safe). ViewSet `declarations-tva/` (`preparer` action derives amounts from the GL — body can't impose them — + CSV `export`), Admin/Responsable. **FG138** adds `selectors.releve_deductions_tva` — the DGI line-by-line deductible-VAT annex (one row per pièce: date/réf/journal/tiers/base HT/TVA/taux, reconciles 1:1 with FG137's `tva_deductible`) at `etats/releve-deductions-tva/` (JSON or `?export=csv`, role-gated). No model — reuses the GL. **FG139** adds **RetenueSource** (Moroccan withholding tax / retenue à la source on fees: `taux` × `base` = `montant`, per pièce/tiers, ref `RAS-` race-safe) + `selectors.bordereau_versement_ras` (totals per prestataire + `total_a_verser`). ViewSet `retenues-source/` (`verser`/`bordereau`/`export` CSV via `?export=csv`), role-gated, montant server-side. Migration compta/0014. **FG140** adds an IS (corporate-tax) aid: `selectors.estimer_is` (CPC résultat ± réintégrations/déductions → résultat fiscal → IS dû = max(progressive barème, cotisation minimale 0.25%/3000 floor)) + `echeancier_acomptes` (4 × 25% at month-end 3/6/9/12) + `regularisation_is`, at `etats/aide-is/` (JSON / `?export=csv`, admin). No model — reuses the CPC. **FG141** adds `selectors.export_fec` — the DGI FEC (fichier des écritures comptables): the 18 standard columns, one ordered row per LigneEcriture (date→pièce→entry order), exercice-bounded, balance-verified, at `etats/export-fec/?exercice=` (JSON / `?export=fec` tab-delimited / `?export=csv`). No model. **FG142** adds `selectors.liasse_fiscale` — the trousse liasse fiscale: assembles bilan + CPC + balance + the FG138 TVA annexe into one package (**reuses the standalone selectors, no recompute**), at `etats/liasse-fiscale/?exercice=` (JSON / `?export=csv` multi-section). No model. **FG143** adds `selectors.declaration_honoraires(company, annee)` — the DGI annual état 9421 (fees paid to third parties), aggregated per-bénéficiaire from the FG139 RAS ledger (brut/retenue/net + IF/ICE + nb pièces), at `etats/declaration-honoraires/?annee=` (JSON / `?export=csv`, role-gated). No model. **FG144** adds **TimbreFiscal** — Moroccan droit de timbre (0.25% + statutory minimum) auto-computed on CASH-settled invoices (non-cash règlements exonérés → None); the origin payment is a string-id ref (no ventes import) and no GL entry is posted (snapshot, FG139 pattern). ViewSet `timbres-fiscaux/` (+ `verser`, `?export=csv`). Migration compta/0015. **FG145** adds **RetenueGarantie** (RG % withheld on a marché, race-safe ref, released at maturity) + **CautionBancaire** (provisoire/définitive/restitution bank guarantees with mainlevée); marché/facture by string-ref (no ventes import); `liberer`/`mainlevee` actions + maturity selectors. ViewSets `retenues-garantie/`, `cautions-bancaires/`. Migration compta/0016. **NTMAR (Maroc & Afrique)** extends compta: `services.export_simpl_tva` (SIMPL-TVA DGI-structured XML, action `declarations-tva/{id}/export-simpl/`) + `selectors.controles_predepot_tva` (pre-deposit alerts, `controles-predepot/`); **AcompteIS** (materialized 4 IS instalments via `services.materialiser_acomptes_is`, `services.export_simpl_is` SIMPL-IS XML at `etats-comptables/export-simpl-is|materialiser-acomptes-is/`, ViewSet `acomptes-is/`); RAS extensions — `RetenueSource.type_prestation` gains `prestation_etrangere` + `pays_beneficiaire`/`convention_appliquee` fields with **ConventionFiscale** (per-country reduced rate, ViewSet `conventions-fiscales/`, applied in `enregistrer_retenue_source`), loyers bordereau (`retenues-source/bordereau-loyers/`) + annual recap (`recapitulatif-annuel/`); **TimbreFiscal.mode_acquittement** (papier/électronique) + monthly synthesis (`timbres-fiscaux/synthese-mensuelle/`); caution `a-restituer/` selector. Migration compta/0121. IR SIMPL export (NTMAR13) is `[BLOCKED]` — needs a paie IR-by-period selector (foreign app).
- **ARC19** adds a nullable `Partenaire.tiers` FK (migration compta/0108, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC28** adds `apps/compta/platform.py` (`kpi_providers`: échéances d'effets 30j/dépassées, consumed by ARC40's federated KPI endpoint; `agent_actions_module` — read-only effets/échéances actions).
- **NTFIN — consolidation grand groupe & multi-référentiel/analytique multi-axes** (migrations compta/0114–0115, all `TenantModel` + `_ComptaBaseViewSet`). Consolidation: **CycleConsolidation** (dated group cycle, statut ouvert→collecte→eliminations→validé→publié + `verrouille` gate refusing aggregate mutation) with `EntiteConsolidation.cycle` FK; **LiasseRemontee** (per-entity balance snapshot, `services.collecter_balance_entite` idempotent via `balance_generale`, head-company collected too); `selectors.controles_collecte` (NTFIN3 blocking/warning anomalies); **MappingConsolidation** (local→group account, longest-prefix); `services.convertir_entite` (NTFIN5 closing/average-rate translation + CTA plug); **OperationInterco** + `apparier_intercos` (NTFIN6 reciprocal matching w/ cycle tolerance); **EcritureElimination** + `generer_eliminations_reciproques` (NTFIN7), `eliminer_marge_interne` (NTFIN8 unrealized stock margin + deferred tax), **EliminationTitres**/`eliminer_titres` (NTFIN9 goodwill), `calculer_interets_minoritaires` (NTFIN10); `selectors.bilan_consolide`/`cpc_consolide_v2` (NTFIN11, distinct from FG153) + `moniteur_consolidation` (NTFIN12). Endpoints under `cycles-consolidation/{id}/` (`ouvrir|verrouiller|collecter|controles-collecte|intercos|apparier|eliminations|generer-reciproques|interets-minoritaires|etats-consolides|moniteur`) + `liasses-remontee/`, `mappings-consolidation/`, `operations-interco/`, `marges-internes-stock/{id}/eliminer`, `eliminations-titres/{id}/eliminer`. Multi-référentiel: **ReferentielComptable** (CGNC/IFRS/GROUPE/FISCAL parallel books, one principal per company via `services.seed_referentiel_principal`) + `LigneEcriture.referentiel` (NULL = principal, `creer_ecriture(referentiel=)`) + `selectors.balance_par_referentiel`; **AjustementGaap**/`services.poster_ajustement_gaap` (GAAP delta posted only in the target book). Analytique multi-axes: **AxeAnalytique** (configurable dimensions) + `CentreCout.axe_ref`, **ImputationAxe** (one line → many axes) + `selectors.balance_analytique` (pivot axe×value) / `resultat_par_axe`. Endpoints `referentiels-comptables/` (+ `seed`), `ajustements-gaap/` (+ `poster`), `axes-analytiques/`, `imputations-axes/`, and `etats/balance-referentiel|balance-analytique|resultat-analytique/`.
- **NTFIN20–56 — allocations, engagement, close management, rapprochements, immos avancées, IFRS 15 & états consolidés** (migrations compta/0116–0120, all `TenantModel` + `_ComptaBaseViewSet`). Allocations/engagement: **CleRepartition**/**LigneCleRepartition** (bases d'allocation, `valider_cle_repartition` Σ=100 %), **RunAllocation**/`executer_allocation`+`reverser_allocation` (déversement réversible), **AllocationRecurrente**/`generer_allocations_recurrentes` + cmd `generer_allocations_dues` (idempotent), **EngagementComptable** `engager`/`liquider` (encumbrance résiduel) + `selectors.disponible_budgetaire`/`execution_budgetaire` + `verifier_disponible_engagement` (budget−engagé−réalisé, blocage). Close management: **ModeleCloture**/**TacheClotureModele** (`seed_modele_cloture_mensuel`), **InstanceCloture**/**TacheCloture** (`instancier_cloture`/`cocher_tache_cloture`) + `pret_a_cloturer`, **AccrualCloture**/`poster_accrual` (OD + extourne J+1), **JustificationVariation** + `analyse_variation` (N vs N-1), `anomalies_ecritures`, `cockpit_cloture`, `ModeleEcriture.cloture`/`generer_od_cloture`. Rapprochements de bilan: **RapprochementCompte**/**LigneJustificationCompte** + `soumettre_/valider_/rejeter_rapprochement_compte` (revue 4 yeux, préparateur≠réviseur), `ouvrir_rapprochement_compte` (report permanentes N-1), `rapprochements_en_retard`. Immos avancées: **ComposantImmobilisation** (IAS 16), **DepreciationImmobilisation** `poster_depreciation_immobilisation`/`reprendre` (IAS 36), **MutationImmobilisation** `muter_immobilisation`, **ImmobilisationEnCours**/**LigneImmobilisationEnCours** `mettre_en_service_encours` (CIP), `registre_immobilisations` (multi-référentiel) / `projection_dotations`. IFRS 15: **ContratRevenu**/**ObligationPerformance** `allouer_prix_transaction` (prorata PVS), **EcheancierReconnaissance** `generer_echeancier_reconnaissance`/`reconnaitre_echeance` (solde PCA 487), `positions_contrat_revenu` (actif/produit différé). États consolidés: `tableau_flux_consolide` (NTFIN50, indirecte, réconcilie tréso), `variation_capitaux_consolides` (NTFIN51), `annexes_consolidation` (NTFIN52), `comparatif_entites` (NTFIN53), `cycles-consolidation/{id}/export-liasse?export=xlsx` (NTFIN54, openpyxl ≥6 onglets), **EtapeAuditConsolidation** append-only hash-chaînée `enregistrer_etape_audit_consolidation` (NTFIN55), `simuler_consolidation` (NTFIN56 what-if périmètre, sans écriture). Endpoints `cles-repartition/`(+`valider`), `lignes-cle-repartition/`, `allocations/`(+`executer`/`reverser`), `allocations-recurrentes/`, `engagements/`(+`liquider`/`verifier-disponible`), `modeles-cloture/`(+`seed`), `taches-cloture-modele/`, `instances-cloture/`(+`instancier`), `taches-cloture/`(+`cocher`/`generer-od`), `accruals-cloture/`(+`poster`), `justifications-variation/`, `rapprochements-compte/`(+`ouvrir`/`recalculer`/`soumettre`/`valider`/`rejeter`), `lignes-justification-compte/`, `composants-immobilisation/`(+`plans`), `depreciations-immobilisation/`(+`poster`/`reprendre`), `mutations-immobilisation/`, `immobilisations-en-cours/`(+`mettre-en-service`), `lignes-immobilisation-en-cours/`, `contrats-revenu/`(+`allouer`), `obligations-performance/`(+`generer-echeancier`), `echeances-reconnaissance/`(+`reconnaitre`), `cycles-consolidation/{id}/`(`tableau-flux|variation-capitaux|annexes|comparatif|etapes-audit|simuler|export-liasse`), and `etats/execution-budgetaire|analyse-variation|anomalies-ecritures|cockpit-cloture|pret-a-cloturer|rapprochements-en-retard|registre-immobilisations|projection-dotations|positions-contrat-revenu/`.
- **WIR107** exposes the pre-existing `ModeleEcriture`/`AbonnementEcriture` recurring-entry engine (XACC8, previously only driven by the `generer_ecritures_recurrentes` command/NTFIN32 close task) over REST for the « Écritures récurrentes » screen: `modeles-ecriture/` (+ `generer`, materializes one brouillon écriture), `lignes-modele-ecriture/` (`?modele=`), `abonnements-ecriture/` (+ `generer-dues`, replays the idempotent-per-period batch on demand). **WIR85** finally wires the XACC6 permanent-inventory auto-posting that had zero production caller: `stock.services.record_stock_movement` now emits a new `core.events.mouvement_stock_enregistre` signal on every `MouvementStock`, consumed here by `receivers._ecriture_pour_mouvement_stock` → `poster_mouvement_stock` (entrée/sortie/rebut/signed ajustement post; transfert never does — internal move, no value change). Still double-gated OFF by default (`COMPTA_AUTO_ECRITURES`/WIR24 toggle **and** `PlanComptable.inventaire_permanent`) — without both opt-ins, unchanged (no écriture).

### frais — expense notes & site allowances (ODX15, new app)  *( `/api/django/frais/` )*
State-only split of FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 OUT of `apps.compta` (migration `frais/0001_odx15_frais_split.py`, `SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `database_operations=[]`): every model **freezes its `db_table` on the historical `compta_*` name** — no table renamed, no data moved, revert is a plain `git revert`. `apps.frais` never imports `apps.compta.models`; it reaches accounts/écritures/trésorerie only via **string-FK** (`'compta.CompteComptable'`, `'compta.EcritureComptable'`, `'compta.CompteTresorerie'`) and calls `apps.compta.services` for every posting + the FG115 period lock. A re-export shim in `apps/compta/models.py` (`from apps.frais.models import ...`) keeps existing importers working. **Deliberately distinct from `rh.NoteDeFrais`** (an employee's self-service portal declaration, no GL posting) — the two surfaces are never merged (see `docs/module-map.md`).
- **NoteFrais** (FG135) — an employee's out-of-pocket expense claim: `company`+`employe` FK, `categorie`, `montant` TTC, `motif`, `justificatif` photo (MinIO FileField), reference `NDF-YYYYMM-NNNN` (race-safe). Cycle `brouillon→soumise→validee`(posts debit charge 6143 / credit personnel-créditeur 4432, **respecting the compta period lock**)`→remboursee`(debit 4432 / credit treasury GL) or `rejetee`. **XACC27** adds `hors_politique` (flagged when it beats a `PlafondNoteFrais.montant_max` for its `categorie` — warning only, never blocking). **XACC28** adds billable-expense refacturation fields (`refacturable`, `taux_marge`, opaque `client_refacturation_id`/`facture_refacturation_id` — string-refs, no ventes import). **ZACC6** adds an optional `rapport` FK→`RapportNoteFrais` — once attached, the note is posted/reimbursed IN BLOCK by its report, never individually.
- **RapportNoteFrais** (ZACC6, Odoo « Expense Report » equivalent) — groups N `NoteFrais` of one employee into one submission: validation posts ONE aggregated écriture (Σ charges by compte / credit 4432), reimbursement settles in ONE payment. A note with `rapport=None` keeps its individual FG135 cycle untouched.
- **PlafondNoteFrais** (XACC27) — per-company, per-`NoteFrais.categorie` spend ceiling referential (`montant_max`, optional `seuil_justificatif_obligatoire`); a category with no configured row is never flagged. **NTP2P11** (migration frais/0003) adds `jours_max_apres_depense` + `escalade_direction_au_dela_de` (both optional, unset = no-op): at `soumettre_note_frais`, `services.note_frais_warning_delai`/`note_frais_escalade_direction` stamp `NoteFrais.warning_delai` (non-blocking text) / `escalade_direction` (bool) server-side and journal them to `records.Activity` (best-effort, never blocks the submission). When `stock.AchatsParametres.sod_stricte` is on, the note's own creator cannot validate its DIRECTION escalation (`_verifier_separation_taches_note_frais`, reads `stock.selectors` only).
- **BaremeIndemnite** + **IndemniteChantier** (FG136) — kilométrique/per-diem site-trip allowances: `BaremeIndemnite` (per-company `taux_km` MAD/km + `per_diem` MAD/jour, one `defaut` active barème); `IndemniteChantier` computes `distance_km` via a LOCAL haversine copy (keeps `frais` decoupled from installations/sav) between GPS départ/chantier × `taux_km` × (`aller_retour` ? 2 : 1) + `per_diem` × `nombre_jours`, all FROZEN at calc. Same `brouillon→soumise→validee`(posts)`→remboursee`/`rejetee` cycle as NoteFrais, respecting the period lock.
- Endpoints (dual-served, byte-identical, at BOTH `/api/django/frais/…` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/…`): `notes-frais/` (+ `soumettre|valider|rejeter|rembourser`), `rapports-notes-frais/`, `plafonds-notes-frais/`, `baremes-indemnite/` (auto-demotes prior default), `indemnites-chantier/` (+ lifecycle actions). **NTP2P26/27** add `notes-frais/releve/` — a per-period statement of expense notes (PDF via `pdf_notes_frais.py` or `?export=xlsx`).

### marketing — campaigns, sequences, events, loyalty, surveys (ODX9/10)  *( `/api/django/marketing/` )*
Previously undocumented here (36 models, predates this run). State-only split OUT of `apps.compta` (**ODX9**, same `db_table='compta_<model>'`/`SeparateDatabaseAndState`/shim pattern as `frais`/`portail`); **ODX10** gave it its own `/api/django/marketing/…` prefix while the historical `/api/django/compta/…` routes keep serving the SAME ViewSets byte-identically (basenames prefixed `mkt-…` to avoid router-name collisions). `apps.marketing.selectors` is the ONLY sanctioned read surface for other apps (never a `marketing.models` import) — **WIR96** (this run) is the first real cross-app consumer: `ouverture_partage_pour_token`/`relances_devis_abandonne`, read by `ventes.views.devis.suivi_partage` (see §4 ventes), and the public proposal open-hook now writes `OuverturePartage` (was routed but previously never populated).
- **Models, by sub-domain** — mailing (`Campagne`, `EnvoiCampagne` one row per recipient, `SuppressionMarketing` opt-out list, `ListeDiffusion`/`AbonnementListe`, `SegmentMarketing`, `RebondSoft`, `ApprobationEnvoiCampagne`, `DomaineEnvoi`); drip sequences (`SequenceRelance` triggered on a lead entering a funnel stage, `EtapeSequence`, `InscriptionSequence`, `ExecutionEtapeSequence`); link tracking (`LienTrackee`, `ClicLien`, `StatutEngagementContact`, **NTMKT18** `ScoreMaturite`/`VariationScoreMaturite` — multi-signal lead maturity score + its change history); quote recovery/capture (`RelanceDevisAbandonne`, `OuverturePartage`, `FormulaireIntake` + landing capture, `MessageWhatsAppEntrant`, `AppelTelephonique`); satisfaction (`EnqueteNPS`, `AvisClient`, `Enquete`/`ReponseEnquete` a configurable survey engine); loyalty (`CompteFidelite`, `MouvementFidelite`, `RegleUpsell`); events (`TypeEvenement`, `EvenementMarketing`, `BilletEvenement`, `QuestionEvenement`, `CommunicationEvenement`, `InscriptionEvenement`); plus `SupportOffline` (QR print collateral) and `PostSocial` (social content calendar).
- **Journey graph (NTMKT12/14/15)** — additive graph-routing layer over the existing linear `SequenceRelance`/`EtapeSequence` engine (a sequence with zero graph node still ticks exactly as before). `NoeudJourney` (déclencheur/attente(J+n)/**attente_jusqu_a** date-ou-créneau-ouvré (NTMKT14)/action/branche/sortie, free `config` JSON, `position_x/y` for the canvas) + `ArcJourney` (source/cible nœuds, `condition` toujours/a_ouvert/a_clique/score_seuil/tag_present, `ordre` — supersedes `EtapeSequence`'s single condition with N evaluated arcs). `ModeleJourney` (NTMKT15, a ready-to-instantiate pre-built graph template, complementing the 5 linear XMKT20 recipes) — seeded by `manage.py seed_modeles_journey`. Endpoints `noeuds-journey/`, `arcs-journey/`, `modeles-journey/`; Celery beat `marketing.executer_journeys` (08:15, company-by-company).
- **Landing pages & content (NTMKT16/23/31)** — `VersionFormulaireIntake` (NTMKT16, an editorial version of a `FormulaireIntake` landing: titre/pitch/image, server-incremented `version`, `publie` flag — the public page always renders the latest published version). `BlocContenu` (NTMKT23, a reusable content fragment texte/image/cta; inserting COPIES it into the campaign body — editing the source never retro-edits a sent campaign). `ParametresMarketing` (NTMKT31, one singleton per company — expéditeur nom/email/domaine, additive silence-window hours + daily send cap, all-NULL = unchanged behaviour). Endpoints `versions-formulaire-intake/`, `blocs-contenu/`, `parametres/` (NTMKT31).
- **Ops/reporting additions** — `heatmap-engagement/` (NTMKT24, read-only day×hour engagement heatmap), `campagnes/importer-couts/` (NTMKT26, CSV import of external Meta/Google Ads spend), `campagnes/{id}/rapport-pdf/` (NTMKT27, internal campaign-bilan PDF), `registre-consentement/export-pdf/` (NTMKT28, CNDP consent-register export), public `preferences/<token>/` (NTMKT22, self-service per-channel/per-list preference centre — never a blanket unsubscribe). `services.purger_tokens_expires` (NTMKT33, daily 03:00 beat, purges expired public unsubscribe/preference tokens) and `services.rappeler_approbations_envoi_en_attente` (NTMKT35, 4-hourly beat, one reminder per pending campaign-send approval past 24h — anti-duplicate).
- Endpoints (dual-served at both `/api/django/marketing/…` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/…`): `campagnes/`, `envois-campagne/`, `approbations-envoi-campagne/`, `listes-diffusion/`, `abonnements-liste/`, `segments-marketing/`, `sequences-relance/`, `etapes-sequence/`, `inscriptions-sequence/`, `relances-devis-abandonnes/`, `ouvertures-partage/`, `formulaires-intake/`, `messages-whatsapp/`, `appels/`, `enquetes-nps/`, `avis-clients/`, `comptes-fidelite/`, `mouvements-fidelite/`, `regles-upsell/`, `enquetes/`, `evenements-marketing/`, `inscriptions-evenement/`, `types-evenement/`, `billets-evenement/`, `questions-evenement/`, `communications-evenement/`, `supports-offline/`, `domaines-envoi/`; **NTMKT18-20/39-41** add `scores-maturite/<lead_id>/` (current maturity score + history), `attribution/comparaison/` (the 4 attribution models side-by-side for a signed devis), `campagnes/export/` (XLSX), `campagnes/<pk>/envois/export/` (CSV), `segments-marketing/<pk>/export/` (XLSX of resolved members), `evenements-marketing/<pk>/importer-inscrits/` (bulk CSV/XLSX import); public token/slug views `webhooks/brevo/`, `webhooks/sms-stop/`, `desinscription/<token>/`, `preferences/<token>/`, `double-optin/<token>/`, `r/<token>/` (tracked-link redirect), `enquetes-publiques/<token>/(+soumettre)`, `evenements-marketing/<id>/inscription-publique/`, `intake/<slug>/(+soumettre)`. **PACT106/107/108** give `avis-clients/` (FG239, + Google Reviews routing), `enquetes-nps/` (FG238, distinct from the Pulse eNPS survey) and `messages-whatsapp/` (FG207, read-only inbound log) their first screens (`/marketing/avis-clients`, `/marketing/enquetes-nps`, `/marketing/messages-whatsapp`). Frontend: `JourneyBuilder`/`LandingVersions`/`HeatmapEnvoi`/`UtmBuilder` (NTMKT25, campaign URL/UTM generator, no model) screens + guided `CampagneWizard`/`SequenceWizard`/`EnqueteWizard` (NTMKT29/30/32, opened from their list's « (guidé) » button) at `/marketing/{campagnes,sequences,enquetes}/nouveau` + `/marketing/parametres` (`MarketingParametres.jsx`) + `/marketing/generateur-url`. **NTMKT44/45** add `CampagneViewSetAudite`/`EnqueteNPSViewSetNotifiant` + `evenement_inscription_publique_notifiante` — envelope subclasses/wrappers over the `apps.compta` originals (audit-log the real send / notify on an NPS detractor / notify on public event signup) registered ONLY on the marketing router; the legacy `/compta/…` routes keep serving the unmodified originals.

### flotte — fleet: vehicles + rolling equipment (FLOTTE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/flotte/` )*
- **Vehicule** (`company` FK; immatriculation, marque, modèle, énergie diesel/essence/électrique/hybride, kilométrage, valeur, statut actif/maintenance/réformé) and **EnginRoulant** (`company` FK; type nacelle/groupe électrogène/chariot, compteur d'heures, marque, modèle, valeur, statut). Company-scoped ViewSets (company forced server-side, an injected body `company` is ignored) at `vehicules/`, `engins/` with énergie/statut/type filters + search. Uses only the `authentication.Company` string FK — no domain-app imports. **FLOTTE3** adds `Vehicule.emplacement_stock_id` (PositiveInteger, NOT a cross-app FK) referencing a `stock.EmplacementStock`; validated same-company + labelled via a function-local `apps.stock.selectors.get_emplacement_scoped` call (degrades to `#id`; never imports stock models). **FLOTTE6** adds **ReferentielFlotte** (editable per-company lookup lists: `domaine` type_vehicule/type_engin/energie/categorie_permis, `code`/`libelle`/`ordre`/`actif`, unique company+domaine+code) — additive (hardcoded choices untouched) — plus an idempotent `seed_referentiels_flotte` command. ViewSet `referentiels/` (`?domaine`/`?actif`). **FLOTTE5** adds **ActifFlotte** — a unified asset reference linking entretien/sinistre/document to EITHER a `Vehicule` OR an `EnginRoulant` via one model (exactly-one-target + same-company enforced in `clean()`/`save()`); company-scoped ViewSet `actifs/` (`?type_actif`) + selectors for cross-app reads. **FLOTTE13** adds fuel/energy consumption: `selectors.consommation_vehicule` computes **L/100 km and kWh/100 km** plein-to-plein from `PleinCarburant` + odometer (per-segment, divide-by-zero guarded, L vs kWh kept separate), surfaced read-only at `pleins/consommation/?vehicule=<id>` (`IsAnyRole`, 400 missing/non-int param, 404 cross-company). No migration. **FLOTTE14** adds **CarteCarburant** (fuel card: `numero`, `plafond`, optional `vehicule`/`conducteur` FK) + `selectors.anomalies_pleins` flagging four families per plein — `km_recul` (odometer decreasing), `km_saut` (>5000 km jump), `conso_aberrante` (>2× the vehicle's median baseline, reusing FLOTTE13's conso), `plafond_depasse` — at `cartes/anomalies/?vehicule=` (read action, `IsAnyRole`). ViewSet `cartes/`. **FLOTTE15** adds **PlanEntretien** (preventive maintenance via an `ActifFlotte` FK — reaches both Vehicule km and EnginRoulant heures): triggers by `intervalle_km`/`intervalle_jours`/`intervalle_heures` + last-done refs + alert margins; `selectors.plans_entretien_status` computes next-due vs current km/date/hours (due/upcoming/ok). ViewSet `plans-entretien/` (+ `echeances/` read action). **FLOTTE16** adds **EcheanceEntretien** (a generated due-maintenance record per plan: `due_le`/`due_km`/`due_heures`, `statut` a_faire/planifie/fait) + `services.generer_echeances_entretien` (idempotent — no duplicate OPEN échéance per plan; best-effort alert via `notifications.notify`) + a `manage.py generer_echeances_entretien` command. ViewSet `echeances-entretien/` (read any role, `generer` write action; POST-create disabled). Migration flotte/0014. **FLOTTE17** adds **Garage** (atelier: nom/adresse/téléphone) + **OrdreReparation** (repair order on an `ActifFlotte`: garage, description, `cout_main_oeuvre`+`cout_pieces`→derived `cout_total`, `statut` ouvert/en_cours/cloture, optional `EcheanceEntretien` link); ViewSets `garages/`, `ordres-reparation/` (+ `couts/` summary, `cloturer/` which solde the linked échéance). Migration flotte/0015. **FLOTTE18** adds **Pneumatique** (tire: position/dimension/montage/dépose/statut/coût) + **PieceFlotte** (part: désignation/réf/quantité/coût, optional OrdreReparation link); ViewSets `pneumatiques/`, `pieces/` + a per-vehicle `synthese/` summary. Migration flotte/0016. **FLOTTE19** adds **EcheanceReglementaire** — generic regulatory deadlines (visite technique/assurance/vignette/carte grise/taxe à l'essieu) on an `ActifFlotte`, with a today-injectable status (a_jour/a_renouveler/expire) and `expirantes/?within=N`; distinct from the FLOTTE16 maintenance échéances. ViewSet `echeances-reglementaires/`. Migration flotte/0017. **FLOTTE20** adds **BaremeVignette** — an editable per-company TSAV grid (énergie × CV bracket → montant, per year) + `Vehicule.puissance_fiscale` + `selectors.calcul_tsav` (electric exempt, no bracket → None) + an idempotent standard-grid seed. ViewSet `baremes-vignette/` + `vehicules/{id}/tsav/`. Migration flotte/0018. **ARC14** adds `Vehicule.custom_data` (JSON), the second `customfields` registry pilot alongside `contrats.Contrat`. **ARC28** adds `apps/flotte/platform.py`.

### ged — document management / DMS (GED1, new app)  *( `/api/django/ged/` )*
- Governed DMS reusing `records.storage` (MinIO `file_key`). **Cabinet** + **Folder** (self-FK tree with a materialized `path` recomputed in `save()`/`services.move_folder`, sub-tree prefix rewrite + cycle refusal), **Document** (lives in a Folder) + **DocumentVersion** (`file_key`, SHA-256 `checksum` for dedupe via `services.find_duplicate`, server-set incremental `version`, `uploaded_by`). All `company`-scoped (company/created_by/uploaded_by forced server-side). Endpoints: `cabinets/`, `folders/` (+ `descendants`), `documents/`, `document-versions/`. **GED4** — move (déplacement) over HTTP: `POST folders/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{parent}`, reparent/to-root, anti-cycle + cross-cabinet refusal via `services.move_folder`) and `POST documents/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{folder}`, via `services.move_document`); destination always resolved inside the caller's company (404 cross-tenant). **GED14** — inline same-origin preview: `GET document-versions/{id}/apercu/` streams the document bytes through Django (PDF/image/text → `Content-Disposition: inline`, else attachment; `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`), gated as a READ action (`IsAnyRole`, like list/retrieve) so read-only roles can preview. **GED17** adds a document lifecycle: `Document.statut` (**brouillon→revue→approuvé→archivé→obsolète**, default brouillon) with a guarded `LIFECYCLE_TRANSITIONS` state machine in `services.change_lifecycle_status` (illegal/unknown/same-status → `ValueError`, cross-company → `PermissionError`, `select_for_update`), exposed read-only on the serializer and advanced only via `POST documents/{id}/cycle-vie/` (responsable/admin) + a `?statut=` filter. Local GED statuses — **separate from the STAGES.py funnel**. **GED18** adds **DemandeApprobation** (review/approval workflow): `demandeur`/`approbateur`, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete; `services.request_review` opens a demande + moves brouillon→revue, `approve_demande`/`reject_demande` **reuse the GED17 `change_lifecycle_status`** (no duplicate state machine) to advance revue→approuvé / back to brouillon; guards duplicate-pending / already-decided / cross-company. Actions `documents/{id}/demander-revue|demandes` + ViewSet `demandes-approbation/` (`approuver`/`rejeter`). **GED19** adds **AclGed** — per-folder/document ACL: exactly-one target (folder XOR document, two CheckConstraints), principal = `utilisateur` and/or `role`, `niveau` lecture/ecriture/gestion, `herite` flag. `selectors.acl_effective` walks up the materialized `Folder.path` (document override > nearer folder > ancestor only when `herite`; most-permissive at equal scope; admin always gestion), softly wired into `documents_visible_to_user` — **backward-compatible: no ACL rows → behaviour unchanged**. Migration ged/0013. **GED20** adds **PartageGed** — a tokenized public document share (`token` via `secrets`, `expires_at`, hashed `password_hash`, `quota_max` + `telechargements` counter, `actif` kill-switch); a PUBLIC `AllowAny` endpoint `GET /api/django/ged/public/<token>/` resolves **by token only** (never trusts request company/identity), streams the document, atomic quota-conditional increment, and returns 404 (unknown/revoked) / 410 (expired/quota) / 403 (missing/wrong password). Management ViewSet `partages/` (+ `revoquer/`) is fully multi-tenant. Migration ged/0014. **GED21** adds watermarking & diffusion control: flags `Document.watermark_diffusion` + `PartageGed.watermark` and `services.apply_watermark` (image via Pillow — already a dep; PDF via PyMuPDF imported lazily, graceful degrade if absent → original bytes), wired into the GED14 `apercu` + GED20 public download (no-watermark path byte-identical). Category DEP but NO hard new dependency. Migration ged/0015. **GED22** adds **PolitiqueRetention** — document retention policies (durée de conservation + action à l'échéance, default the NON-destructive `signaler`) + `selectors.documents_echus(company, today)` (most-specific policy, today-injectable) + a `lister_documents_echus` command. Never auto-deletes passively. ViewSet `politiques-retention/` (+ `echus`). Migration ged/0016. **GED23** adds **ArchivageLegal** — legal probative-value write-once archiving (SHA-256 integrity hash, best-effort MinIO object-lock with graceful degrade — no hard dep). Once archived, app-layer immutability blocks edit/delete/new-version/move/lifecycle/check-out/check-in (all → 403, never 500); the ArchivageLegal row is create-only. ViewSet `archivages-legaux/` (+ `documents/{id}/archiver-legalement`). Migration ged/0017.
- **WIR163** finally gives the GED19 `AclGed` model a ViewSet — `acls/` (was model-only/no REST surface before). **WIR164** exposes the pre-existing XGED16 `TamponSociete` (per-company custom document stamp, on top of the 3 hardcoded système stamps) via `tampons-societe/` (was model-only before too). **WIR73** schedules the GED7 `migrate_attachments_to_ged` importer (idempotent by company+file_key) as a weekly Celery beat (`ged.migrer_pieces_jointes`) — a `records.Attachment` created after the last MANUAL import previously never reached GED on its own. **WIR165** gives `core.events.document_produit` its first real emitter: `apps/ventes/utils/pdf.py generate_facture_pdf` sends it (`source='ventes_facture'`) right after storing the legacy invoice PDF; the receiver stays a no-op until an admin creates a `RoutageDocumentaire(source='ventes_facture', …)` for their company. *(Note: this section's endpoint/model inventory pre-dates several XGED*-prefixed features — e.g. `coffres/`, `demandes-document/`, `regles-dossier/`, `regles-approbation/`, `validations-ocr/`, `annotations/`, e-signature champs/signataires, quotas — that already exist in `apps/ged/urls.py` but were never written up here; out of scope for this pass.)*

### rh — human resources: employee master (FG154, new app)  *( `/api/django/rh/` )*
- **DossierEmploye** (`company` FK; employee record). **FG155** adds the employment-contract layer: `type_contrat` (TextChoices CDI/CDD/ANAPEC/stage/intérim) + `contrat_date_debut`/`contrat_date_fin` (nullable dates; empty `date_fin` = open-ended/CDI). Company-scoped ViewSet (`employes/`) with `@action cdd-a-echeance/?within=N` (default 30 days) returning only same-company CDDs whose `contrat_date_fin` falls within the window. **FG156** adds the mandatory Moroccan payroll identity fields to `DossierEmploye`: `cnss`/`cimr`/`amo`, `situation_familiale` (célibataire/marié/divorcé/veuf), `nombre_enfants` (IR deductions) — all nullable (CIN/RIB already existed). **FG166** adds **Pointage** (clock-in/out: `company`+`employe` FK, `type_pointage` arrivée/départ/complet, server-set `heure_arrivee`/`heure_depart`, optional GPS, computed `duree_minutes`) with actions `pointages/pointager-arrivee` + `pointages/{id}/pointager-depart` (server timestamp; → COMPLET + duration once arrival is set). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **FG172** adds the skills matrix: **Competence** (per-company catalogue, `domaine` pose_structure/raccordement_dc/raccordement_ac/mes_onduleur/pompage/soudure/autre, unique `(company, code)`) + **CompetenceEmploye** (one row per `(employe, competence)`, `niveau` 0–4 Non-acquis→Expert, server-set `evalue_par`/`evalue_le`). ViewSets `competences/`, `competences-employe/` (+ `matrice/` grid action, `?domaine`/`?niveau_min` filters), admin-gated, company forced server-side; duplicate-code → clean 400. **FG173** adds **Habilitation** — electrical authorizations (NF C 18-510: B0/H0/B1V/B2V/BR…) per employee with `organisme`, `date_obtention`, `date_validite` (expiry) and a computed `valide` flag; ViewSet `habilitations/` + `expirantes/?expire_within=N` (expiring-soon + already-expired), unique `(employe, type_habilitation)`. Distinct from the FG172 competence matrix. **FG174** adds **Certification** — the non-electrical family (travail_hauteur/harnais/caces_nacelle/secourisme_sst/conduite/autre) per employee with `organisme`, `date_validite` + computed `valide`; ViewSet `certifications/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), unique `(employe, type_certification)`. Modelled distinctly from the FG173 electrical Habilitation. **FG175** adds `selectors.echeances_rh` — a unified expiry-alert engine unioning expiring Habilitations + Certifications + DocumentEmploye within X days (normalized `{type, employe, libelle, date_validite, jours_restants}`, `today` a param) at `echeances/?within=N`, plus a `manage.py alertes_expiration_rh` command dispatching one notification per échéance via the shared `notifications.notify` service (function-local import). No model. **FG176** adds `selectors.verifier_habilitation_requise(company, employe, type_requis)` → `{autorise, manquantes, expirees, message}` (reuses FG173's validity rule; `INTERVENTION_HABILITATIONS` map) + endpoint `employes/{id}/verifier-habilitation/?type=&intervention=`. A SOFT guard (reports; enforcement at assignment stays in installations). No model. **FG177** adds **VisiteMedicale** (occupational-health visits per employee: `date_visite`, `prochaine_visite`, `aptitude` apte/restrictions/inapte, médecin/organisme, computed `a_jour`); ViewSet `visites-medicales/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), and feeds FG175's `echeances_rh` with a `visite_medicale` family. Migration rh/0017. **FG178** adds **EpiCatalogue** (PPE catalogue: `type_epi` casque/harnais/gants_isolants/chaussures/lunettes/autre) + **DotationEpi** (nominative issue: `employe`, `epi`, `taille`, `date_dotation`, `date_renouvellement`, `quantite`); ViewSets `epi-catalogue/`, `dotations-epi/` (+ `a-renouveler/`, `employe/`), feeds FG175. Migration rh/0018. **FG179** adds EPI life/expiry tracking: `EpiCatalogue.duree_vie_mois`/`intervalle_controle_mois` → derived `DotationEpi.date_peremption`/`date_prochain_controle` (end-of-month-clamped month math), computed `perime`/`a_controler` (`today`-injectable), endpoint `dotations-epi/a-remplacer-controler/?expire_within=N`; feeds FG175 with `epi_peremption`/`epi_controle` families. Migration rh/0019. **FG180** adds **EmargementEpi** (signed EPI-handover acknowledgement, loi 53-05 typed name + server-side IP/user-agent evidence) + `accuse_remise`/`date_accuse` on DotationEpi; `services.emarger_dotation` records it (company + acting user server-side, accusé frozen at first signature); actions `dotations-epi/{id}/emarger|emargements`. No external e-sign dep. Migration rh/0020. **FG181** adds **AccidentTravail** — workplace-accident register (race-safe `AT-` reference, date/lieu/employé/gravité/arrêt+jours/photo, CNSS declaration flags) + a CNSS CSV export (`?export=csv`). ViewSet `accidents-travail/`. Migration rh/0021. **FG182** adds **PresquAccident** — a lightweight near-miss register (race-safe `NM-` ref, lieu/gravité potentielle/mesure corrective, server-side declarant; no injured person/CNSS — distinct from FG181) + a stats-by-gravité selector. ViewSet `presqu-accidents/`. Migration rh/0022.
- **ARC19** adds a nullable `DossierEmploye.tiers` FK (migration rh/0082, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/rh/platform.py` — `agent_actions_module` (read-only employés + demandes de congé actions) and `kpi_providers` (effectif actif + absences en cours, consumed by the ARC40 federated KPI endpoint).

### paie — Moroccan payroll engine (Groupe PAIE, new app)  *( `/api/django/paie/` )*
Full CNSS/CIMR/AMO/IR payroll run, distinct from `rh.DossierEmploye` (the employee master `paie` reads via `ProfilPaie`, string/FK-id, never a duplicate identity). `company`-scoped throughout, additive-only.
- **Paramétrage versionné** — `ParametrePaie` (PAIE2: SMIG/SMAG, plafond CNSS, taux CNSS/AMO/taxe formation professionnelle, versioned per `date_effet` — history never edited) + `BaremeIR`/`TrancheIR` (PAIE4: versioned IR tax brackets) + `Rubrique` (PAIE6: configurable pay-slip line items) + `StructurePaie`/`StructurePaieRubrique` (named rubrique bundles applied to a profil).
- **Profil employé** — `ProfilPaie` (PAIE8: salary/contract payroll profile), `RubriqueEmploye` (PAIE9: recurring per-employee rubriques), `RegimeMutuelle`/`AdhesionMutuelle` (private health-insurance plan + enrolment).
- **Cycle mensuel** — `PeriodePaie` (PAIE10: one monthly run, statut-gated) + `ElementVariable`/`TypeEntreePonctuelle` (PAIE11: one-off variable entries e.g. overtime/bonus) → `BulletinPaie`/`LigneBulletin` (PAIE17: the frozen/immutable payslip snapshot once validated) → `CumulAnnuel` (PAIE27: per-employee YTD brut/net-imposable/IR/CNSS/congés) + `ProvisionPaieMensuelle` (13th-month/severance accrual) + `VentilationAnalytiquePaie` (fixed analytic cost-center split).
- **Recouvrement & virement** — `AvanceSalarie` (PAIE28: salary advance/loan + monthly deduction), `SaisieArret` (PAIE29: statutory wage-garnishment, seizable-quota guarded), `OrdreVirement`/`LigneVirement` (PAIE30: bank payroll-transfer file).
- **Déclaratif** — `EcheanceDeclarative` (statutory filing deadlines) + `DepotBDS` (CNSS BDS deposit).
- Endpoints: `parametres/`, `baremes/`, `rubriques/`, `types-entree-ponctuelle/`, `profils/` (+ `synchroniser-salaire`, `attestation`, `stc`/`stc-pdf` solde-de-tout-compte, `simulation`, `registre-conges`, `historique-carriere`), `rubriques-employe/`, `structures/` (+ `appliquer`), `regimes-mutuelle/`/`adhesions-mutuelle/`, `periodes/` (+ `run-gratification`, `importer-elements-rh`, `reporter-elements`, `cloturer`, `livre-de-paie`, `cout-global`, `cout-employeur`, `journal-ventile`, `provisions`, `rapprochement-gl`), `elements-variables/`, `bulletins/` (+ `bulletin`, `declaration-cnss`, `declaration-cimr`, `fichier-cimr`, `fichier-damancom(-strict)`, `mouvements-cnss`, `etat-ir`, `etat-charges`), `cumuls-annuels/`, `avances/`, `saisies/`, `ordres-virement/`, `lignes-virement/`, `echeances-declaratives/` (+ `echeances`, `notifier-echeances-retard`), `mes-bulletins/` (employee self-service payslip vault). **PACT95** gives `types-entree-ponctuelle/` (`TypeEntreePonctuelle`, ZPAI9) its first screen, `/paie/types-entree-ponctuelle`. GL reconciliation + PDF slips reuse `core.pdf.render_pdf` (ARC11) — no bespoke PDF plumbing. **WIR89** adds `selectors.divergences_cnss_periode` + `services.controler_coherence_cnss` — the CNSS-number analogue of the pre-existing ARC25 RIB-divergence check (compares `ProfilPaie.numero_cnss` against `rh.DossierEmploye.cnss` via `apps.rh.selectors`, a signal only — never a fusion): run automatically inside `declaration_cnss`, best-effort, and notifies payroll managers (`notify_many`, event key `paie_cnss_divergence`) only when a genuine mismatch exists.

### gestion_projet — project management (PROJ1, new app)  *( `/api/django/gestion-projet/` )*
- **Projet** + **ProjetChantier** (`company`-scoped). **PROJ2** adds **ProjetLien** (`company` + `projet` FK; `type_cible` devis/facture/ticket/achat, `cible_id` target PK, cached `libelle`) linking a project to other apps' documents by **string-FK only** (no real cross-app FK). Endpoints: `projet-liens/` (CRUD, `?projet=`/`?type_cible=` filters) + `projets/{id}/liens/` (enriched). `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriches devis links via a function-local `apps.ventes.selectors.devis_card` call and degrades to the stored label otherwise (cross-app boundary respected; import-linter clean). **PROJ3** adds a project-lifecycle state machine on `Projet` (`statut` brouillon→planifie→en_cours⇄en_pause→termine, annule from any non-terminal — **independent of `STAGES.py`**, rule #2) via actions `planifier`/`demarrer`/`mettre-en-pause`/`reprendre`/`terminer`/`annuler` (illegal → 400; statut read-only outside actions) + a **ProjetActivity** transition log (`historique/`). **PROJ4** adds **PhaseProjet** (project WBS: `type_phase` etude/appro/pose/mes/reception — own enum, not STAGES; prévu/réel dates, `statut`, `avancement_pct` 0-100; unique projet+type_phase) + `services.instancier_phases_standard` (5 ordered phases, idempotent). ViewSet `phases/` + action `projets/{id}/instancier-phases`. **PROJ14** adds delay detection: `selectors.retards_projet` + `GET projets/{id}/retards/` classifying unfinished tasks and unreached milestones as `en_retard` (past due) or `a_risque` (due within `seuil_jours`, default 7) with `retard_jours` (no migration). **PROJ18** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge` — per-resource capacity (working days − Indisponibilité × hours/day) vs allocated (AffectationRessource charge, direct + team-split, pro-rated to the window) over a period with a `surcharge` flag + `utilisation_pct` (None when capacity 0); endpoint `ressources/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ19** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` — double-booking: same `RessourceProfil` allocated to ≥2 `AffectationRessource` whose windows overlap (direct + via équipe; bonus: allocation during an Indisponibilité), at `ressources/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ20** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) — proposes moving direct affectations off over-allocated RessourceProfil to under-loaded ones without creating a PROJ19 conflict (read-only), at `ressources/nivellement-charge/`.
