No model. **PROJ21** adds **BudgetProjet** + **LigneBudgetProjet** (categorie materiel/main_oeuvre/sous_traitance/divers, montant_prevu, optional quantite/pu) + `selectors.budget_total` (total + par_categorie, all 4 categories present); ViewSets `budgets/`, `lignes-budget/` + a `/total/` action. Migration gestion_projet/0013. **PROJ22** adds `selectors.couts_engages_vs_reels` — committed/actual project cost vs the PROJ21 budget per category (labour from internal AffectationRessource quantized to 2dp; matériel/sous-traitance via ProjetLien with graceful degrade — no cross-app amount selector exists yet), écart + écart % (divide-by-zero guarded), at `projets/{id}/couts-engages-reels/`. No model. **ARC22** adds an additive nullable `SousTraitant.fournisseur` FK (migration gestion_projet/0042) bridging the project's local subcontractor notebook to the DC34 unified `stock.Fournisseur` master; `services.creer_sous_traitant_via_master` is the recommended creation path (calls `stock.services.create_sous_traitant` via a function-local import, never `apps.stock.models`) + `management/backfill_sous_traitant_fournisseur` for existing rows. **WIR87** closes the ARC22 gap for good: `SousTraitantViewSet.perform_create`/`perform_update` are now the ONLY write path, always setting `fournisseur` on create and freezing the duplicated columns (nom/specialite/contact/telephone/email/actif) on both sides of every update — they can no longer diverge; the canonical screen (`RisquesPage.jsx` → Sous-traitance tab) no longer even calls this local endpoint, reading/writing the `stock.Fournisseur` master directly via `installations/sous-traitants/`.
- **PeriodeVerrouilleeTemps** + **PortailProjetToken** + **RecurrenceTache** (previously undocumented here) — timesheet month-locks, the project client-portal share-link tokens, and recurring-task templates; endpoints `periodes-verrouillees-temps/`, `portail-tokens/`, `recurrences-tache/`. **PACT78** gives all three their first screen, `/projets/parametres` (« Paramètres avancés »).

### qhse — quality / health / safety / environment (QHSE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/qhse/` )*
- NCR/CAPA (non-conformities + corrective/preventive actions), `company`-scoped. **QHSE2** adds the ITP (inspection & test plan) templates: **PlanInspectionModele** (code/nom/actif) + **PointControleModele** (FK plan; `phase`, `type_releve` mesure/visuel/document/essai, `hold_point` bool, `ordre`). ViewSets `plans-inspection/`, `points-controle/` (company forced server-side; a point is validated to share its plan's company → 400 otherwise). **QHSE3** adds an idempotent `seed_itp_solaire` management command (per-company or `--company`) seeding 3 solar ITP templates (résidentiel réseau / autoconsommation indus-com / pompage agricole), 7 points each, hold-points on Raccordement + Mise en service. **QHSE4** adds the APPLIED instance: **PlanInspectionChantier** (FK template `PlanInspectionModele`, `chantier_id` string-FK, `statut`) + **ReleveControle** (FK point; `valeur`, `conforme` NullBoolean, `photo_key` MinIO, `releve_par`); `services.instancier_plan_chantier` materialises one relevé per template point (idempotent, backfills). ViewSets `plans-chantier/` (+ `instancier`), `releves/`. `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **QHSE19** adds **RetourClientQualite** (client quality satisfaction): `note_satisfaction` 1–5 + `commentaire`, string-id cross-app links `chantier_id`/`client_id` (no model import), `traite` bool, `selectors.satisfaction_moyenne` + ViewSet `retours-client/` (+ `moyenne/` action, `?chantier_id`/`?traite` filters). **QHSE20** adds `selectors.iso9001_readiness` — a read-only « ISO 9001 readiness » dashboard: weighted global score + 6 criteria mapped to ISO 9001:2015 clauses (NCR closed 10.2, CAPA on-time 10.2, audits 9.2, procedures published 7.5, ITP coverage 8.5/8.6, client satisfaction 9.1.2), divide-by-zero guarded, at `iso9001-readiness/` (responsable/admin). No model. **QHSE21** adds **EvaluationRisque** (document unique d'évaluation des risques: `reference` `DUER-` race-safe, `statut` brouillon/validee/archivee, string-ref `chantier_id`) + **LigneEvaluationRisque** (poste/activité/danger, `gravite`×`probabilite` (1–5) = stored `criticite`, mesures, risque résiduel). ViewSets `evaluations-risque/` (+ `criticite/` summary), `lignes-evaluation-risque/`, role-gated. Migration qhse/0014. **QHSE22** adds the document-unique gate: `selectors.document_unique_valide(company, chantier_id)` (True iff ≥1 validated EvaluationRisque with lines) + `services.exiger_document_unique` (raises `ValidationError` — consumed by `installations` to gate the pose transition) + endpoint `evaluations-risque/document-unique-statut/?chantier_id=`. chantier_id is a string-ref (no installations import). No model. **QHSE23** adds **PermisTravail** (work permit: hauteur/consignation_elec/point_chaud/espace_confine, server-set race-safe `PT-` reference, string-ref `chantier_id`, validity dates, `valider`/`cloturer` actions). ViewSet `permis-travail/`. Migration qhse/0015. **QHSE24** adds **ConsignationLoto** — a lockout-tagout electrical-isolation record on a QHSE23 `PermisTravail` (point de consignation, cadenas/étiquette, vérif absence tension, server-set race-safe ref, statut consignée/déconsignée) with a `deconsigner` action. ViewSet `consignations-loto/`. Migration qhse/0016.
- **ARC10** makes NCR closure the pilot domain consumer of the generic `core` BPM engine: `services.demarrer_workflow_cloture_ncr` installs the FG369 `cloture_ncr` template idempotently per company and attaches a `WorkflowInstance` to the NCR via contenttypes (qhse drives the engine; `core` never imports qhse) — a two-step agent→responsable approval cycle; the rule going forward is any NEW multi-step approval uses this engine, never an ad hoc one. **ARC38** repatriates `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (see notifications paragraph). **ARC28** adds `apps/qhse/platform.py`.

### contrats — contracts (CONTRAT1, new app)  *( `/api/django/contrats/` )*
- **Contrat** (`company`-scoped). **CONTRAT3** adds **PartieContrat** (`company` + `contrat` FK `related_name='parties'`; `type_partie` client/prestataire/temoin/garant/autre, `nom`, `fonction`, `email`, `telephone`, `ordre`) — the parties/signatories of a contract. ViewSet `parties/` (CRUD, `?contrat=` filter; a party is validated same-company as its contract → 400). The "≥2 signatories" rule lives in `Contrat.valider_parties()` for finalization (not enforced at create). **CONTRAT4** adds **ContratLien** (string-FK devis/lead/installation/maintenance, like ProjetLien) with `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriching via function-local `ventes`/`crm`/`installations` selectors (sav degrades to stored label). **CONTRAT5** adds `Contrat.sav_contrat_maintenance_id` (PositiveInteger, string-id to `sav.ContratMaintenance` — additive, no sav import, validation deferred until a sav selector exists). **CONTRAT6** adds `Contrat.confidentialite` (public/interne/confidentiel, default interne) — CONFIDENTIEL contracts are visible only to Administrators, gated in `get_queryset` on the authoritative `user.menu_tier` (not the unreliable `role_legacy`/Role-FK divergence). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **CONTRAT13** adds **RegleApprobation** (approval rule by `type_contrat` and/or `montant_min`/`montant_max` bounds + `niveau_approbation`/`nombre_approbateurs`/`priorite`/`actif`) with `selectors.resoudre_regle_approbation` (most-specific wins: exact type > narrowest bounded interval > priorité > id). ViewSet `regles-approbation/` + `GET /resoudre/?montant=&type_contrat=`, company forced server-side (never body-set). **CONTRAT14** adds **EtapeApprobation** (internal approval workflow): `services.lancer_workflow_approbation` instantiates one step per the matching RegleApprobation's `nombre_approbateurs` (via the `resoudre_regle_approbation` selector), and `approuver_etape`/`rejeter_etape` advance it sequentially (out-of-order → 400, relaunch refused). Statuses are local (en_attente/approuve/rejete) and the workflow **never mutates `Contrat.statut`**. Actions `contrats/{id}/lancer-approbation|etapes-approbation|approuver-etape|rejeter-etape`. **CONTRAT15** adds **ContratActivity** (chatter/journal): auto-logs statut/confidentialité transitions + the approval-workflow steps (LOG, with `field`/`old_value`/`new_value` snapshots in TextField) plus manual notes; actions `contrats/{id}/historique` (most-recent-first timeline) + `noter`. Acting user + company always server-side. **CONTRAT16** adds **SignatureContrat** (in-app e-sign, loi 53-05 typed name): `signataire_nom` + server-side evidence (`ip_adresse`/`user_agent`/acting user), `role_signataire`, `methode`; `services.signer_contrat` records it, logs via the chatter, and flips `Contrat.statut`→signé through the existing state machine **only when all required parties (client+prestataire) have signed**. Unique `(contrat, role_signataire)`. Actions `contrats/{id}/signer|signatures`. No external e-sign provider. Migration contrats/0013. **CONTRAT17** chains an auto signé→actif: once all required parties have signed, `signer_contrat` advances the contract to `actif` via the existing state machine **iff `date_debut` is null or ≤ today** (future start stays `signe`), logged in the CONTRAT15 chatter (`today` injectable). No model. **CONTRAT18** adds **VersionContrat** (immutable contract-render versioning): server-incremented `version` (`select_for_update` max+1, never count()+1), frozen `contenu` + optional MinIO `fichier_key`; `services.creer_version` snapshots on demand + auto-snapshots on the signé transition (best-effort, CONTRAT16/17 preserved); read-only retrieval viewset `versions/` (paginated). Migration contrats/0014.
- **Revenus récurrents** (NTSUB1-4, previously undocumented here) — a sellable-offer catalogue: **PlanAbonnement** (Zuora/Chargebee-style Product Catalog, distinct from the pre-existing `PlanRecurrent` billing-cadence-only model) + **AddOnAbonnement**/**AbonnementAddOnLigne** + **PalierUsage**, nullably referenced by `Contrat.plan_abonnement` (`services.appliquer_plan_abonnement` snapshots price/cadence onto the contract at attach time, never a live link). **SequenceDunning**/**EtapeDunning** (NTSUB8) — configurable multi-step unpaid-invoice dunning sequences, also nullably attached. Endpoints `plans-abonnement/`, `addons-abonnement/`, `addon-lignes/`, `paliers-usage/`, `sequences-dunning/`, `etapes-dunning/`. **PACT138** gives the subscription catalogue its first screen (`/contrats/abonnements`); **PACT139** gives dunning sequences theirs (`/contrats/relances-impayes`).
- **SCA35** adopts the `core.documents` kit's TRANSITIONS read-contract on `Contrat` WITHOUT inheriting `DocumentMetier` itself: `Contrat` keeps its own `company` FK (historical `related_name='contrats'`) and `date_creation` rather than switching to `TenantModel`'s generic accessor (no functional gain, would break the historical accessor); a read-only `TRANSITIONS`/`transitions_permises` property exposes the EXISTING `machine_etats._transitions()` state graph (CONTRAT12) in the kit's expected shape — `services.changer_statut` (the `machine_etats.changer_statut` alias) stays the one write path, still enforcing the "≥2 parties" guard the generic kit doesn't know about. **ARC14** adds `Contrat.custom_data` (JSON), the first `customfields` pilot alongside `flotte.Vehicule`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/contrats/platform.py` (`agent_actions_module` — read-only list + `contrat_voir`; `kpi_providers`).

### kb — knowledge base (KB1, new app)  *( `/api/django/kb/` )*
- **KbArticle** (`company`-scoped; `statut` brouillon/publie/obsolete). **KB2** adds **KbArticleVersion** (`company` + `article` FK `related_name='versions'`; server-incremented `version` via `select_for_update` — never count()+1; `titre`/`contenu`/`auteur` snapshot). Actions `articles/{id}/publier/` (statut→publie + snapshot) and `articles/{id}/nouvelle-version/`; a version is also snapshotted on every article update. Read-only `versions/` viewset (company-scoped, `?article=` filter). **KB3** adds full-text-ish search (`?search=` over titre/corps/categorie/tags) + `?categorie=`/`?tag=`/`?statut=` filters on the article viewset, applied after company scoping (no cross-tenant leak; reuses existing fields, no migration). **KB4** adds **KbArticleLien** (string-FK produit/equipement/type_intervention, like ContratLien) with selector enrichment (produit via `stock.selectors`; others degrade) + a reverse lookup `article-liens/articles/?type_cible=&cible_id=`. **KB7** adds **KbArticleAcl** (role-tier ACL, niveau lecture/edition) + **KbLecture** (read tracking). `selectors.visible_articles_qs` filters the article queryset by ACL — **backward-compatible: an article with no ACL row stays visible to all, admin always sees all**; `marquer-lu` (idempotent) + `resume-lecture` actions + ACL management viewset `article-acls/`. Migration kb/0005. **NTMIG-playbooks** adds `KbArticle.type_article` + `contenu_structure` (JSON playbook steps, mirrored on `KbArticleVersion`) — the source a `migration.PlaybookInstance` snapshots at instantiation; selectors `phases_playbook(article)`/`playbooks_qs(company)`.

### litiges — disputes / claims (LITIGE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/litiges/` )*
- **Reclamation** (`company`-scoped; `statut` ouverte/en_traitement/resolue/rejetee). **LITIGE2** adds a server-enforced state machine (actions `prendre-en-charge`/`resoudre`/`rejeter`, illegal transitions → 400; statut read-only outside actions) plus a chatter **ReclamationActivity** (`company` + `reclamation` FK; `type` log/note, `old_value`/`new_value`/`message`/`auteur`) — auto-logs each transition and manual notes via `noter/`; timeline via `historique/`. Acting user + company always server-side. **LITIGE6** adds `selectors.tableau_bord_litiges(company, debut, fin)` — a disputes dashboard aggregating existing Reclamation data: counts by statut, total `montant_conteste`, and average resolution delay (from the `resolue` chatter log, divide-by-zero guarded → None), at `reclamations/tableau-bord/`. No model.

### adsengine — Meta Ads autonomous engine (Groupe ENG/ADSENG, new app)  *( `/api/django/adsengine/` )*
An in-ERP ads engine that runs Meta (Facebook/Instagram) campaigns on the company's own behalf — guardrail-gated propose→approve→apply, never a black box. **Company-scoped and OFF by default**: with no `MetaConnection` row the whole app is inert (no sync, no spend, no writes). **Every campaign the engine creates is born `PAUSED`** (`meta_client.py`, rule #3) — nothing goes live without a human flipping it.
- **Models** — `MetaConnection` (per-company Meta creds/ad-account, encrypted at rest), `GuardrailConfig` (spend/CPA/frequency ceilings gating every write), `EngineAction` (the propose→approve→apply spine — every mutating step is a row here first), `EngineAlert` (guardian-detected anomalies), `CreativeAsset` + `CreativePolicy` (ad creative library + compliance rules), `AdCampaignMirror`/`AdSetMirror`/`AdMirror` (read-mirror of live Meta objects), `InsightSnapshot` (daily performance pull), `WeeklyBrief` (auto-generated FR ops summary), `Experiment`/`ExperimentArm`/`ArmDailyStat`/`DecisionLog` (A/B test + bandit decision trail), `RulePolicy`/`AnomalyEvent`/`PacingState` (rules-engine + budget pacing), `CreativeGenerationBatch`/`CreativeBacklogItem` (creative production queue), `FlightPlan`/`FlightPhase` (launch sequencing + preflight), `ReconciliationSnapshot` (Meta-vs-ERP spend/lead reconciliation); **ADSDEEP P0-P3**: `InsightBreakdown` (age_gender/platform/region/hourly demographics), `AdCreativeMirror` (per-ad creative body/video/image/preview mirror), `MetaLeadMirror` (leads-per-ad, fed by the `meta_lead_captured` domain event + pull-sync, phone-key attribution to Odoo signatures per ad). `InsightSnapshot` gained typed columns (impressions/reach/clicks/link_clicks/conversations/leads_count/video_metrics). **ASG/AGEN/SIG (Assumption Engine)**: `AssumptionNode` (the living belief tree — per-class weekly-forgetting Beta posteriors α/β + prior α₀/β₀, half-lives 8/13/26 wk, parent + `invalidation_links` DAG, season tags, statut assumed/testing/validated/stale/retired), `FactTable`/`FactEntry` (versioned brouillon/publiée checked-facts table — the ONLY source of publishable numbers for grounded generation, one published table/company), plus audit columns on `CreativeGenerationBatch` (fact_table_version/claim_verdicts/template_quarantined) and fixed health-score weights on `GuardrailConfig`.
- **Key modules** — `meta_client.py` (the PAUSED-only Meta Marketing API client); `services.py` (the propose→approve→apply `EngineAction` spine, guardrail-gated dispatch); `guardrails.py`; decision engine (`bandit.py` Thompson beta-binomial, `allocation.py`, `rewards.py`, `authority.py`, `mde.py`); guardian (`rules_engine.py`, `anomaly.py`, `rule_templates.py`, `watchdog.py`, `alerts.py`); treasury (`pacing.py`, `budget_applier.py`, `identity.py`, `launch_templates.py`); creative (`rotation.py`, `recombine.py`, `backlog.py`, `flightplan.py`, `dco.py`, `creative_factory.py`); attribution (`reconciliation.py`, `capi_crm.py`, `reporting.py`, `attribution.py`, `metrics.py`); Odoo cost-per-signature connector (`odoo_client.py` READ-ONLY JSON-RPC — rule #1, key-gated `ODOO_*`; `odoo_selectors.py` won-lead/confirmed-sale-order signed deals; `odoo_metrics.py` spend÷Odoo-signatures with phone-match per-campaign attribution; `odoo_views.py`; `manage.py odoo_pull` diagnostic); runner (`flightrunner.py` state machine, `simulator.py`, `preflight.py`); `platforms/` (the `AdsPlatform` interface — ADSENG48/49, Google/Snapchat/TikTok adapters GATED behind founder budget/product decisions); **Assumption Engine (ASG/AGEN/SIG)** — living tree (`assumption_decay.py` weekly ρ=0.5^(1/H) forgetting, `voi.py` VoI scheduler `S·U·R·T/C` behind cache flag `voi_scheduler_active` OFF-by-default replacing the fixed FlightRunner transition, `assumption_graph.py` invalidation cascade, `seeding.py` idempotent YAML seed+preflight, `priors.py` intra-tenant hierarchical priors — never cross-company); autonomous creative generation (`generation.py` FactTable-grounded, `claim_check.py` FR-numeric whitelist, `groundedness.py`, `policy_lint.py`+`policy_lint_config.py`, `tier_router.py` A/B/C tiers — C never generated, `video_queue.py`, `blast_radius.py` born-PAUSED + home-grown auto-pause, `generation_audit.py` rollback+quarantine — all key-gated NO-OP, AI produces ASSETS never decisions); multi-signal (`health.py` two isolated creative/ops scores NEVER fed to the bandit, `signal_guards.py` brake-only guardrail quadrant, `cohorts.py` maturity watermarks); scheduler+generation scenarios added to `simulator.py`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/adsengine/`): `status/`, `metrics/cout-par-signature/`, `metrics/cost-per-signature-odoo/` (Odoo-backed, read-only connector), `wiring-health/`, `reporting/variantes|entonnoir|cohortes|export/`; ViewSets `connexions/`, `garde-fous/`, `actions/`, `alertes/`, `creatifs/`, `policy-creative/`, `experiences/`, `bras/`, `stats-bras/`, `decisions/`, `regles/`, `anomalies/`, `pacing/`, `lots-creatifs/`, `backlog-creatif/`, `plans-vol/`, `phases-vol/`, `reconciliations/`; **ADSDEEP P0-P3** read endpoints: `breakdowns/`, `media/<ref>/` (fresh playable URL, never persisted), `ads/<ad>/previews/`, real lead-count + Odoo signatures-per-ad metrics; management command `insights_backfill`. **ASG/AGEN/SIG** ViewSets `noeuds-hypothese/` (AssumptionNode CRUD), `table-faits/` (FactTable, `publish` action) + `faits/` (FactEntry); management command `seed_fact_table` (idempotent Taqinor checked-facts seed); two Celery beats `adsengine.decay_assumptions_weekly` + `adsengine.autopause_blast_radius`. **ADSDEEP P4-P11**: `whatsapp/webhook/` (CTWA env-gated, HMAC-verified → `CtwaReferral`), CAPI signature loop (`CapiOdooEvent`, beat `emit_capi_signatures`), Meta write surface (swap-creative/rename/spend-cap + EngineAction kinds edit_copy/set_spend_cap/rename/set_schedule/duplicate/create_ad_study/edit_post/create_post/boost_post/comment+IG kinds — all propose→approve→apply, PAUSED-only invariant intact), rules v2 (compared-window templates, name-pattern targeting, `cadence_minutes` 15-min beat), creative analytics (hook/hold/fatigue, naming tags), organic (`PagePostMirror`, `CommentMirror`/`CommentKeywordRule` + `commentaires/*` endpoints, Instagram `InstagramMediaMirror`/`InstagramCommentMirror`/`InstagramPublishJob` + `instagram/*` endpoints), consent-gated Custom/Lookalike + engagement `audiences/*`, console endpoints `metrics/ads-cockpit/`, `metrics/dashboard-v2/`, `campaigns/<id>/hierarchie/`, `reporting/audit/`, `reporting/creatifs/{classement,nuage}/`, `regles/journal/`, digest beat `daily_ads_digest`. **PUB batch 1 (2026-07-19)**: routes `signaux/`+`signaux/cohorte/` (health/signal_guards/cohorts enfin câblés), actions `file-voi`/`tests`/`tests/<id>/leads` sur `noeuds-hypothese/`, `annotations/` (Annotation, mig 0038), `sync-status/`, `today-queue/` (file « Aujourd'hui »), `ads/<meta_id>/histoire/` (fiche ad agrégée), `reporting/entonnoir-variantes/`, `reporting/incrementalite/` (geo-holdout, `incrementality.py`), `generation/variantes-ancrees/` (pipeline génération IA enfin exposé, key-gated), `alertes/<id>/snooze/`, `actions/proposer/<kind>/` + `annuler` (proposition inverse), `experiences/<id>/conclure/` (verdict humain → `evidence.py` α/β) ; composeurs manuels pour TOUS les kinds réels ; rewards/VoI/réconciliation quotidienne branchés en beats ; token-death → EngineAlert + `token_expires_at` (mig 0034) ; kill-switch/autonomie persistés (mig 0035) ; quality rankings (mig 0036) + attribution incrémentale (mig 0037) synchronisés ; HMAC sur le webhook Lead Ads ; auto-création Lead CTWA ; `MotifPerte.est_junk` + junk-rate/no-show/entonnoir par variante (crm 0065-0066) ; vigies vélocité d'apprentissage + structure Andromeda dans la boucle gardien ; garde de contrat front↔back (`adsengineApi.contractGuard.pub115.test.mjs`, node --test). Frontend : écrans Table des faits, Aujourd'hui (`/publicite`), fiche ad (`/publicite/ad/:id`), Comparateur ; DateRangeBar+SyncStatusBanner+AlertCenter+CommandPalette+MetricHelp ; vues enregistrées cockpit ; wizard de connexion ; e2e 10 écrans de plus. **PUB batch 2 (2026-07-19)** : croissance (audiences devis-vu/expiré/cross-sell/lookalike-signatures via la porte consentement, heatmap villes, halo géo, saisonnalité historique, SLA première réponse, carte de partage) ; créatif (`ConsentRecord` CNDP bloquant, `BrandKit`, `CreativeCalendarEvent`, `CompetitorPage`/`CompetitorAdObservation` (veille SANS scraping), FactEntry régional, photos chantier→créathèque, `ProposalTemplate`, chatter `AdEngineActivity` (⚠ grandfathered ARC8), beats de script persistés, mines commentaires/objections, fatigue visuelle, déclencheur météo, couverture, ROI par lane, provenance) ; science (regret, MDE opérateur, exploration, `data_quality`, feedback d'alertes, backtest historique, perte-espérée + bandit à décote EN VARIANTES — originaux byte-identiques, dérive postérieurs, cannibalisation) ; finance/plateforme (`compta_bridge` écritures brouillon + TVA auto-liquidation, solde prépayé, `platform.py`+`agent_actions.py` ARC28, purge CNDP + `lead_erased`, santé compte, vigie EOL, quatre-yeux `require_four_eyes`+`proposed_by` (mig 0050), `InsightMonthlyRollup` (mig 0051), rattrapage backfill, chat NL lecture seule via fastapi_ia). Migrations adsengine 0039-0051. **DATAPUB (2026-07-20)** : parité données Ads Manager — attribution des leads Odoo par FORMULAIRE (`odoo_leads.py`, rattache ~80 % des leads sans paire téléphone/e-mail) + pagination des leads Odoo ; endpoints `reporting/attribution-bilan/` (bilan d'attribution, aucun lead ignoré), `reporting/leads-timeseries/` (leads Odoo jour/semaine + attribués + dépense), `reporting/audience/` (démographie genre/âge + couverture par dimension) ; cockpit parité colonnes Ads Manager (sélecteur de colonnes persisté) + étiquette devise du CPL Odoo (devise du compte, jamais MAD forcé).

### cpq — Configure-Price-Quote enterprise (Groupe NTCPQ, new app)  *( `/api/django/cpq/` )*
Satellite app UPSTREAM of the quote PDF — models/rules/config only, NEVER renders a client PDF (rule #4: the premium engine at `apps/ventes/quote_engine` + `/proposal` stays the only client-quote path). String-FK to ventes/stock/crm; company-scoped.
- **Models** — `OptionProduit`/`ContrainteCompatibilite` (product option groups + INCOMPATIBLE/REQUIERT/RECOMMANDE constraints, NTCPQ1), `RegleProduitCPQ` (product rules evaluated through `core.rules.evaluate_condition_group`, NTCPQ2; **NTCPQ21** adds `bloquante` — a triggered rule is a warning badge by default, only a `bloquante=True` rule invalidates the configuration), `OffreGroupee`/`LigneOffreGroupee` (cascaded-price bundles, NTCPQ3), `PrixContractuel` (per-client/product negotiated price, priority-1 in `prix-applicable`, NTCPQ5), `SeuilMargeFamille` (min-margin guard per category — INTERNAL flag `marge_sous_seuil`, never client-facing, NTCPQ6), `RegleApprobationRemise`/`EtapeApprobationDevis` (discount-depth approval matrix modelled on `contrats.RegleApprobation`, NTCPQ7), `QuestionConfigurateur`/`SessionConfigurateur`/`ReponseConfigurateur` (guided configurator, NTCPQ9), `ClauseCGV` (dynamic deal-type clauses snapshotted onto `Devis.clauses_appliquees` at send, NTCPQ11), `ProduitEquivalent` (per-tier économique/standard/premium product-substitution rule powering the variant generator, NTCPQ16), `ParametresCPQ` (NTCPQ30, per-company CPQ settings singleton, OneToOne `company`, extends `core.models.TenantModel`).
- **reports.py** (NTCPQ19/22/23/24) — a module SEPARATE from `selectors.py` on purpose (avoids reopening the `ventes.models → cpq.selectors → ventes.selectors` import cycle); reads `ventes` only via `apps.ventes.selectors`. `suggestions_produit` (cross-sell/upsell, max 3, `RECOMMANDE` constraints then co-purchase frequency, purely suggestive), `feuille_configuration` (internal technical config sheet, never client-facing), `marge_sous_seuil` dashboard (staff), `rapport_conformite` (config compliance-rate report).
- Endpoints: `valider-compatibilite/`, `regles/` (+`evaluer/`), `offres-groupees/{id}/appliquer/`, `prix-contractuels/`, `clauses-cgv/` (NTCPQ12), `produits-equivalents/` (NTCPQ16), `devis/{id}/variantes/` (NTCPQ16, generates the tier variant devis), `devis/{id}/feuille-configuration/` (NTCPQ22), `suggestions/` (NTCPQ19), `marge-sous-seuil/` (NTCPQ23), `rapports/conformite/` (NTCPQ24), `parametres-cpq/` (NTCPQ30, singleton ViewSet), `prix-contractuels/import-csv/` (NTCPQ41), `rapports/approbations/` (NTCPQ25, discount-approval history), `rapports/catalogue-regles/` (NTCPQ42, read-only compatibility-rule catalogue export), `devis/{id}/comparaison-variantes/` (NTCPQ26, INTERNAL variant comparison sheet, never client-facing), `devis/{id}/relancer-approbation/` (NTCPQ28), `configurateur/demarrer|{s}/repondre|{s}/resultat|{s}/generer-devis/`. Devis-side actions `approuver-etape/`/`rejeter-etape/` live in `apps/ventes/views.py` (call `cpq.services`).
- **PACT118** adds `apps/cpq/bulk_targets.py`, registered from `CpqConfig.ready()`: the generic `core.bulk_edit` registry (`GET core/bulk-edit/targets/`, `POST core/bulk-edit/appliquer/`) previously had **zero registered targets in production** (only the socle's own tests called `register_bulk_target`). CPQ is the first real caller, declaring 3 company-scoped targets with narrow field whitelists (never a price field) — `cpq.offre-groupee` (`actif`), `cpq.question-configurateur` (`actif`/`ordre`), `cpq.contrainte-compatibilite` (`type`/`message_utilisateur`).

### territoires — territory assignment engine (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/territoires/` )*
Sits ABOVE the existing round-robin (XSAL11): a lead is routed by territory rules first, else falls back byte-identically. No crm-model imports (function-local reads + string-FK).
- **Models** — `Territoire` (GEO/SEGMENT/SECTEUR + criteria JSON), `TerritoireRegle` (priority-ordered condition via `core.rules`, action = assign to team/user), `TerritoireMembre` (member + race-safe rotation counter, quota). `services.assigner_lead_territoire` (select_for_update, never a modulo on count()).
- Endpoints: `territoires/` CRUD, `territoires/{id}/resoudre/?lead_id=` (non-mutating preview), `couverture/?jours=30` (**NTCRM25**, `selectors.rapport_couverture` — recent leads via `crm.selectors.leads_recents_pour_couverture` matching NO active territory rule, counted par région/segment; read-only, Responsable/Admin, no rotation side effect). Frontend `TerritoryCoverageWidget.jsx`.

### contacts — multi-role client contacts (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/contacts/` )*
Additive org-chart of buying contacts per client; string-FK to `crm.Client`, keeps Client's existing single-contact fields (implicit principal when none exists).
- **Models** — `ContactClient` (nom/poste/email/tel/whatsapp, `role_achat` DECIDEUR/INFLUENCEUR/UTILISATEUR/GATEKEEPER/SPONSOR, single-principal guard via `clean()`).
- Endpoints: `contacts-client/` CRUD (filter `?client=`).

### onboarding — product onboarding « Premiers pas » (Groupe NTDMO, new app)  *( `/api/django/onboarding/` )*
Light app: global checklist catalogue + company-scoped progress, auto-completed via the `core.events` bus (never a cross-app model import).
- **Models** — `OnboardingChecklistItem` (stable key, target role, order, target screen), `OnboardingProgress` (company+user+item, `complete_le`/`ignore_le`, unique together). **NTDMO14** adds `ProductTourStep` (catalogue of « product tour » spotlight steps — `tour_key`+`ordre`, CSS `selecteur`, `ecran_cible`; `company` NULL = global catalogue entry, same socle divergence as `OnboardingChecklistItem`) + `TourProgress` (per-user completion, `vu_le` NULL = never shown/finished; only an explicit « Revoir », NTDMO16, resets it). **NTDMO28** adds `OnboardingChecklistItem.masque_pour` (M2M→`authentication.Company`) — additive per-company hiding of a global catalogue item, never deletes it for other companies.
- **Wiring** — `receivers.py` subscribes in `apps.py::ready()` to existing domain signals (devis_sent/devis_accepted/facture_payee, **WIR59** adds `intervention_completed` → the « Suivre votre 1er chantier » item, which previously had an empty `event_key` and could never auto-complete) to auto-tick items.
- Endpoints: `onboarding/progress/` (list, `{id}/ignorer/`, `ignorer-tout/`); `onboarding/tours/` (NTDMO14, catalogue + per-user vu/non-vu in one call) + `{tour_key}/vu/` (idempotent) + `{tour_key}/revoir/` (NTDMO16 reset); `onboarding/items-masques/` (NTDMO28, + `masquer/`/`demasquer/`). Frontend `components/ProductTour.jsx` (spotlight player) + `pages/parametres` « Visites guidées » block (revoir button per tour).

### agriculture — farms, plots, campaigns, agronomic inputs (NTAGR*)  *( `/api/django/agriculture/` )*
Vertical agribusiness. **NTAGR1** adds **Exploitation** (farm master) + **Parcelle** (cultivable plot: surface/GPS geometry/crop/soil/statut). **NTAGR2** adds **CampagneCulturale** (sowing→harvest cycle per plot: culture/variety/dates/yield/statut) with `clean()` guard: one plot ≤1 EN_COURS campaign at a time. **NTAGR3/NTAGR6** adds **EtapeCampagne** (dated campaign steps: semis/traitement/irrigation/désherbage/fertilisation/récolte; cost + optional intrant link) + `check_dar_guard()` — blocking pre-harvest-delay (DAR) compliance on phyto treatments (ONSSA conformity, lève `ValidationError` if traitement applies within DAR days of harvest date, never silent). **NTAGR5** adds **IntrantAgricole** (agronomic attributes on stock.Produit: catégorie semence/engrais/phyto, dose/ha, DAR jours, matière active/AMM — produit_id string-ref to stock, unique, never import stock.models). **NTAGR9** adds seasonal labour: **EquipeSaisonniere** (team) + **PointageAgricole** (daily timesheet: equipe/travailleur_nom/campagne/parcelle/date/tâche/journées/taux — optional employe_id string-ref to rh.DossierEmploye; informative only, no payroll effect). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `exploitations/`, `parcelles/`, `campagnes/`, `etapes-campagne/`, `intrants-agricoles/`, `equipes-saisonnieres/`, `pointages/`, `lots-recolte/`. **WIR141** exposes the pre-existing NTAGR14 irrigation-cost selectors (`cout_irrigation_campagne`, `volume_irrigation_solaire_campagne` — until now with no REST caller) at `campagnes/{id}/cout-irrigation/` (paid-irrigation cost + solar-pumped volume over the campaign window). **LotRecolte** (NTAGR15/16, previously undocumented here) — a harvest batch tied to `stock` (never a second lot-tracking system), traced via `stock.selectors.trace_serie(company, numero_lot=...)`. **PACT79** gives it its first screen (`/agriculture/lots-recolte`) — the 12th agricultural resource, until then the only one with neither route nor menu entry.

### btp_chantier — BTP/EPC vertical: site issues, requests, document approvals, journals  *( `/api/django/btp-chantier/` )*
Project-execution traceback for EPC/BTP contractors. **NTCON1** adds **ReserveChantier** (site issue/finding: géo-located on plan, statut, severity, photo/document refs). **NTCON2** adds **RFI** (request-for-info: dated, destinataire, topic, response statut). **NTCON3** adds **VisaDocument** — technical document approval workflow tied to ged.DocumentVersion: statut en_attente/visé/rouvert, signataire, `clean()` enforces visa prerequisite before document advance (ged.Statut brouillon→revue path). **NTCON5** auto-opens expired visas on ged new version (via a receiver wired in `apps.ready()`, function-local `apps.get_model('ged.DocumentVersion')`, never imports ged.models). **NTCON4** adds **JournalChantier** (site journal entries: date/auteur/événement/photo). All `company`-scoped, string-refs to installations.Chantier (`chantier_id`, never imports installations.models). ViewSets `reserves-chantier/`, `rfi/`, `visas/`, `journal-chantier/`.

### esg — ESG/sustainability reporting (cross-app, lié qhse via selectors)  *( `/api/django/esg/` )*
ESG/durability consolidated reporting (GRI-lite framework, aggregation, target tracking). Multi-app reader (qhse, rh, stock, installations raw indicators via function-local selectors — never imports their models). **NTESG1** adds **PeriodeReportingESG** (frozen reporting period: année, trimestre/semestre/année, `date_debut/fin`, scope perimeter, statut brouillon/finalisee/archivee). **NTESG2** adds **CatalogueIndicateurESG** (GRI-lite catalogue: code, libelle, unite, scope S/E/G, formula description — company master copy per scope). **NTESG3** adds **ObjectifESGTrajectoire** (target + yearly milestones per indicator per period: objectif_2030, annee_baseline, trajectory points `{annee, valeur}`). Selectors aggregate `qhse` raw HSE data + rh attrition/training + installations energy-yield into normalized tiles. ViewSets `periodes-esg/`, `catalogue-esg/`, `objectifs-esg/` (read any-role on finalized periods). All `company`-scoped.

### hospitality — hotel/riad/restaurant vertical: rooms, reservations, guest folios, housekeeping  *( `/api/django/hospitality/` )*
Vertical for hospitality operators. **NTHOT1** adds room/unit master: **TypeChambre** (category: Standard/Suite…, capacity) + **Chambre** (physical unit: type/statut libre/occupée/sale/en_nettoyage, optional rate-link). **NTHOT2** adds rate calendars: **PlanTarifaire** (pricing grid: per room-type, per date-range, per occupancy-basis). **NTHOT3** adds guest lifecycle: **Reservation** (dates/guest/room/statut en_attente/confirmée/arrivée/annulée, optional client_ventes_id string-ref) + actions `arrivee/` `depart/` (state machine). **NTHOT4** adds unified **Folio** (guest bill: reservation, room-charges + additional services, montant_ttc, statut brouillon/emise/payée/en_retard). **NTHOT5** adds housekeeping: **TacheMenage** (cleaning task: chambre, date_prevue, priorite, statut, agent assigné, durée, notes). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `types-chambre/`, `chambres/`, `plans-tarifaires/`, `reservations/`, `folios/`, `taches-menage/` + a `tableau-bord/` dashboard (overview: taux_occupation, revenu_jour, taches_en_retard).

### immobilier — real-estate portfolio, leases, rent accounting, GMAO  *( `/api/django/immobilier/` )*
Vertical for property management (foncières, syndics, facility managers). Hierarchical patrimony **Site** → **Bâtiment** → **Niveau** → **Local** (unit type/surface/tantièmes/statut libre/loué/en_travaux); all `company`-scoped with materialized FK chains. **NTPRO2** adds **Locataire** (tenant: type_locataire particulier/societe, coordinates, optional client_ventes_id string-ref to crm.Client via `selectors`, never imports crm.models). **NTPRO3** adds **Bail** (lease: type habitation/commercial, dates, loyer_ht/charges/depot_garantie, statut brouillon/actif/preavis/resilie/expire + snapshot nom bailleur/locataire at signature, NTPRO5 deposit lifecycle: recu/restitue with retenue justifiée). **NTPRO4** adds **RevisionLoyer** (immutable rent-revision history: date_effet/ancien/nouveau loyer, indice, taux_variation — audit trail). **NTPRO6** adds **EcheanceLoyer** (monthly rent due: bail/periode/montant_loyer_ht/charges/total, statut a_emettre/emise/payee/impayee/relancee, optional facture_ventes_id string-ref). **NTPRO8** adds **RelanceLoyer** (delinquency escalation distinct from ventes relances: niveau 1-3, canal whatsapp/email/courrier, template). Unique(bail, periode_debut) on EcheanceLoyer. ViewSets `sites/`, `batiments/`, `niveaux/`, `locaux/`, `locataires/`, `baux/`, `echeances-loyer/`, `relances-loyer/`, `etats-lieux/`, `pieces-etat-lieux/`, `elements-etat-lieux/`. **WIR148** schedules the pre-existing NTPRO6 `services.generer_echeancier` as a daily Celery beat (`immobilier.generer_echeances_loyer`, same autodiscovered-task pattern as `ged.migrer_pieces_jointes`) — active baux' rent due-dates previously only generated via a manual `manage.py generer_echeances_loyer` run. **EtatLieuxImmo** + **PieceEtatLieux** + **ElementEtatLieux** (NTPRO15/16, previously undocumented here) — move-in/move-out inspection reports (room→element breakdown, before/after comparison photos). **PACT77** gives it its first screen (`/immobilier/etats-lieux`).
### extensions — extension marketplace catalogue (NTEXT13, new app)  *( `/api/django/extensions/` )*
A GLOBAL, read-only registry of installable no-code extension packages (templates describing the custom objects / automation rules / reports a tenant could install). Not tenant business data — company-less by design (reviewed global, in `tenant_exempt_models.txt`/`tenant_view_allowlist.txt`); per-tenant installation is a separate future brick (NTEXT14).
- **Models** — `ExtensionPackage` (code unique, nom, version, categorie, `manifest` JSON, no company FK).
- Endpoints — `GET extensions/catalogue/` (read-only list of global packages). **NTEXT25** adds `journal.py` + `GET extensions/journal/` — a company-scoped unified timeline of platform execution events (admin). **NTEXT40** adds `statistiques.py` + `GET extensions/statistiques/` — platform-usage stats (admin cockpit) for the requester's own company.

### sante — cabinet/clinic (agenda, admission, acts nomenclature, patient/third-party billing) (NTSAN1, new app)  *( `/api/django/sante/` )*
Administrative-only healthcare vertical (no clinical medical data): multi-praticien scheduling, admission, act catalogue, patient/tiers-payant invoicing. Every model `TenantModel`-scoped, cross-tenant isolation tested.
- **Models** — `Praticien` (**WIR92** adds an optional `employe_id` — string-ref to `rh.DossierEmploye`, resolved lazily via `apps.rh.selectors`, never an `rh.models` import — for a salaried practitioner's HR/payroll link; additive, no lien = unchanged behaviour), `Salle`, `Patient` (numero_dossier via `core.numbering`), `RendezVous` (overlap guard), `Admission`, `ActeMedical`, `Convention`, `GrilleTarifaire`, `ActeRealise` (tarif snapshot), `PriseEnCharge`, `FactureSante` (tiers-payant/patient split), `PaiementSante`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sante/`): CRUD viewsets per model + agenda calendar filters; cross-app `Client` resolution via `crm.selectors` string-FK. **CycleSterilisation** + **InstrumentSterilise** (NTSAN23/24, previously undocumented here) — autoclave cycle traceability + a lightweight instrument↔patient M2M; a non-conforming cycle blocks use. Endpoints `cycles-sterilisation/`, `instruments-sterilises/`. **PACT114** gives it its first screen (`/sante/sterilisation`, open to all roles) — the app had no API client and no screen for this before.

### veille_ao — veille appels d'offres : voir les avis publics, capter les tuyaux privés (Groupe VAO, new app)  *( `/api/django/veille_ao/` )*
App NEUVE et délibérément SÉPARÉE d'`apps/ao` (dont la chaîne de migrations est réservée au groupe AOF ; côté écran `features/ao/**` appartient à AOF, la veille a `features/veille_ao/`). **Ne promet JAMAIS l'exhaustivité** : l'AO fondateur (FRDISI) n'a jamais été publié — c'était une consultation privée sur invitation — d'où DEUX portes, l'automatique (portail public) et la manuelle (tuyau partenaire), la seconde étant la seule qui l'aurait capté.
- **Models** — `SourceVeille` (catalogue des sources EN BASE, jamais une URL en dur dans le code, `actif` = interrupteur), `AvisMarche` (le SAS où atterrissent tous les avis quelle que soit la porte ; dédoublonnage à deux niveaux : contrainte partielle sur l'identité portail + empreinte SHA-256), `MotCleVeille` (mots-clés + poids = DONNÉE ; `scoring.py` ne contient aucun littéral), `RegleExclusion` (« Ignorer » APPREND ; motif FR obligatoire), `ExecutionCollecte` (+ **alarme de collecte silencieuse**), `AcheteurCible` (carnet des acheteurs à démarcher — la vraie contre-mesure FRDISI, livré VIDE).
- **Services** — `changer_statut_avis` est l'UNIQUE point de mutation du statut (gardé par introspection AST) ; collecte orchestrée par un REGISTRE de lecteurs (`lecteurs.py`) pour que le collecteur du portail se branche sans réécriture ; « Retenir » → `AppelOffre` via `apps.ao.services.creer_appel_offre_depuis_avis` (unique point de contact cross-app) ; attribution du CA par source et par informateur (calculée, jamais stockée).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/veille_ao/`) : `sources/`, `avis/` (+ `retenir`/`ignorer`), `mots-cles/`, `regles-exclusion/`, `acheteurs-cibles/` (+ `relances-dues`), `executions/` (lecture seule), `collecter/` (même job que la nuit, via `core.jobs.submit`), `sante/`, `attribution/`. Permissions `veille_ao_voir` / `veille_ao_gerer`.
- **Le collecteur HTTP (VAO15-20) N'EXISTE PAS** et un test AST échoue si un module de l'app importe `httpx`/`requests`/`urllib`. Il attend une action fondateur (VAO2 : ouvrir le compte gratuit du portail et VÉRIFIER si le flux RSS authentifié existe — s'il existe, le collecteur ne sera jamais écrit). Règle #5 : fichier de risque `tos_risk/marchespublics_gov_ma.md`, User-Agent HONNÊTE et **arrêt sur 403, jamais de maquillage**. La tâche beat 06:00 ship **DÉSARMÉE** (`VEILLE_AO_COLLECTE_ACTIVE=0`).

### btp_chantier — vertical BTP/EPC : réserves, RFI, visas, DGD (Groupe PACT §E2)  *( `/api/django/btp-chantier/` )*
Le backend existait depuis longtemps ; PACT62-68 lui a donné son PREMIER écran (jusque-là 7 ressources sans aucune interface).
- Écrans (`frontend/src/features/btp_chantier/`) : réserves posées sur un plan GED cliquable (épingles colorées par gravité), RFI avec badge de retard, visas de documents techniques (soumission→revue→décision), journal de chantier (effectifs interne/sous-traitant, visiteurs, export PDF), avenants (chiffrage → envoi client → décision), DGD (notifier/contester/finaliser + comparatif déboursé sec vs facturé), diffusion de plans (accusé de réception + alerte plan périmé).
- Glyphe d'app `Construction` et accent `success` — distincts d'`installations` (`HardHat`), contrainte des gardes `appGlyph` et `AppIcon.voisinage`.

### portail (côté ERP) — administration du portail client (Groupe PACT §E3)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
L'app servait le portail CLIENT depuis longtemps ; PACT96-101 lui a donné sa face ERP (un hub à 6 onglets, `features/portail/admin/`) : comptes portail (provisionner un accès sans jamais afficher le mot de passe), preuves d'acceptation de devis (consultation seule — ces lignes ne naissent que du geste du client), paiements à rapprocher, documents déposés (action dédiée `marquer_traite`, le champ étant `read_only`), jalons de chantier publiés, demandes de ticket (rattachement à un ticket SAV existant — l'action serveur ne CRÉE aucun ticket, contrairement à ce que dit sa docstring).
- Glyphe `DoorOpen`, accent `lune`.

### innovation — internal idea box + innovation campaigns + product feedback (NTIDE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/innovation/` )*
Company-scoped suggestion box: propose/vote/triage ideas, admin dashboard, targeted campaigns, in-app feedback CTA. Chatter via generic `records.Activity` (ARC8, no bespoke *Activity), bulk tagging via `records.Tag`/`TaggedItem`.
- **Models** — `Idee` (statut/contexte/votes_count/opaque devis-ticket-chantier link), `VoteIdee` (unique per idee/votant), `InnovationSettings` (singleton, `SettingsAuditLog` on change).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/innovation/`): `idees/` (+ filters, vote/unvote, status transitions, historique, bulk, export_xlsx), `contextes/` (top-5 autocomplete), dashboard selectors.
- `management/seed_innovation_demo` — idempotent demo-data seeder (sample idées/votes) for a fresh tenant.

### credit — client credit management (Groupe NTCRD, new app)  *( `/api/django/credit/` )*
Credit limits, credit-hold (avertissement/blocage), payment-behaviour scoring, dérogations, credit-insurance coverage and consolidated exposure. **Additive & non-blocking by default** — no `LimiteCredit` for a client means unchanged behaviour (no hold); references ventes/crm strictly by string-FK (`crm.Client`, `ventes.Devis`, `ventes.BonCommande`), reads encours via `ventes.selectors` only.
- **Models** — `LimiteCredit` (unique per company+client, `montant_limite` nullable = unlimited), `ReglageCredit` (per-company settings singleton), `ConditionPaiementSegment` + `SegmentClientCredit`, `EncoursCache`, `DerogationCredit` (request/approve/reject lifecycle lifting hold), `PoliceAssuranceCredit`, `EncoursGarantiClient` (per police+client insurer quota).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/credit/`): ViewSets `limites/`, `derogations/`, `conditions-segment/`, `segments-client/`, `polices-assurance/`, `encours-garantis/`; `reglage/`, `clients/<id>/fiche|score|position-pdf|limite-suggeree/`, `import-limites/`, `exposition/`, `rapport-derogations/`, `badges/`. Selectors: `encours_client`, `disponible_credit`, `fiche_credit`, `rapport_exposition`, `quota_assurance_utilise`. Service `verifier_hold_credit`. Beat tasks: `recalculer_encours_quotidien`, `expirer_derogations`, `alerter_exposition_globale`, `alerter_polices_expirantes`. **PACT47** gives `import-limites/` its first screen (`/credit/import-limites`); **PACT48** gives `polices-assurance/` theirs (`/credit/assurance`); **PACT50** gives `segments-client/` theirs (`/credit/segments-clients`).
- **WIR93 — coexistence, documented and locked.** Two credit-hold engines exist in the repo: THIS app (`LimiteCredit`/`verifier_hold_credit`, NTCRD6 — has **no production caller today**, its NTCRD7/8 hooks are `[BLOCKED]`) and the actually-wired `crm.Client.plafond_credit`/`CompanyProfile.credit_hold_actif` → `ventes.services.verifier_credit_hold` (FG41/XFAC28, consumed at devis-accept + tranche generation). `services.ecart_encours_moteurs(client)` materializes the ONE authorized gap between them (engine B intentionally excludes `brouillon` factures, via the new `ventes.selectors.reste_du_factures_brouillon`) and is locked by `test_wir93_encours_non_divergence.py` — any future drift (new statut, one-sided assiette change) turns it red. No behaviour change; a documentation + regression-test decision, switching to a single source stays open to the founder.

### fpa — enterprise FP&A / budgeting (Groupe NTFPA, new app)  *( `/api/django/fpa/` )*
Financial Planning & Analysis: departmental budget cycles, submission/validation workflow, rolling forecasts, what-if scenarios + sensitivity, variance analysis (budget vs actual vs forecast). DISTINCT from per-chantier micro-budget (`gestion_projet.BudgetProjet`). Reads compta/ventes/crm/paie only via their `selectors.py`; emits `budget_cycle_clos` domain event on `core/events.py`.
- **Models** — `Departement` (hierarchy), `CycleBudgetaire` (brouillon/ouvert_saisie/en_validation/clos state machine, write-locked once clos), `LigneBudgetDepartement` (month×category), `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`, `MappingCategorieCompte` (FP&A category ↔ CGNC prefix), `PrevisionGlissante` + `LignePrevisionGlissante`, `HypotheseRecrutement`, `ScenarioBudgetaire` + `LigneScenario` (deltas, one base per cycle), `CommentaireVariance`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fpa/`): ViewSets `departements/`, `cycles-budgetaires/` (+ `ouvrir-saisie`/`dupliquer`/`export`), `lignes-budget/`, `soumissions-budget/` (+ `historique`/`noter` chatter actions on `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`), `previsions/`, `scenarios/`, `variance/`, `commentaires-variance/`, `mappings-categorie/`, `drivers/` (revenu-pipeline/engage, masse-salariale), `consolidation/`. **PACT53** fixes `historique`/`noter` to serialize through `ChatterActivitySerializer` (was `ActivitySerializer`, which exposes neither `body` nor the author — notes rendered empty/anonymous) and gives the submission-validation workflow its first screen, `/fpa/soumissions`. Frontend routes `/fpa/*` (saisie, dashboard, previsions, scenarios, variance, soumissions) gated Directeur/FP&A.

### assurances — enterprise insurance & claims registry (Groupe NTASS, new app)  *( `/api/django/assurances/` )*
Company insurance policies (RC pro, décennale, multirisque, cyber, homme-clé) with guarantees, premium schedules, covered assets, claims (sinistres) + indemnities and attestations. DISTINCT from vehicle policies (flotte) and market bank-guarantees (compta). Reads flotte/rh via selectors + string-FK; proposes compta écritures (brouillon) via `compta.services`.
- **Models** — `Assureur`, `Courtier`, `PoliceAssurance` (+ `PoliceActivity` chatter, `cyber_clauses`), `GarantiePolice`, `EcheancePrime`, `ActifCouvert`, `DeclarationSinistre` (+ `SinistreActivity` chatter), `IndemnisationSinistre`, `AttestationAssurance`, `ExigenceAssuranceMarche`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/assurances/`): ViewSets `assureurs/`, `courtiers/`, `polices/` (+ nested garanties/échéancier, renouveler, xlsx export), plus sinistres/indemnisations/attestations/exigences; function views `couverture-actif/`, `tableau-bord/`. Alerts via `notify()` (renouvellement J-60/30/7, warranty_expiring). RBAC keys `assurances_voir/gerer/proposer_ecriture`.

### einvoice — Moroccan DGI e-invoicing (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/einvoice/` )*
Flag-gated (`EINVOICE_ENABLED`, default OFF → app entirely inert) generator of DGI-schema electronic invoices. RE-USES the existing `apps.ventes.dgi` UBL export + art.145 validator via the module API + `ventes.selectors.get_facture_scoped` (never a ventes model import); no new render engine.
- **Models** — `FactureElectronique` (facture-id string-ref + dénormalized ref, format UBL/CII, mode dry_run/réel, statut brouillon→genere→signe→transmis/rejete, immutable `version`, `xml_key` MinIO, `hash_contenu`, signature scaffold fields), `TransmissionDGI` (inert Simpl queue, unique per e-facture).
- **Services** — `generer` (dry-run XML → MinIO, new version), `regenerer` (NTMAR9 immutable re-gen), `preparer_signature` (NTMAR6 empreinte only, never signs — `EINVOICE_SIGNATURE_PROVIDER=noop`), `transmettre` (NTMAR7 no-op without `DGI_TRANSMISSION_ENABLED`+URL), `validators.controler_avant_transmission` (NTMAR8, reuses art.145 validator).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/einvoice/`): ViewSets `factures-electroniques/` (actions `generer`/`regenerer`/`telecharger`/`preparer-signature`/`transmettre`/`controler`), `transmissions/` (read-only). Live DGI transmission + certified signature stay `[BLOCKED-ESCALATE]` (gate G14, no DGI API/credential). **PACT54** gives `transmissions/` its first screen — `/fiscal/transmissions-dgi` (grouped under the `fiscal` frontend module for UX, still reads `einvoiceApi`).

### fiscal — Moroccan fiscal compliance calendar & attestations (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/fiscal/` )*
Full Moroccan tax calendar per obligation + reminders + compliance dashboard, tenant attestations with expiry, UBO registry, actionable regulatory watch. Distinct from `compta.ObligationFiscale` (XACC9 one-off calendar view) — here the recurring RULE + materialized dated deadlines, covering obligations compta doesn't (CNSS/taxe pro/enregistrement).
- **Models** — `ObligationFiscale` (recurring rule: type TVA/IS/IR/acompte_IS/timbre/RAS/CNSS/taxe_pro/enregistrement, périodicité, règle d'échéance), `EcheanceFiscale` (dated deadline, `rappel_envoye_le` idempotence, declaration string-ref), `AttestationTenant` (expiry), `BeneficiaireEffectif` (UBO), `VeilleReglementaire` (per-company or global, `parametre_cible` actionable link).
- **Services/selectors** — `seed_obligations_standard`, `calendrier(company, annee)` (idempotent), `envoyer_rappels_fiscaux` (+ `manage.py rappels_fiscaux`), `tableau_conformite` (feu tricolore), `attestations_expirantes`, `pieces_reutilisables_attestations` (NTMAR29 source for ao dossiers), `registre_ubo` (+ completeness alert), `export_declaration_ubo` (OMPIC-shaped).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fiscal/`): ViewSets `obligations/` (`seed-standard`/`calendrier`/`tableau-conformite`/`rappels`), `echeances/`, `attestations-tenant/` (`expirantes`/`pieces-reutilisables`), `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (`registre`/`export-declaration`), `veille/` (`marquer-impact-traite`); function view `tableau-conformite/`. **PACT52** gives `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (UBO registry) its first screen, `/fiscal/beneficiaires-effectifs` (completeness alert + OMPIC export).

### education — école/établissement scolaire (NTEDU, new app)  *( `/api/django/education/` )*
Company-scoped school ops: année scolaire / niveau / classe, dossier famille/élève, inscriptions (réinscription masse + liste d'attente FIFO), scolarité (grille tarifaire, remises fratrie/bourse en `brouillon` jusqu'à approbation, échéancier auto), présences (bulk-saisie + notif absence famille), matières/coefficients, évaluations/notes, emploi du temps, cantine, discipline, and a parent self-service account — a wider surface than previously written up here.
- **Models** (all `TenantModel` unless noted) — `AnneeScolaire`, `Niveau`, `Classe`, `Famille` (**WIR91** adds an optional `client` FK→`crm.Client`, string-referenced/never a `crm.models` import, resolved via `services.resoudre_client_pour_famille` — same pattern as `sante.Patient.client`/`crm.services.resolve_client_for_lead`: a family is never re-entered as a duplicate CRM client), `Eleve`, `Inscription`, `GrilleTarifaire`, `Remise`, `EcheancierScolarite`/`LigneEcheance`, `Seance`/`Presence`, `Matiere`/`MatiereClasse`, `Evaluation`/`Note`, `CertificatScolarite`, `CreneauEmploiDuTemps`, `MenuCantine`/`InscriptionCantine`, `IncidentDiscipline`, `CompteParent` (parent portal account), `ParametresEducation` (plain `models.Model` settings singleton, not tenant-FK'd the same way).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/education/`): `annees-scolaires/`, `niveaux/`, `classes/`, `familles/`, `eleves/`, `inscriptions/`, `grilles-tarifaires/`, `remises/`, `echeanciers/`, `seances/`, `presences/`, `matieres/`, `matieres-classe/`, `evaluations/`, `notes/`, `parametres/`, `emploi-du-temps/`, `menus-cantine/`, `inscriptions-cantine/`, `incidents/`, `transport/circuits`, `transport/arrets`, `transport/affectations`; public parent portal at `api/django/public/education/` (NTEDU31/32/34, no login).
- **CircuitTransport / ArretTransport / AffectationTransport** (NTEDU23, previously undocumented here) — school bus transport: circuits, stops, per-student assignment; `Eleve.transport_mensuel` / `EcheancierScolarite.transport_montant` (NTEDU24) fold the fee into the billing schedule. **PACT80** gives it its first screen (`/education/transport`) — the backend existed complete, the education client had no entry point for it.

### uxviews — vues sauvegardées + primitives DataTable (NTUX, new app)  *( `/api/django/uxviews/` )*
Cross-cutting UX layer: `SavedView` (vues sauvegardées par écran, portée owner/équipe, défaut-par-rôle) via `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`; frontend `ui/datatable/*` gains FilterBuilder (AND/OR groups, relative dates), BulkEditDialog + undo, spreadsheet cell nav, quick-create event bus, RecentEntitiesWidget.

### entites — hiérarchie d'entités intra-tenant (Groupe NTADM, new app)  *( `/api/django/entites/` )*
Intra-tenant org structure (holding→filiale→agence): `Entite` (TenantModel, `code` unique par company, `parent` self-FK SET_NULL, `actif`), anti-cycle guard (a parent can never be its own descendant, validated client + server 400). Emits `entite_created`/`entite_deactivated` on `core/events.py` (NTADM40). Chatter via `records.Activity` (NTADM47, `historique`/`noter`). NTADM2 shipped (2026-08-05): nullable `entite` FK (SET_NULL) on `crm.Lead`/`ventes.Devis`/`facturation.Facture`/`stock.Produit` + optional `?entite=` filter via `core/entite_scoping.py::EntiteScopeMixin`; NTADM3: `Role.entites_visibles` M2M opt-in data perimeter (NULL rows stay visible, out-of-scope rows 404 on detail, 403 on write); writes on `Entite` gated by fine permission `adminops_entites_gerer` (NTADM39, `noter` exempt).
- **Models** — `Entite` (TenantModel).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/entites/`): ViewSet `entites/` (Administrateur-only, CRUD + `?tree=1` nested tree + `desactiver`/`historique`/`noter` actions + `groupe/` NTADM25 read-only per-entity KPI columns via ventes/crm/stock selectors + `mes-entites/` NTADM26 any-role list feeding the header `EntiteSwitcher`). DELETE = soft-deactivate (never hard-delete).

### adminops — administration enterprise (Groupe NTADM, new app)  *( `/api/django/adminops/` )*
SaaS-ops surface: `calculer_health_score(company)` (0-100, 3 sub-scores config-completeness/usage/data-quality + 3 recommandations, strictly company-scoped); sandbox self-service (`SandboxEnvironment` clone the tenant Company+CompanyProfile with a `-sandbox` slug via a Celery task; metier-data anonymized clone is DEFERRED — cross-app surface); exportable config packages (`ConfigPackage` snapshot of custom Role/CustomFieldDef/MessageTemplate — never metier/client data — with dry-run diff `previsualiser` + idempotent `appliquer` logged in `ConfigPackageApplication`); privacy-safe adoption analytics (`EvenementUsage`, no free payload, 180j purge); per-tenant `AdminOpsSettings` (sandbox duration/grace, seats-alert %, usage retention, `sandbox_autorise`); support/diagnostic (non-sensitive tenant snapshot + support-bundle .zip, `is_taqinor_support` or tenant Administrateur). KPI provider `kpi_adminops` on the federated `reporting` endpoint.
- **Models** — `HealthScoreSnapshot`, `SandboxEnvironment`, `ConfigPackage`, `ConfigPackageApplication`, `EvenementUsage`, `AdminOpsSettings` (all TenantModel); `PlanLicence` (NTADM7, GLOBAL editor catalog starter/pro/enterprise, no company FK, seeded); `SessionImpersonation` (NTADM22, consent-gated support session, bounded access token, audit rows marked `[IMPERSONATION …]` pre_save); `AnnonceProduit`/`LectureAnnonce` (NTADM18, founder-published release notes via notifications type `PRODUCT_ANNOUNCEMENT`); `FactureLicence` (N100e, founder licence-billing ledger, never client-facing); `DemandeInscription` (N101b, design-partner signup requests, parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED`).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/adminops/`): `health-score/`, `adoption/`, `tracker-usage/`, `settings/` (GET/PATCH), `diagnostic/` (+ `support-bundle/` zip), `rapports/journal-admin/` (WeasyPrint interne via `core.pdf`), `licences/` + `licences/pdf/` (NTADM9/29), `impersonation/…` (demande/cibles/en-attente/session-active/consentir/refuser/demarrer/terminer), `annonces/` (+ `marquer-lu`), `facturation-licences/` (+ `export-csv`/`marquer-payee`), `demandes-inscription/` (+ `approuver`/`refuser`), ViewSets `sandbox/` (+ `creer`/`prolonger`), `config-packages/` (+ `exporter`/`previsualiser`/`appliquer`). Fine permissions NTADM39 (`adminops_*`, explicit in-body checks). Celery beat: `purger_sandbox_expires`, `rappeler_sandbox_a_expirer`, `recalculer_health_score_tenants`, `purger_config_packages_anciens`, `purger_evenements_usage`, `perimer_demandes_impersonation` (NTADM37). All @actions carry explicit `permission_classes`.

### core — foundation layer (events bus, signing, AI scorers, BPM engine, platform kernel)  *(`backend/django_core/core`, NOT under apps/)*
The base layer everything depends on and that imports no domain app (import-linter `core-foundation-is-a-base-layer`). Holds `events.py` (the Django-signal domain-event bus, M6), `signing`, and PURE stateless scorers fed data as input — `forecast.py` (FG361), `win_probability.py` (FG362), `churn_risk.py` (FG363), `stock_reorder.py` (FG364), `payment_delay.py` (FG365), `anomaly.py` (FG360 `AnomalyFlag`). **FG366** adds a generic **BPM/workflow engine**: `WorkflowDefinition` + `WorkflowStepDefinition` (templates), `WorkflowInstance` (runs on ANY model via a `contenttypes` GenericForeignKey — no domain import) + `WorkflowStepInstance` (per-step statut, `sla_echeance` = start + `sla_heures`, assignee). `core/workflow.py` services `demarrer_workflow`/`avancer`/`approuver_etape`/`rejeter_etape`/`escalader_etape` + selector `etapes_sla_depassees(company, now)` (now injected) + a `escalate_workflow_sla` management command. All `company`-scoped. Migration core/0002. **ARC10** makes this the SAME engine any app attaches an approval chain to, not a bespoke one per app — pilot: `qhse` NCR closure routes through `core.workflow` rather than a local approval flag. **FG367** adds `core/rules.py` — a generic multi-criteria rule engine (no model): `evaluate_condition_group(group, context)` (nested AND/OR/NOT tree; 11 leaf operators eq/ne/gt/gte/lt/lte/in/not_in/contains/startswith/exists; short-circuit; missing-field tolerant; never raises), `validate_condition_group` (structural errors), `sequential_actions` (ordered stop-on-error helper). Reusable by `apps/automation`'s rules (wiring deferred); **ARC34**'s `RECORD_STATE_CHANGE` trigger evaluates its conditions through it (never a second evaluator). **FG368** adds `core/jobs.py` + **ScheduledJobViewSet** (`/api/django/core/jobs/`) — introspects the Celery `current_app.conf.beat_schedule` (+ optional django-celery-beat) into a normalized job list, with an admin-only `jobs/run/` manual trigger (`send_task`, broker-down → 503). Jobs are global infra (no company scoping), `IsAdminRole`-gated, no new dependency. core's first URLConf (`core/urls.py`, wired into the root). **FG369** adds `core/workflow_templates.py` — a pure-data catalogue of pre-built workflow templates (relance devis, onboarding chantier, rappel garantie) + idempotent `installer_modele_workflow(company, code)` materializing the FG366 `WorkflowDefinition`/`WorkflowStepDefinition` per company, exposed via `WorkflowTemplateViewSet` (list any-auth / `installer` admin-responsable) on `core/urls.py`. No new dependency; core stays foundation. `core/saved-queries/` (`datasets/` catalogue + `run/` execution, previously undocumented here — no `features/core/` frontend existed to call it) is a saved-query data explorer over registry-declared datasets. **PACT122** gives it its first screen, `/donnees/explorateur` (responsable/admin, new `features/core/` module).
- **Multi-tenant + viewset socle.** **ARC1** adds `core/models.py` `TenantModel` — an abstract `company` FK (→ `authentication.Company`, `related_name='%(app_label)s_%(class)s_set'`) + `TimestampedModel` (`created_at`/`updated_at`) in one mixin; the PLAYBOOK rule is every NEW multi-tenant model inherits it instead of hand-rolling the pair, and a model CONVERTED from a hand-written FK redeclares `company` in its own body to keep its historical `related_name` (never a renamed accessor). **ARC15** records the adoption census for `core.SoftDeleteModel` (FG388, `is_deleted`/`deleted_at`/`deleted_by` + `SoftDeleteManager` hiding deleted rows + `DeletionRecord` trash journal): zero business models inherit it yet — it is the acted-upon socle that the future YDATA17 soft-delete wave builds pilots on (never a second mixin). **ARC2/ARC55** add `core/viewsets.py` `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` — `TenantMixin` + `ModelViewSet` with `ScopedPermission` as the unified DEFAULT `permission_classes` (no `read_permission`/`write_permission`/`get_permissions` of its own ⇒ authenticated-only, strictly equivalent to the prior project default); the single new base a domain viewset inherits from, with named-but-unimplemented extension points reserved for pagination (YAPIC1) and filter backends (YAPIC2).
- **Numbering, PDF, audit funnel.** **ARC6** relocates the race-safe reference factory (highest-used+1 per company+period, savepoint+retry, never `count()+1`) to `core/numbering.py`; `apps/ventes/utils/references.py` becomes a byte-identical re-export shim so existing importers are untouched. **ARC11** adds `core/pdf.py` `render_pdf(html=None, *, template=None, context=None, company=None, header=False, footer=False, upload_to=None, upload_bucket=None)` — the shared WeasyPrint plumbing (lazy import, optional branded header/footer from `parametres.CompanyProfile` resolved via `apps.get_model`, optional MinIO upload) that ~45 internal PDF call sites (rh/compta/qhse/pos/paie/reporting…) converge on; rule #4 EXCLUDES `apps/ventes/quote_engine/**` and the legacy Facture PDF permanently (never migrated here). **ARC16** adds `apps/audit/recorder.py` `record_field_change` — the single funnel that writes both the `AuditLog` entry and (opt-in) the `records.Activity` chatter line for one field change in one call, replacing two independently-drifting call sites.
- **ODY25 — journal d'installation des apps.** Chaque bascule de `ModuleToggle` emet `module_toggled` sur le bus de signaux `core/events.py` (aux seuls VRAIS franchissements, une entree par module reellement bascule, cascade comprise) ; l'historique reutilise le chatter generique `records.Activity` cible sur le `ModuleToggle` lui-meme — le cloisonnement multi-societes est donc STRUCTUREL, pas un filtre a ne pas oublier. Lu par `GET /core/modules/journal/` et affiche dans la boutique Applications. Le receveur vit dans `apps/records/receivers.py` et NON dans `core/` : `records.services` importe des selectors crm/ventes/stock, donc le brancher depuis `core` creerait la chaine `core -> apps.crm` interdite par le contrat import-linter.
- **Platform registry (the "declare once, appear everywhere" kernel).** **ARC28** adds `core/platform.py` — each app declares a `PLATFORM` dict in its own `apps/<x>/platform.py` across 7 surfaces (`searchable_models`, `record_targets`, `customfield_models`, `import_specs`, `agent_actions_module`, `automation_state_fields`, `kpi_providers`); `core` collects them generically via `get_app_configs()` + `importlib` at runtime (never a static `import apps.x` — import-linter stays green), gated per company by `ModuleToggle` (extends ODX23: an OFF module vanishes from every surface at once). 15 apps now carry a `platform.py` (ao/compta/contrats/crm/flotte/ged/gestion_projet/installations/kb/outillage/qhse/rh/sav/stock/ventes). Surfaces switched to read the registry in follow-on tasks: **ARC29** `reporting/search.py` global search iterates `searchable_models` (gated); **ARC30** `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS` becomes a lazy union of `record_targets` (19 historical targets migrated in, drop-in set-identical); **ARC31** `customfields` registry loads `customfield_models` centrally in `CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`; **ARC32** `dataimport`'s `TARGETS` becomes `FIELD_MAPS ∪ import_specs`; **ARC33** `apps/agent/registry.py` auto-discovers `agent_actions_module` in `AgentConfig.ready()` (convention `register_actions()`, idempotent, gated by `ModuleToggle` in `for_user`); **ARC40** adds a federated KPI endpoint `GET reporting/kpi-federes/` resolving `kpi_providers` dotted callables into normalized `{id,label,valeur,unite?}` tiles (3 pilot providers: rh/compta/gestion_projet). **ARC41** adds `core/platform_coverage.py` — a drift matrix cross-referencing `record_targets` vs `searchable_models` (a chatter-ised-but-unsearchable model, and the inverse), baseline-frozen with a "red only on regression" policy, extending `event_coverage.py` (YEVNT7) to the declarative surfaces.
- **Document kit, request cache, misc foundation.** **SCA30-33** add `core/documents.py` — the `DocumentMetier` kit composing the ARC1/2/6/11 bricks for any NEW "header + statut + lignes + totaux + reference + PDF + chatter" document type: `DocumentMetierMeta` (a metaclass, not `__init_subclass__`, so each subclass gets its OWN `statut` field/choices instead of mutating the shared abstract field), `LigneDocumentMetier` + `TotauxDocumentMixin` (line/totals factoring), `document_viewset()` (scoping+numbering+chatter in one declaration), `render_document_pdf()` (the ARC11 PDF hook). Rule #4 EXCLUSION is permanent and named: Devis/Facture/BonCommande/Avoir never retrofit onto this kit. Pilots: `installations.OrdreSousTraitance` (SCA34), `installations.DemandeAchat` (SCA36), `contrats.Contrat` (SCA35, adopts the kit's read contract without forced renumbering). **SCA43** adds `core/request_cache.py` — a per-request `contextvar` memo for config accessors (`CompanyProfile`, tax/payment-terms referentials) that de-duplicates the ~6 identical config queries `quote_engine.build_quote_data` re-issued per Devis in a list (N+1 fix, pilot NTPLT16/QPERF1); outside a request scope (Celery, shell, tests) it degrades to calling the producer every time — byte-identical rendering. **SCA48** adds `core/benchmarking.py` `BENCHMARK_MIN_COMPANIES = 5` — the legal k-anonymity floor any cross-tenant aggregate (NTDATA46/47) must respect before publishing a stratum. **SCA20/28** add `core/signup_hooks.py` (`register_signup_hook`/`run_signup_hooks`, the same producer/subscriber pattern as the M6 event bus, best-effort per-hook isolation) + `core/services.py` `seed_tenant_branding` — seeds a neutral `TenantTheme` + default `BrandedTemplate` rows (e.g. the email signature) idempotently at company signup, alongside the pre-existing roles/catalogue seeds. **WIR138** adds `core/esign.py` (FG372) — a recorded DECISION naming `core.esign` the ONE canonical socle for e-signature REQUESTS sent to an external provider (Yousign/DocuSign…: `EsignProvider`/`GenericEsignProvider` interface, `creer_demande`/`envoyer`/`rafraichir_statut` against `EsignRequest`, contenttypes-targeted so `core` imports no domain app) — deliberately PARKED/dormant with **no endpoint exposed** until the founder provisions a real provider account (locked by `core/tests/test_wir138_esign_socle.py`). Explicitly NOT a merge target for `ventes.DevisSignature` (loi 53-05 acceptance proof — a different object) or `ged.DemandeSignatureDocument` (internal GED signature circuit — the one path that will one day delegate its external send to this socle). See `docs/esign-socle.md`. **NTEXT17** adds `VuePersonnalisee.est_defaut` + `role_tier` (a default view can also target a role tier, not just the owner) and `core/vues.py::resoudre_vue_defaut` + `GET core/vues/?cible=<x>&defaut=1` — resolves the one default list view for a caller. **NTEXT37** adds `core/formule_api.py` — `POST core/formule/tester/` evaluates a `core.formula` expression against a sample context (dry-run test bench for admin-authored formulas) — no persistence.
- **Semantic search index (NTAI24).** `core/ai/search.py` + `core/models.py` **SearchChunk** (`TenantModel`, pgvector, migration core/0044) — a single cross-module index of fiches (lead/client/devis/installation/ticket/contrat/kb-article) for `ai_governance`'s `recherche-globale/` (NTAI25) to answer "sur les documents" WITH CITATIONS, where the existing NL→SQL agent answers on aggregates. Indexable models are declared by STRING in `SPECS_PAR_DEFAUT` (`core` imports no business app); an app can extend via `register_indexable` in its own `apps.py` `ready()`. Key-gated OFF by default: `settings.AI_SEMANTIC_INDEX_ENABLED` (writes byte-identical when off) and a pluggable embedding provider (`embedding` stays NULL, search degrades to full-text keyword matching, without one). `indexer`/`desindexer` are BEST-EFFORT (never raise) and `rechercher(company, question)` is always company-scoped.

### FastAPI AI service (`backend/fastapi_ia`, root_path `/api/fastapi`)
JWT-protected, key-gated. `GET /health`; `/ocr/*` (Zhipu bill/invoice OCR →
structured data, `ZHIPU_API_KEY`); `/sql-agent/*` (LangChain natural-language→SQL,
SELECT-only, tenant-filtered, pgvector table routing, Redis history; `GROQ_API_KEY`
or OpenAI/Anthropic via `SQL_AGENT_PROVIDER`). Group R/S additions: `/sql-agent/confirm` (run a stashed propose→confirm action by signed token), registry-driven agent tools built from the Django `/api/django/agent/actions/` catalogue with proposals surfaced on `/query`, `/sql-agent/transcribe` (Groq `whisper-large-v3` assistant voice, reuses `GROQ_API_KEY`), and `/chat/transcribe` (self-hosted `faster-whisper` for chat voice memos, behind `CHAT_TRANSCRIPTION_ENABLED`, lazy model load). **WIR60** adds `/kb/*` (`POST /kb/redaction`, actions générer/reformuler/corriger/traduire/résumer) — the KB editor's `AiWritingToolbar.jsx` was already calling it, 404ing every time (no `/kb` router existed); reuses the SAME key-gated LLM as the SQL agent (`SQLAgentService._build_llm`, no new dependency/paid service), degrading to a 503 the frontend already recognizes (`isKeyMissing`) when no provider key is configured. **WIR153** retires the 3 hardcoded agent-action tools (ticket SAV / BC brouillon / visite planifiée) in favour of the registry-driven catalogue above — they had no caller left once AG1/AG2 landed.

---

## 5. Frontend, feature by feature

SPA built with React 19 + Redux Toolkit + react-router 7 + Tailwind 4. `features/`
holds Redux slices and domain logic; `pages/` holds screens; `api/` holds one axios
module per backend area. The **design system** (refonte UI) lives in `design/`
(tokens + theme), `lib/` (cn + format utils), and `ui/` (primitives) — see below.

### Coquille par app — le paradigme ODY (fondateur 2026-08-01)

L'ERP s'ouvre sur SES apps, on entre DANS une app, on en sort pour en choisir une autre.
Tout passe par `frontend/src/lib/apps/` — **jamais un second registre d'apps** :

| Module | Role |
|---|---|
| `useInstalledApps.js` | **LA source unique** de la liste d'apps : registre `moduleConfigs` ∩ modules actifs de la societe (ODX6) ∩ role/permission (ARC47). Consommee par le Menu d'accueil, le lanceur VX9, les epingles VX10, l'ecran Applications et les surfaces transverses. |
| `ActiveAppContext.jsx` | Derive l'app ACTIVE de la route (fonction pure du `pathname`, donc deep-link/F5/retour rendent la meme coquille). Expose `useActiveApp`, `useAppVisibility`, `crossAppNavigate`. |
| `appPrefs.js` / `appSearch.js` / `appIcon.js` / `appTransition.js` | Favoris + ordre + reprise par app (ODY13/29), recherche type-ahead, resolution d'icone, transition d'entree/sortie (ODY11). |
| `useAppBadges.js` | Badges vivants de la grille — UN appel agrege sur l'endpoint federe EXISTANT `GET /reporting/reports/kpi-federes/` (ARC40), jamais une re-agregation locale. |

En immersion, `Sidebar`/`Header`/`BottomTabBar` ne rendent QUE l'app active (identite + ses
ecrans + la sortie « ⊞ Toutes les apps »). Le flag de build `VITE_APPS_SHELL` (ODY30, defaut ON,
triplet `.env.example`/`docker-compose.yml`/`Dockerfile.prod`) est un kill-switch d'urgence ;
son retrait est la tache ODY33, **gatee sur validation fondateur en production**.

### Routes (`frontend/src/router`)
| Path | Page |
|---|---|
| `/` , `/login` | Login |
| `/apps` | **HomeMenu — le Menu d'accueil plein ecran (ODY2).** LA porte d'entree : grille des apps installees (ModuleToggle ODX6) ∩ autorisees par le role (ARC47), source unique `lib/apps/useInstalledApps.js`. Type-ahead, favoris, recents, reordonnancement au glisser. |
| `/app-non-activee` | AppNotInstalled (ODY8) — ecran dedie quand la route vise une app non installee pour la societe ; remplace le renvoi muet vers `/dashboard`. |
| `/crm/cockpit` | CrmCockpit (ODY15). **N'est PAS la porte du CRM** : `nav.items[0]` est `/crm/leads` (decision fondateur APX1). |
